### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 22 (2026-11-03; HELD27 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorConfirmHoldConfirmHeld27.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (4 of 6 cells).** The designated `m33f80` against 80% of exact's count: WD30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD; WD30L: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD. **4 of 8 predictions held; X3, X5, X6, X7 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 174 (60/59/55); `long+fix+forget` 172 (57/59/56); `m11f80@WD30` 111 (39/35/37); `m22f80@WD30` 140 (54/36/50); `m33f80@WD30` 142 (54/41/47); `m23f80@WD30` 133 (54/35/44); `m24f80@WD30` 136 (54/35/47); `m34f80@WD30` 136 (49/36/51); `m11f80@WD30L` 133 (40/58/35); `m22f80@WD30L` 160 (55/55/50); `m33f80@WD30L` 146 (52/44/50); `m23f80@WD30L` 156 (52/55/49); `m24f80@WD30L` 160 (52/57/51); `m34f80@WD30L` 152 (53/51/48); `m33f80@clean` 171 (59/59/53).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **X1 HELD**: separating wall from smear beats the memory where the clear cleans: under WD30, m33f80 reaches at least 5 more than m23f80. m33f80@WD30 142, m23f80@WD30 133: +9 against the 5 asked.
* **X2 HELD**: and the run's price shows where cells are cleared: under WD30L, m33f80 reaches at least 3 fewer than m23f80. m23f80@WD30L 156, m33f80@WD30L 146: +10 against the 3 asked.
* **X3 MISSED**: unlike follow-up 20's, the family does not collapse: under WD30, the five confirming rules have pooled counts more than 10 apart. m22f80 140, m33f80 142, m23f80 133, m24f80 136, m34f80 136: 9 apart (more than 10 asked).
* **X4 HELD**: every confirmation still repairs the white false returns: under WD30 each of the five reaches at least 20 more than m11f80. m22f80 140, m33f80 142, m23f80 133, m24f80 136, m34f80 136 against m11f80@WD30 111 (20 asked).
* **X5 MISSED**: the memory is worth something again: under WD30L, m23f80 reaches at least 5 more than m22f80. m23f80@WD30L 156, m22f80@WD30L 160: -4 against the 5 asked.
* **X6 MISSED**: m33f80 holds the bar in every kind under WD30. m33f80@WD30: 54/48.0 41/47.2 47/44.0 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **X7 MISSED**: and under WD30L. m33f80@WD30L: 52/48.0 44/47.2 50/44.0 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **X8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults m33f80 is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. m33f80@clean 171, long+fix+forget 172: 1 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated run of three holds four of six bar cells, and the family is level under the faults it was registered for.** The designated `m33f80` (a run of three to mark, the evidence hold of cap 30, the clear confirmed at 0.8) holds islands and current under both seas (54 and 47 under WD30, 52 and 50 under WD30L against 48.0 and 44.0 needed) and misses the gaps kind in both (41 under WD30 and 44 under WD30L against the 47.2 needed): **PARTLY SUPPORTED**; four of eight predictions held (X1, X2, X4, X8). Counts of 180 under WD30: `m33f80` **142**, `m22f80` 140, `m24f80` 136, `m34f80` 136, `m23f80` 133, `m11f80` 111 (exact 174, clean `long+fix+forget` 172); under WD30L: `m22f80` 160, `m24f80` 160, `m23f80` 156, `m34f80` 152, `m33f80` 146, `m11f80` 133.
* **X3 missed: the five confirming rules are within 9 of each other under WD30** (133 to 142, where "more than 10 apart" was registered), so follow-up 20's result (one rule under a hold, a spread of 6) holds with the clear confirmed too. POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `m33f80` against `m22f80` +22 / +20 (p 0.88), against `m23f80` +30 / +21 (p 0.26), against `m24f80` +26 / +20 (p 0.46), against `m34f80` +22 / +16 (p 0.42); against the mark of one +48 / +17 (p 0.0002). X1 held (`m33f80` 9 above `m23f80` under WD30, 5 asked), X4 held (each of the five 22 to 31 above `m11f80`, 20 asked), and neither is separable from the set's noise: the same detector `m23f80` (follow-up 21's `f80`) reached 142 and 163 in that set and 133 and 156 in this one, so one arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios.
* **Under the leak the run of three is the lowest of the five, and X2 held by more than asked.** `m33f80` 146 against `m23f80` 156 under WD30L (10 fewer, 3 asked); POST HOC against `m22f80` +10 / +24 (p 0.024) and against `m24f80` +13 / +27 (p 0.039), unadjusted for the several comparisons made. The 24 m gaps reached under WD30L (of 20; exact 19): `m22f80` 16, `m23f80` 17, `m24f80` 18, `m34f80` 13, **`m33f80` 7**, `m11f80` 19; the run of three marks far fewer cells (mean marks a flight 69 against 102 to 308 for the others, median plans 49 against 58 to 98), so a cell has to be seen three times in a row to enter the plan at all. That a run of three is slow to mark is the registered tension; that it costs the 24 m gaps under the leak and not under WD30 (8 of 20) is not isolated by an arm.
* **X5 missed:** under WD30L `m23f80` 156 against `m22f80` 160 (4 below, 5 above asked): the memory is not worth anything in this set either, as follow-up 20 found. X6 and X7 missed on the gaps kind, as the confirming arms did in follow-ups 16, 17, 20 and 21.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing.** X8 held: `m33f80@clean` 171 against the clean map's 172 (POST HOC +6 / +7, p 1.0).

**What it does not say.** That a run of three is the best of the family or that the family is one rule (the five are level to within the set-to-set noise of 7 to 9 flights; a 4-of-5 or a likelihood sum is untried). That the confirmed clear is what leaves the run of three short of the bar (follow-up 22 has no arm without it; follow-up 21 found it no better than the run clear). That 0.8, 30 and a leak of 0.15 are the right numbers (the leak is made up and WD30L is the easier sea in every arm). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 23: THE FOUR STAGES, EACH TAKEN OUT IN TURN (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-confirm-hold-confirm"; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, and registered while follow-ups 7 to 22 were unflown, so nothing below was shaped by their results)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. Follow-up 21 (`hold-confirm-hold`) read the name as three stages: **the evidence hold**, **the confirmed mark** (`M` of `N`) and **the confirmed clear** (`K` of the last `min(30, occ)` free); and follow-up 18 had named a fourth thing, **the ghost** (a cell cleared after at least 4 occupied readings is re-marked at its next occupied reading for 60 readings), and flagged its risk: *a ghost re-marks a persistent phantom at its first reading*. This name has one stage more than follow-up 21's, and it is taken as that fourth stage, **the ghost's re-mark, confirmed**: hold, confirm, hold, confirm. The confirmation is a **spatial** one, cheap and the one thing a single-cell phantom cannot give it: **a ghost cell read occupied is re-marked only if at least one of its eight neighbours is also read occupied at the same ping, or is marked.** A persistent false return is a single cell (follow-up 5, and its stated limit: *"real ones cluster"*); a real obstacle returns several adjacent cells at one ping. This round puts the four stages in one detector and **takes each out in turn**. If it meant something else (a fourth stage of another kind, or a temporal confirmation of the ghost), say so before the build, which waits for follow-up 7's set to be flown.

**The first thing to say about the neighbour test is the case it could fail.** A whole-obstacle dropout clears the whole obstacle (every cell of it reads free), so after it ends **no cell of the obstacle is marked and no marked neighbour can confirm a ghost**. The test is therefore the neighbours **read occupied at the same ping**, not only the marked ones, so a restored obstacle is re-marked as a block at the first ping that sees it. That is the design; whether it works is the study.

**The rule** (follow-up 21's detector, `f80`, with the ghost). Each cell keeps `occ` (occupied readings since last cleared), a window of its last 32 readings and a ghost life. A free cell is **marked** when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied (`occ` then starts at that count); a marked cell is **cleared** when, among its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings, at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` were free, and **a cell cleared with `occ >= 4` becomes a ghost with a life of 60 readings** (the window and `occ` kept, each reading of a ghost cell spends one life; at zero it is forgotten); **a ghost cell read occupied is marked at that reading if at least one of its eight neighbours is also read occupied at the same ping or is marked**, with `occ` restored to 4. The hold is at every range.

**Conditions** (everything else as follow-ups 3 to 22):
* **`WD30L`**: follow-up 21's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings with the range law, the dropouts leaking (chance 0.15 per reading): the full set of faults the stages are made for.
* **`P`**: follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: the ghost's test, with single-cell persistent phantoms.
* And a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `WD30L` the seven detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`full`** (**the designated: all four stages**), **`noG`** (no ghost: follow-up 21's `f80`), **`gU`** (the ghost with an unconfirmed re-mark: follow-up 18's, at one reading), **`noK`** (the clear a run of free readings, with the confirmed ghost), **`noM`** (a mark of one reading, the rest as `full`) and **`noE`** (the clear's window a fixed 30 instead of `min(30, occ)`); under `P` the five detectors `inc`, `full`, `noG`, `gU` and `noK`; and `full` on a sea with no faults. Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD28 = seeds 2801 to 2860 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `full` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `WD30L` and under `P`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (Follow-up 5's finding was that the map that forgets already holds the bar under `P`: those three cells are the ones a detector can break, and the verdict counts them.)

**The tension, said before the data.** Four stages stack four prices: the confirmation's delay at first marking, the evidence hold's smear, the K-of-N clear's shorter hold against a leaking dropout, and the ghost's re-mark of a thing that was never there. The ablation is the way to see which of them the others cover: if removing a stage costs nothing, it is not earning its place, and a detector with four stages of which one is idle is a worse detector than three.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason).**
* **Z1** the confirmed mark matters: under `WD30L`, `full` reaches at least 20 more than `noM`. (A false return at 0.01 a cell a ping has to repeat.)
* **Z2** the confirmed clear has the price follow-up 21 predicted: under `WD30L`, `noK` reaches at least 5 more than `full`. (A leaking dropout is free 85% of the time, which a run of 30 never gets and a clear of 24 in 30 does; that is follow-up 21's A4 asked here of the whole detector.)
* **Z3** the evidence is idle at this clear: under `WD30L`, `noE` is within 10 of `full` (either side). (With the mark of 2-of-3 few phantoms survive to need a short hold.)
* **Z4** the neighbour test is what makes the ghost safe: under `P`, `gU` reaches at least 5 fewer than `full`. (A persistent phantom is a single cell: the ungated ghost brings it back at its first reading; the gated one never.)
* **Z5** the ghost earns its place against the leak: under `WD30L`, `full` reaches at least 5 more than `noG`. (A K-of-N clear leaves cells cleared that the ghost restores at one reading.)
* **Z6** under the persistent mix `P`, `full` reaches more than `inc`.
* **Z7** `full` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `WD30L`.
* **Z8** on a sea with no faults `full` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`full` against `noG` under `P`, `noK` against `inc`, the per-kind and per-gap counts, and the number of ghost re-marks refused for want of a neighbour are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 2-of-3, 0.8, 30, 4, 60 and "one neighbour" are the right numbers; that the neighbour test works for a real obstacle only one cell wide (it cannot, and the generator's obstacles are wider; the study does not vary that); that persistent phantoms of a real sonar are single cells (follow-up 5's limit); that a spatial confirmation of the *mark* (an isolated occupied cell is distrusted) is not the better use of the idea (untried, and the natural next stage); that the stages are independent of one another. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 23 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`), ping's dropout object and leak, and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD28 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers (the detector module 161 mutants, 152 red and 9 equivalent; ping's new lines 63 mutants, all red; the study engine and the 516 mechanical mutants of the specs, each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against the registrations; the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `inc@WD30L` 17/18; `full@WD30L` 17/18; `noG@WD30L` 17/18; `gU@WD30L` 18/18; `noK@WD30L` 16/18; `noM@WD30L` 13/18; `noE@WD30L` 15/18; `inc@P` 16/18; `full@P` 17/18; `noG@P` 16/18; `gU@P` 17/18; `noK@P` 18/18; `full@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**The ghost's window, said plainly.** The registration of follow-up 18 and of this round say the ghost keeps *the window and `occ`* while its life lasts (and follow-up 16 says a cleared cell is emptied); the module does exactly that: a cell cleared with enough evidence to become a ghost keeps its window, evidence and readings count until the ghost expires or is re-marked, every other cleared cell is emptied. A consequence worth knowing before the data: a kept window can satisfy the ordinary `M`-of-`N` mark rule on a later reading, so a ghost cell can be re-marked by the *ordinary* rule (not by the ghost) and then it is neither counted as a ghost mark nor tested by the neighbour rule. *The neighbour test* counts a neighbour that was read occupied at the same ping **or was marked when the ping began** (the registration said "or is marked"; fixing the instant makes the result independent of the order the cells are visited in). *Reported and not predicted* in the registration was the number of ghost re-marks refused for want of a neighbour: the record carries it (`detStats.ghostRefused`).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `full`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD28 = seeds 2801 to 2860 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 23 (2026-11-03; HELD28 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorHoldConfirmHoldConfirmHeld28.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (5 of 6 cells).** The designated `full` against 80% of exact's count: WD30L: islands HELD, gaps HELD, current HELD; P: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD. **4 of 8 predictions held; Z2, Z4, Z5, Z6 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 174 (60/59/55); `long+fix+forget` 171 (59/58/54); `inc@WD30L` 134 (39/56/39); `full@WD30L` 160 (52/53/55); `noG@WD30L` 157 (51/56/50); `gU@WD30L` 161 (52/54/55); `noK@WD30L` 163 (53/56/54); `noM@WD30L` 135 (38/58/39); `noE@WD30L` 163 (54/58/51); `inc@P` 151 (52/46/53); `full@P` 149 (50/44/55); `noG@P` 152 (49/50/53); `gU@P` 149 (50/44/55); `noK@P` 152 (49/48/55); `full@clean` 173 (59/59/55).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **Z1 HELD**: the confirmed mark matters: under WD30L, full reaches at least 20 more than noM. full@WD30L 160, noM@WD30L 135: +25 against the 20 asked.
* **Z2 MISSED**: the confirmed clear has the price follow-up 21 predicted: under WD30L, noK reaches at least 5 more than full. noK@WD30L 163, full@WD30L 160: +3 against the 5 asked.
* **Z3 HELD**: the evidence is idle at this clear: under WD30L, noE is within 10 of full. noE@WD30L 163, full@WD30L 160: 3 apart, within 10 asked.
* **Z4 MISSED**: the neighbour test is what makes the ghost safe: under P, gU reaches at least 5 fewer than full. full@P 149, gU@P 149: +0 against the 5 asked.
* **Z5 MISSED**: the ghost earns its place against the leak: under WD30L, full reaches at least 5 more than noG. full@WD30L 160, noG@WD30L 157: +3 against the 5 asked.
* **Z6 MISSED**: under the persistent mix P, full reaches more than inc. full@P 149 against inc@P 151.
* **Z7 HELD**: full holds the bar in every kind under WD30L. full@WD30L: 52/48.0 53/47.2 55/44.0 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **Z8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults full is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. full@clean 173, long+fix+forget 171: 2 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **Of the four stages only the confirmed mark does anything measurable.** The designated `full` (a 2-of-3 mark, the evidence hold of cap 30, the clear confirmed at 0.8 and the ghost with the neighbour test) holds five of six bar cells: all three under WD30L (52, 53, 55 against 48.0, 47.2 and 44.0 needed) and islands and current under P (50 and 55); the gaps kind under P is 44 against 47.2: **PARTLY SUPPORTED**; four of eight predictions held (Z1, Z3, Z7, Z8). Counts of 180 under WD30L: `noK` 163 (a run clear), `noE` 163 (a plain count of thirty in place of the evidence), `gU` 161 (the ghost without the neighbour test), `full` 160, `noG` 157 (no ghost), `noM` 135 (a mark of one), `inc` 134 (exact 174, clean `long+fix+forget` 171); under P: `noG` 152, `noK` 152, `inc` 151, `full` 149, `gU` 149. Z1 held: `full` is **25 above** `noM` under WD30L (20 asked; POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) +36 / +11, p 0.0003), and `noM` is level with the map that forgets (135, 134).
* **Every other stage is within a few flights of nothing.** Under WD30L `full` is 3 below `noK`, 3 below `noE`, 1 below `gU` and 3 above `noG`: a spread of 6 across the five detectors that all have the confirmed mark (157 to 163), smaller than the 7 to 9 flights one arm moves between two sets of scenarios (follow-up 22). POST HOC `full` against `noG` +11 / +8 (p 0.65), against `noK` +3 / +6 (p 0.51), against `noE` +14 / +17 (p 0.72), against `gU` +0 / +1 (p 1.0). Z2 missed (the confirmed clear's registered price, `noK` 5 above `full`, is 3), Z3 held (`noE` within 10), Z5 missed (the ghost earns 3, 5 asked).
* **The neighbour test is almost never exercised.** Mean ghost re-marks a flight under WD30L: `full` 29 accepted and 1 refused, `gU` 30 and 0; under P `full` 28 and 0, `gU` 29 and 0. So the ghost cells that are read occupied nearly all have an occupied neighbour at that ping, and `full` and `gU` differ by 1 flight under WD30L (160, 161) and 0 under P (149, 149): Z4 missed ("the neighbour test is what makes the ghost safe", `gU` 5 below `full`: it is level). The registered worry (a restored obstacle after a whole-obstacle dropout has no marked neighbour) did not arise as a refusal either, at 1 in 30.
* **Under the persistent mix nothing beats the map that forgets.** Z6 missed: `full@P` 149 against `inc@P` 151 (POST HOC +16 / +18, p 0.86); `noG` and `noK` are 152. The gaps kind is the cell that fails for `full` (44 of 60; `inc` 46, `noK` 48, `noG` 50), and the 24 m gaps reached of 20 are `full` 10, `gU` 9, `noG` 12, `noK` 12, `inc` 9 (exact 19): the ghost-bearing arms are the lowest of the confirmed-mark arms, by 2 to 3 of 20, which is inside the noise of a set.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing.** Z8 held: `full@clean` 173 against the clean map's 171 (POST HOC +6 / +4, p 0.75).

**What it does not say.** That the hold, the confirmed clear, the ghost and the neighbour test are useless: each is level with its absence in these two seas, in a set where one arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between sets of scenarios, and the persistent mix passes the mark of the detector so that none of the later stages has much to do. That the neighbour test could not refuse in a sea whose false returns are clustered (these are single cells by construction and the ones that matter are not). That 0.8, 30, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers, or that the leak (0.15, made up) is a measured flicker. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 24: THE RANGE-GATED HOLD, WITH THE CLEAR CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-confirm", asked together with "Let's do 8 to 23"; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT OR FOR FOLLOW-UPS 8 TO 23 WAS WRITTEN, and with follow-ups 8 to 23 and 7's neighbours unflown, so nothing below was shaped by their results; follow-up 7's set (HELD12) had been flown and is the only result in hand)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 13's name (`detector-range-hold`, the evidence hold within `near` of the sonar and one free reading beyond) with `-confirm` on the end, and the word before the `-confirm` is the hold, so the confirmation is taken to be **the hold's: the confirmed clear of follow-up 21**, a marked cell cleared when `K` of the last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free and not `L` of `L`. That is the pairing follow-ups 13 and 21 leave open: **both are cures for the same cost, the fix smear a long hold brings back** (13 by *where* the hold applies, 21 by *how* a cell is cleared), so the round asks whether they **substitute** (either alone is enough) or **add**. The confirmed *mark* with a range is follow-up 14 and 17's; the four stages together are follow-up 23's. If it meant one of those, say so before the build.

**The rule** (follow-up 16's detector, a 2-of-3 mark at every range, with the clear varied on two switches). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings and `occ`. A marked cell **within `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping (inclusive; believed position to the cell's centre) is cleared when at least `K = ceil(f x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free; **beyond `near`**, in the **gated** form, after one free reading; in the **ungated** form, by the same `K`-of-`L` rule at every range. `f = 1` is a run of `L` free readings (follow-up 12's and 16's hold); `f = 0.8` is follow-up 21's `f80`.

**Conditions** (everything else as follow-ups 3 to 23): **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings with the range law, the dropouts leaking at 0.15 a reading), **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the same white faults with the misses and the false returns following the range law, the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with the dropouts of 30 pings and no leak), and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and a 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `WD30L` and `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23`** (one free reading to clear), **`m23e30`** (the hold at every range, a run: follow-up 16's designated), **`m23e30k`** (the hold at every range, `f80`: follow-up 21's), **`m23re30`** (the gated hold, a run: follow-up 13's hold behind a 2-of-3 mark) and **`m23re30k`** (**the gated hold with `f80`: the designated**) (12 arms); and `m23re30k` on a sea with no faults. Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD29 = seeds 2901 to 2960 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23re30k` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `WD30L` and under `R3D30`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise.

**The tension, said before the data.** The two cures aim at one cost from two sides, so they may do the same job and the gated confirmed clear be a hold with two switches and one effect. And each carries its own price into the pairing: the gate loses the cell a dropout takes far away (follow-up 17's tension, and follow-up 18's J1 bets on it), the confirmed clear leaves a leaking dropout cleared sooner (follow-up 21's A4), and **the pair puts both prices on the one condition that has both** (`WD30L` has the leak; `R3D30` has the far dropouts the range law makes common).

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason).**
* **I1** the gate costs where the dropouts begin far away: under `R3D30`, `m23e30` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30`. (Follow-up 17's tension, and follow-up 18's J1, asked of a 2-of-3 mark at every range.)
* **I2** behind the gate the confirmed clear changes little: under `WD30L`, `m23re30k` is within 8 of `m23re30` (either side). (The confirmed clear acts only within 80 m, a sixth of the sector's area.)
* **I3** without the gate the confirmed clear pays in the narrow gaps: in `hard-gaps` under `WD30L`, `m23e30k` reaches at least 3 more than `m23e30`. (Follow-up 21's A1 on the hold at every range.)
* **I4** the two cures substitute and do not add: under `WD30L`, `m23re30k` reaches at most 5 more than the larger of `m23re30` and `m23e30k`.
* **I5** `m23re30k` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `WD30L`.
* **I6** and under `R3D30`.
* **I7** the hold is still worth having: under `R3D30`, `m23re30k` reaches at least 10 more than `m23`.
* **I8** on a sea with no faults `m23re30k` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23e30k` against `m23re30k`, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the pooled number of occupied cells at the last plan are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 2-of-3, a cap of 30 and `f` = 0.8 are the right numbers (one point of a large plane, and the leak is follow-up 21's made-up flicker); that a step in range and not a smooth function is right (follow-up 15); that the pair is better than any other treatment of the smear (a filtered fix, a thinning of the map, the leaky counter are untried). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 24 (2026-11-03; HELD29 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorRangeHoldConfirmHeld29.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (5 of 6 cells).** The designated `m23re30k` against 80% of exact's count: WD30L: islands HELD, gaps HELD, current HELD; R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD. **5 of 8 predictions held; I3, I6, I7 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 176 (60/59/57); `long+fix+forget` 174 (58/58/58); `inc@WD30L` 135 (41/60/34); `m23@WD30L` 158 (53/53/52); `m23e30@WD30L` 166 (54/58/54); `m23e30k@WD30L` 162 (52/57/53); `m23re30@WD30L` 155 (53/49/53); `m23re30k@WD30L` 153 (51/49/53); `inc@R3D30` 125 (41/43/41); `m23@R3D30` 140 (50/41/49); `m23e30@R3D30` 141 (53/39/49); `m23e30k@R3D30` 138 (50/37/51); `m23re30@R3D30` 132 (48/35/49); `m23re30k@R3D30` 130 (49/35/46); `m23re30k@clean` 170 (54/58/58).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **I1 HELD**: the gate costs where the dropouts begin far away: under R3D30, m23e30 reaches at least 5 more than m23re30. m23e30@R3D30 141, m23re30@R3D30 132: +9 against the 5 asked.
* **I2 HELD**: behind the gate the confirmed clear changes little: under WD30L, m23re30k is within 8 of m23re30. m23re30k@WD30L 153, m23re30@WD30L 155: 2 apart, within 8 asked.
* **I3 MISSED**: without the gate the confirmed clear pays in the narrow gaps: in hard-gaps under WD30L, m23e30k reaches at least 3 more than m23e30. m23e30k@WD30L[gaps] 57, m23e30@WD30L[gaps] 58: -1 against the 3 asked.
* **I4 HELD**: the two cures substitute and do not add: under WD30L, m23re30k reaches at most 5 more than the larger of m23re30 and m23e30k. m23re30k@WD30L 153, max(m23re30@WD30L,m23e30k@WD30L) 162: -9, at most 5 asked.
* **I5 HELD**: m23re30k holds the bar in every kind under WD30L. m23re30k@WD30L: 51/48.0 49/47.2 53/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **I6 MISSED**: and under R3D30. m23re30k@R3D30: 49/48.0 35/47.2 46/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **I7 MISSED**: the hold is still worth having: under R3D30, m23re30k reaches at least 10 more than m23. m23re30k@R3D30 130, m23@R3D30 140: -10 against the 10 asked.
* **I8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults m23re30k is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. m23re30k@clean 170, long+fix+forget 174: 4 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated holds five of six bar cells, and it is the weaker half of both pairs it was built from.** The designated `m23re30k` (a 2-of-3 mark at every range, the evidence hold of cap 30 within 80 m and one free reading beyond, the clear confirmed at 0.8) holds all three kinds under WD30L (51, 49, 53 against 48.0, 47.2 and 45.6 needed) and islands (49) and current (46) under R3D30; the gaps kind under R3D30 is 35 against 47.2: **PARTLY SUPPORTED**; five of eight predictions held (I1, I2, I4, I5, I8). Counts of 180 under WD30L: `m23e30` (the run clear, no gate: follow-up 16's pair) **166**, `m23e30k` (no gate, confirmed) 162, `m23` (the mark alone) 158, `m23re30` (gated, run) 155, `m23re30k` 153, `inc` 135 (exact 176); under R3D30: `m23e30` 141, `m23` 140, `m23e30k` 138, `m23re30` 132, `m23re30k` 130, `inc` 125.
* **Taking the gate off the hold pays about ten, measured three times.** I1 held: under R3D30 `m23e30` is 9 above `m23re30` (5 asked); under WD30L it is 11 above (166, 155). POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) the designated against `m23e30` under WD30L +4 / +17 (p 0.0072). Follow-up 18 measured +10 for the same switch on the range-aware mark (`rm23e30` against `rm23re30`). The 24 m gaps under WD30L show where it is paid: `m23e30` 18 of 20, `m23e30k` 17, `m23re30` 11, `m23re30k` 11 (exact 19; `inc` 20). Follow-up 13's bet for the gate (that it keeps the fix smear off far walls) is not supported by any of the three measurements.
* **The confirmed clear is idle again, and the two cures neither substitute nor add: the pair is below either alone.** I2 held (`m23re30k` 153 against `m23re30` 155 under WD30L) and I4 held (the designated is 9 below the larger of `m23re30` and `m23e30k`, where at most 5 above was asked). In all four pairs the confirmed clear is 2 to 4 below its run clear (WD30L 153 against 155 and 162 against 166; R3D30 130 against 132 and 138 against 141), the same direction and size as follow-up 21's (142 against 147 and 163 against 168); none is separable alone (POST HOC `m23re30k` against `m23re30` +0 / +2, p 0.5; against `m23re30` under R3D30 +1 / +3, p 0.63). I3 missed because there is nothing to cure in the gaps kind under WD30L: `m23e30k` 57 against `m23e30` 58, `inc` 60.
* **The hold is worth having only without the gate.** I7 missed: under R3D30 the designated is **10 below** the mark alone (130 against 140; asked 10 above; POST HOC +8 / +18, p 0.076), and the ungated hold is level with it (141 against 140). Under WD30L the ungated hold is 8 above the mark alone (166 against 158). The median number of occupied cells at the last plan under WD30L is 40 for the mark alone, 87 with the ungated hold and 62 with the gated one, so the gate thins the map by about 30% and the reach goes with it; no arm isolates why.
* **The gaps kind decides the bar again.** I6 missed on the gaps cell under R3D30: 35 for the designated, 35 to 41 for the other 2-of-3 arms and 43 for the map that forgets, against 47.2 needed. A sea with no faults costs little: I8 held, `m23re30k@clean` 170 against the clean map's 174 (POST HOC +5 / +9, p 0.42).

**What it does not say.** That a gate or a confirmed clear is never worth having: the set shows no gain from either under two seas that carry the fix smear and dropouts together, and the mechanism each was registered for (the smear's thickening of far walls) was not measured directly. That 80 m, 30 and 0.8 are the right numbers, or that the leak (0.15, made up) is a measured flicker. One arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios (follow-up 22), so the 2 to 4 flights by which the confirmed clear trails its run clear are inside that. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 25: THE WHOLE DETECTOR WITH THE CLEAR CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-confirm-hold-confirm", asked while the DEV runs of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ANY SET OF 8 TO 25 IS FLOWN, so nothing below was shaped by a result)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 19's name (`detector-range-confirm-hold`: the range-aware memory confirmation with a lock) with `-confirm` on the end, and follow-up 24 did the same to follow-up 13 (`range-hold`, then `range-hold-confirm`). The word before the `-confirm` is the hold, so the confirmation is the hold's: **the confirmed clear of follow-up 21** (`K` of the last `min(30, occ)` readings free, `K` = 0.8 of the window). This round is therefore **follow-up 24 with the mark of follow-up 14** (one reading within 80 m, 2-of-3 beyond) and **follow-up 17's whole detector with the clear confirmed**: it asks whether the two smear cures, the range gate on the hold and the confirmed clear, still do one job when the mark is also range-aware, and whether the range-aware mark's advantage (follow-up 17's Q1) survives them. If it meant the ghost or the neighbour test (follow-ups 18 and 23) or something else, say so before the build.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown, and two detectors this round needs are not in the registry (`rm23f80` and `rm23re30k`, below). They, a registry gate row each, their mutants, the set HELD30 and this round's spec are added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD30 is: the same order as every earlier round.

**The rule** (follow-up 17's detector with the clear confirmed). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings and `occ`. A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell **within `near`** is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, one **beyond `near`** in the **gated** form after one free reading and in the **ungated** form by the same `K`-of-`L` rule at every range.

**Conditions** (everything else as follow-ups 3 to 24): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the false rate 0.01 uniform, dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`W`** (the uniform false rate of 0.01, no dropouts) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`rm23`** (follow-up 14's: the range-aware mark, a clear of one), **`rm23re30`** (follow-up 17's designated: the range-aware mark and the gated evidence hold, a run), **`rm23f80`** (the range-aware mark and the confirmed clear at every range), **`rm23re30k`** (**the designated: the range-aware mark and the gated evidence hold with the confirmed clear**) and **`m23re30k`** (follow-up 24's designated: the uniform 2-of-3 mark, the gated confirmed hold); under `WD30L` `inc`, `rm23re30`, `rm23f80` and `rm23re30k` (4); under `W` `inc` and `rm23re30k` (2); and `rm23re30k` on a sea with no faults. Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD30 = seeds 3001 to 3060 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm23re30k` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`W` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** Three prices meet. The range gate loses a cell that a dropout beginning far away takes (follow-up 17's tension, follow-up 18's J1); the confirmed clear clears a leaking dropout sooner (follow-up 21's A4); and the range-aware mark marks a near false return at the first reading (follow-up 9's K4). Only `WD30L` has the leak and only `R3D30` has the far dropouts the range law makes common, so a detector that holds both bars has paid all three.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason).**
* **V1** the confirmed clear pays in the range-aware detector: under `R3D30`, `rm23re30k` reaches at least 5 more than `rm23re30`. (Follow-up 21's A2 and follow-up 24's I2, asked of follow-up 17's detector.)
* **V2** and the range-aware mark's advantage survives it: under `R3D30`, `rm23re30k` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30k`. (Follow-up 17's Q1 predicted ten.)
* **V3** the gate on the hold costs under the joint stress whatever the clear: under `R3D30`, `rm23f80` reaches at least 5 more than `rm23re30k`. (Follow-up 18's J1.)
* **V4** behind the gate the confirmed clear changes little where the dropouts leak: under `WD30L`, `rm23re30k` is within 8 of `rm23re30` (either side). (Follow-up 24's I2.)
* **V5** `rm23re30k` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **V6** and under `WD30L`.
* **V7** it is still a cure where the law does not hold: under `W`, `rm23re30k` reaches at least 10 more than `inc`. (Follow-up 17's Q6.)
* **V8** on a sea with no faults `rm23re30k` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm23f80` against `rm23re30` under `WD30L`, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the pooled occupied cells at the last plan are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 2-of-3, 1-of-1, a cap of 30 and `f` = 0.8 are the right numbers; that the range at the ping is the right gate (follow-up 15); that the pair of cures is better than any other treatment of the smear; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.
