### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 25 (2026-11-03; HELD30 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorRangeConfirmHoldConfirmHeld30.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: NOT SUPPORTED (1 of 6 cells).** The designated `rm23re30k` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current MISSED; WD30L: islands MISSED, gaps MISSED, current MISSED. **3 of 8 predictions held; V1, V2, V5, V6, V7 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 174 (59/59/56); `long+fix+forget` 173 (59/59/55); `inc@R3D30` 124 (43/42/39); `rm23@R3D30` 125 (50/30/45); `rm23re30@R3D30` 118 (49/23/46); `rm23f80@R3D30` 127 (48/33/46); `rm23re30k@R3D30` 119 (52/23/44); `m23re30k@R3D30` 128 (51/29/48); `inc@WD30L` 126 (40/54/32); `rm23re30@WD30L` 82 (30/31/21); `rm23f80@WD30L` 117 (35/53/29); `rm23re30k@WD30L` 87 (34/32/21); `inc@W` 126 (37/59/30); `rm23re30k@W` 99 (35/38/26); `rm23re30k@clean` 171 (57/60/54).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **V1 MISSED**: the confirmed clear pays in the range-aware detector: under R3D30, rm23re30k reaches at least 5 more than rm23re30. rm23re30k@R3D30 119, rm23re30@R3D30 118: +1 against the 5 asked.
* **V2 MISSED**: and the range-aware mark's advantage survives it: under R3D30, rm23re30k reaches at least 5 more than m23re30k. rm23re30k@R3D30 119, m23re30k@R3D30 128: -9 against the 5 asked.
* **V3 HELD**: the gate on the hold costs under the joint stress whatever the clear: under R3D30, rm23f80 reaches at least 5 more than rm23re30k. rm23f80@R3D30 127, rm23re30k@R3D30 119: +8 against the 5 asked.
* **V4 HELD**: behind the gate the confirmed clear changes little where the dropouts leak: under WD30L, rm23re30k is within 8 of rm23re30. rm23re30k@WD30L 87, rm23re30@WD30L 82: 5 apart, within 8 asked.
* **V5 MISSED**: rm23re30k holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. rm23re30k@R3D30: 52/47.2 23/47.2 44/44.8 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **V6 MISSED**: and under WD30L. rm23re30k@WD30L: 34/47.2 32/47.2 21/44.8 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **V7 MISSED**: it is still a cure where the law does not hold: under W, rm23re30k reaches at least 10 more than inc. rm23re30k@W 99, inc@W 126: -27 against the 10 asked.
* **V8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults rm23re30k is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. rm23re30k@clean 171, long+fix+forget 173: 2 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated range-aware detector with the confirmed clear holds one of six bar cells: NOT SUPPORTED.** `rm23re30k` (a 2-of-3 mark beyond 80 m and a first reading within, the evidence hold of cap 30 gated by range, the clear confirmed at 0.8) holds islands under R3D30 (52 against 47.2 needed) and nothing else: gaps 23 under R3D30 (47.2), current 44 under R3D30 (44.8, short by one), and 34, 32 and 21 under WD30L (47.2, 47.2 and 44.8). Three of eight predictions held (V3, V4, V8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `m23re30k` (the uniform 2-of-3 with the same gated hold: follow-up 24's designated) **128**, `rm23f80` (no gate) 127, `rm23` (the mark alone) 125, `inc` 124, `rm23re30k` **119**, `rm23re30` (the run clear, follow-up 17's) 118 (exact 174, clean `long+fix+forget` 173); under WD30L: `inc` 126, `rm23f80` 117, `rm23re30k` **87**, `rm23re30` 82; under W: `inc` 126, `rm23re30k` 99.
* **The confirmed clear is idle again (V1 missed, V4 held).** `rm23re30k` 119 against `rm23re30` 118 under R3D30 (5 more asked) and 87 against 82 under WD30L (within 8 was asked and held); POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) +3 / +2 (p 1.0) and +7 / +2 (p 0.18). That is the direction and size follow-ups 21 and 24 found for the confirmed clear: small, and not separable.
* **The gated hold is what the range-aware mark cannot afford under the joint stress (V3 held), and V2 missed: the uniform mark is 9 above it.** Under R3D30 `rm23f80` (the confirmed clear at every range, no gate) is **8 above** `rm23re30k` (5 asked) and `m23re30k` is **9 above** it (V2 asked `rm23re30k` 5 above `m23re30k`). POST HOC `rm23re30k` against `rm23f80` +20 / +28 (p 0.31), against `m23re30k` +15 / +24 (p 0.20), against `rm23` +10 / +16 (p 0.33), against `inc` +32 / +37 (p 0.63): none of them separable. The gaps kind is where the gated rule is lowest: 23 of 59 for both `rm23re30` and `rm23re30k`, against 29 to 33 for `m23re30k`, `rm23` and `rm23f80` and 42 for `inc`; the 32 m gaps reached (of 20; exact 20) are 4 and 4 for the gated range-aware rules, 9 for `rm23` and `m23re30k`, 14 for `rm23f80` and 15 for `inc`.
* **Under the uniform false rate with leaking dropouts the range-aware detector collapses, and it does so with or without the gate (V6 and V7 missed).** Under WD30L `rm23re30k` reaches 87 and `rm23re30` 82 against 126 for `inc` (POST HOC `rm23re30k` against `inc` +11 / +50, p below 0.0001, and against `rm23f80` +19 / +49, p 0.0004); it collides in 78 flights of 180 (`rm23re30` 81), with a median minimum clearance of 2.9 m (`inc`: 42 collisions, 7.8 m; `rm23f80`: 47). Under W V7 asked `rm23re30k` 10 above `inc`; it is **27 below** (99 against 126; POST HOC +23 / +50, p 0.0021). The same direction as follow-up 26 found for the strict far rule (`rm34e30` 82 against 127 under WD30) and the registered mechanism is the one named there: a first reading within 80 m marks a uniform false return at once. No arm here isolates the near zone's first reading.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing (V8 held).** `rm23re30k@clean` 171 against the clean map's 173 (POST HOC +2 / +4, p 0.69). Collisions under R3D30: `rm23re30` 62, `rm23re30k` 61, `inc` 55, `rm23` 54, `rm23f80` 50, `m23re30k` 48; median occupied cells at the last plan 77 to 78 for the two gated range-aware rules, 98 for `rm23f80`, 69 for `rm23` and 65 for `m23re30k` (`inc` 134).

**What it does not say.** That a range-aware mark cannot work: its first reading within 80 m is what the uniform false rate exploits, and follow-up 28 registers the near confirmation that tests the repair. That the confirmed clear is useless (it is level with the run clear in a set where one arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios, follow-up 22). That the range law and the leak (0.15, made up) are true of a real sonar. That 80 m, 30 and 0.8 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 26: WHICH CONFIRMATION WHEN THE HOLD IS THERE, AND IT KNOWS HOW FAR (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-confirm-hold-range", asked while the DEV runs of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ANY SET OF 8 TO 26 IS FLOWN, so nothing below was shaped by a result)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 20's name (`detector-confirm-hold`: which `M`-of-`N` mark when the evidence hold is there) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-up 17 made on follow-up 16. Taken as **follow-up 20's question asked of the range-aware mark**: with the evidence hold of cap 30 in place at every range, which rule should the mark use **beyond 80 m**, with one reading within it? Follow-up 14 compared `rm23`, `rm24` and `rm34` with a clear of one; follow-up 20 compared the family with the hold but the same rule at every range; this round is the table those two leave: the range-aware family with the hold. If it meant the hold range-gated as well (follow-ups 17, 19 and 24's `re30` forms), say so before the build.

**The question.** Under the range law the far zone is where the false returns are (0.06 a cell a ping at 200 m against 0.0054 at 60 m) and where the vessel has time, so a strict far rule is cheap there; near the vessel a delay costs a collision. Follow-up 20 bet that strictness is affordable under a hold; this round bets it is **more** affordable far away, and puts the price (a near false return marked at the first reading, the law failing) beside it.

**The rule** (follow-up 20's detector with the near rule): a free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** from the sonar at the ping is marked when at least `M` of its last `N` readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is cleared after `min(30, occ)` free readings in a row, at every range (the evidence hold of follow-up 12 as in follow-up 20).

**Conditions** (everything else as follow-ups 3 to 25): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings), **`WD30`** (follow-up 16's: the false rate **uniform** at 0.01 with the same dropouts: the law NOT holding) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the eight detectors **`m11e30`** (a mark of one everywhere: the hold alone), **`rm22e30`**, **`rm33e30`**, **`rm23e30`**, **`rm24e30`** and **`rm34e30`** (**the designated**: 3-of-4 beyond 80 m, one reading within), and, for the comparison with the same rule everywhere, **`m34e30`** (follow-up 20's designated) and **`m23e30`** (follow-up 16's); under `WD30` `m11e30`, `rm23e30`, `rm34e30` and `m34e30` (4); and `rm34e30` on a sea with no faults. Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD31 = seeds 3101 to 3160 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm34e30` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30`**: three cells, the range law with dropouts. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`WD30` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar: they ask what the bet on the law costs.)

**The tension, said before the data.** A strict far rule delays marking a wall the long sonar sees early, and a dropout that begins before the confirmation hides the obstacle from the hold altogether (follow-up 16's tension), which the range law makes likeliest far away; and the near zone, marked at the first reading, takes a near false return into the plan at once.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AA because every single letter is taken).**
* **AA1** the family still collapses under a hold: under `R3D30`, the five confirming rules (`rm22e30`, `rm33e30`, `rm23e30`, `rm24e30`, `rm34e30`) have pooled counts within 20 of each other. (Follow-up 20's Y1, with the near zone alike in all five.)
* **AA2** every far confirmation repairs the range-law false returns: under `R3D30` each of the five reaches at least 15 more than `m11e30`. (Follow-up 9's K1.)
* **AA3** strictness pays far away where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `rm34e30` reaches more than `rm23e30`.
* **AA4** and a run of three is dearer than a run of two there: under `R3D30`, `rm33e30` reaches at least 5 fewer than `rm22e30`. (A far real obstacle at the sonar's edge is read three times in a row about a fifth of the time.)
* **AA5** `rm34e30` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AA6** the bet on the law has a price: under `WD30`, `rm34e30` reaches at least 10 fewer than `m34e30`. (Follow-up 9's K4: the near zone marks a uniform false return at its first reading.)
* **AA7** range-awareness pays where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `rm34e30` reaches at least 5 more than `m34e30`. (No delay near.)
* **AA8** on a sea with no faults `rm34e30` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23e30` against `rm23e30` under `R3D30`, `rm34e30` against `m11e30` under `WD30`, the per-kind and per-gap counts are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m and 3-of-4 are the right numbers or that the far rule should be a step and not a smooth function of range; that the readings of a cell are independent; that the range law is true of a real sonar; that a gated hold (follow-up 24) or a confirmed clear (follow-up 21) changes the order of the rules. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs four detectors the registry does not have (`rm22e30`, `rm33e30`, `rm24e30`, `rm34e30`, each a registry row), the set HELD31 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD31 is (as follow-up 25's are).


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 26 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD31 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers: the detector module with the five optional fields of this stretch (`clear.fNear`, `ghost.carry`, `ghost.zone`, `ghost.at`, `lock`) and 77 more registry rows (59 mutants of the new lines, 55 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 3 equivalent and said; 154 mechanical mutants of the 77 rows, all red); the table of sets (HELD30 to HELD63, seeds 3101 to 3160 for this one); the study engine with its new operation `noBar` and the 34 more specs (1,377 mechanical mutants of the specs, all red by the row that holds them to the registrations; 10 of the helpers they call and 2 of the list of operations; 12 of the engine's `noBar` line, 9 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 2 equivalent and said; each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against a literal copy of the registrations in the gate, and the table that generated both against the registered prose by a script (272 predictions' operations, thresholds, directions and arms mechanically, 14 by hand: no disagreement); the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `m11e30@R3D30` 13/18; `rm22e30@R3D30` 13/18; `rm33e30@R3D30` 15/18; `rm23e30@R3D30` 14/18; `rm24e30@R3D30` 14/18; `rm34e30@R3D30` 12/18; `m34e30@R3D30` 13/18; `m23e30@R3D30` 15/18; `m11e30@WD30` 13/18; `rm23e30@WD30` 7/18; `rm34e30@WD30` 9/18; `m34e30@WD30` 14/18; `rm34e30@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `rm34e30`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD31 = seeds 3101 to 3160 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 26 (2026-11-03; HELD31 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorConfirmHoldRangeHeld31.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: NOT SUPPORTED (1 of 3 cells).** The designated `rm34e30` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current MISSED. **4 of 8 predictions held; AA2, AA3, AA5, AA7 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 174 (60/59/55); `long+fix+forget` 170 (56/59/55); `m11e30@R3D30` 130 (40/49/41); `rm22e30@R3D30` 139 (52/39/48); `rm33e30@R3D30` 131 (51/36/44); `rm23e30@R3D30` 136 (51/40/45); `rm24e30@R3D30` 144 (53/42/49); `rm34e30@R3D30` 126 (50/33/43); `m34e30@R3D30` 132 (50/32/50); `m23e30@R3D30` 144 (55/42/47); `m11e30@WD30` 111 (37/38/36); `rm23e30@WD30` 92 (38/28/26); `rm34e30@WD30` 82 (33/22/27); `m34e30@WD30` 127 (50/31/46); `rm34e30@clean` 168 (56/60/52).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AA1 HELD**: the family still collapses under a hold: under R3D30, the five confirming rules have pooled counts within 20 of each other. rm22e30 139, rm33e30 131, rm23e30 136, rm24e30 144, rm34e30 126: 18 apart (within 20 asked).
* **AA2 MISSED**: every far confirmation repairs the range-law false returns: under R3D30 each of the five reaches at least 15 more than m11e30. rm22e30 139, rm33e30 131, rm23e30 136, rm24e30 144, rm34e30 126 against m11e30@R3D30 130 (15 asked).
* **AA3 MISSED**: strictness pays far away where the law holds: under R3D30, rm34e30 reaches more than rm23e30. rm34e30@R3D30 126 against rm23e30@R3D30 136.
* **AA4 HELD**: and a run of three is dearer than a run of two there: under R3D30, rm33e30 reaches at least 5 fewer than rm22e30. rm22e30@R3D30 139, rm33e30@R3D30 131: +8 against the 5 asked.
* **AA5 MISSED**: rm34e30 holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. rm34e30@R3D30: 50/48.0 33/47.2 43/44.0 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AA6 HELD**: the bet on the law has a price: under WD30, rm34e30 reaches at least 10 fewer than m34e30. m34e30@WD30 127, rm34e30@WD30 82: +45 against the 10 asked.
* **AA7 MISSED**: range-awareness pays where the law holds: under R3D30, rm34e30 reaches at least 5 more than m34e30. rm34e30@R3D30 126, m34e30@R3D30 132: -6 against the 5 asked.
* **AA8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults rm34e30 is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. rm34e30@clean 168, long+fix+forget 170: 2 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated range-aware strict rule holds one of three bar cells: NOT SUPPORTED.** `rm34e30` (3 of 4 beyond 80 m, a single reading within it, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range) holds islands under R3D30 (50 against 48.0 needed), misses gaps (33 against 47.2) and misses current by one (43 against 44.0). Four of eight predictions held (AA1, AA4, AA6, AA8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `rm24e30` **144**, `m23e30` (2-of-3 at every range) **144**, `rm22e30` 139, `rm23e30` 136, `m34e30` (3-of-4 at every range) 132, `rm33e30` 131, `m11e30` (a mark of one: the hold alone) 130, `rm34e30` **126** (exact 174, clean `long+fix+forget` 170); under WD30: `m34e30` 127, `m11e30` 111, `rm23e30` 92, `rm34e30` 82.
* **The designated is the lowest of the five far confirmations, not the best (AA3 and AA7 missed).** Under R3D30 `rm34e30` is **10 below** `rm23e30` (AA3 asked it above) and **6 below** `m34e30`, the same strictness at every range (AA7 asked 5 above); the uniform 2-of-3 rule `m23e30` is 18 above it. POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `rm34e30` against `rm23e30` +19 / +29 (p 0.19), against `rm22e30` +13 / +26 (p 0.053), against `rm24e30` +16 / +34 (p 0.015), against `m34e30` +21 / +27 (p 0.47), against `m23e30` +14 / +32 (p 0.011). The five far confirmations (`rm22e30`, `rm33e30`, `rm23e30`, `rm24e30`, `rm34e30`) are 18 apart (126 to 144; AA1 held, 20 allowed), with the strictest at the bottom and `rm24e30` at the top. AA4 held: a run of three far (`rm33e30` 131) is 8 below a run of two (`rm22e30` 139).
* **None of the far confirmations repairs the range-law false returns by the registered 15 (AA2 missed), because the hold alone is already at their level.** `m11e30` reaches 130 under R3D30 and the five reach 126 to 144 (the nearest to the asked 15 is `rm24e30`, 14 above; `rm34e30` is 4 below). What the confirmations change is which kind they hold: `m11e30` holds the gaps kind (49 against 47.2) and misses islands (40 against 48.0) and current (41); every confirming arm holds islands (50 to 55) and misses gaps (32 to 42), as in follow-ups 16, 17, 20 to 22 and 28. The 24 m gaps reached under R3D30 (of 20; exact 19, clean `long+fix+forget` 19): `m11e30` 14, `rm24e30` 10, `m23e30` 9, `rm22e30` 7, `rm23e30` 6, `m34e30` 6, `rm33e30` 4, `rm34e30` 4.
* **The bet on the law has a price, and it is far larger than registered (AA6 held).** Under the uniform false rate `rm34e30` reaches **82**, **45 below** `m34e30` (127; 10 asked) and 29 below the hold alone (111): it collides in 83 flights of 180, leaves the arena in 11 and times out in 4, with a median minimum clearance of 2.0 m (`m34e30`: 51 collisions, 8.2 m). `rm23e30` is 92. POST HOC `rm34e30` against `m34e30` under WD30 +14 / +59 (p below 0.0001), against `m11e30` +18 / +47 (p 0.0004), against `rm23e30` +27 / +37 (p 0.26). A first reading within 80 m marks a uniform false return at once, and the strict far rule does not help there; no arm isolates the near zone's first reading from the rest (follow-up 28 registers the near confirmation).
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing (AA8 held).** `rm34e30@clean` 168 against the clean map's 170 (POST HOC +4 / +6, p 0.75). Median plans under R3D30 are 125 to 131 for the five range-aware rules and the mark of one, 116 for `m23e30` and **63 for `m34e30`** (a mean of 103 marks a flight against 334 to 674 for the five range-aware rules).

**What it does not say.** That range-awareness is useless: the range-aware rules lose to the uniform ones by 0 to 18 here, in a set where one arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios (follow-up 22), and only the strictest of them is a candidate for the bottom. That a first reading within 80 m is what sinks `rm34e30` under the uniform false rate (it is the registered mechanism and is not isolated by an arm). That 80 m, 30 and 3-of-4 are the right numbers, or that the range law (a made-up law) is true of a real sonar. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 27: WHICH RANGE THE CONFIRMED HOLD GOES BY (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-confirm", asked while the DEV runs of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ANY SET OF 8 TO 27 IS FLOWN, so nothing below was shaped by a result)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 15's name (`detector-hold-range`: which range the hold goes by, and whether the gate is a step or a ramp) with `-confirm` on the end, and follow-up 24's name (`detector-range-hold-confirm`) with the emphasis moved the way follow-up 15 moved follow-up 13's. The confirmation is the hold's, as in follow-up 24: **the confirmed clear of follow-up 21**. Taken as **follow-up 15's question asked of the confirmed hold**: the clear is `K` = 0.8 of the last `min(30, occ)` readings free, the hold's length is gated by a range, and the range is the cell's range **at the ping**, **at its marking** or **the nearest it has read occupied from**, as a **step** at 80 m or a **ramp** to 200 m. The mark is follow-up 24's (2-of-3 at every range), so the hold is the only thing that varies. If it meant something else, say so before the build.

**The question.** Follow-up 15 asked which range suits a hold that is a run of free readings. A confirmed clear is not a run: it clears a cell that flickers occupied, and it clears a leaking dropout sooner. Whether the choice of range still matters when the clear tolerates an occupied reading in five is the question, and it is asked under the two conditions that carry the two prices (far dropouts, a leaking dropout).

**The rule** (follow-up 24's detector with the gate's range and shape chosen): a free cell is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range; a marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L` readings were free, where `L = min(g(r), occ)` and `g(r)` is **step**: 30 if `r` <= 80 m, else 1; or **ramp**: `max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))`; `r` is the cell's range **`ping`** (now), **`mark`** (at its marking) or **`min`** (the least range it has read occupied from since it was last cleared).

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 26): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the false rate 0.01 uniform, dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the seven detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23e30k`** (the hold at every range), **`m23re30k`** (follow-up 24's designated: step, `ping`), **`m23mk30k`** (step, `mark`), **`m23mn30k`** (step, `min`), **`m23rp30k`** (ramp, `ping`) and **`m23rn30k`** (**the designated: ramp, `min`**, as follow-up 15's); under `WD30L` `inc`, `m23e30k`, `m23re30k` and `m23rn30k` (4); and `m23re30k` and `m23rn30k` on a sea with no faults (2). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD32 = seeds 3201 to 3260 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23rn30k` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise.

**The tension, said before the data.** The ramp is shorter than the step inside 80 m and longer outside it, so it gives up some near hold for the 80 to 160 m band, where the fix smear is dearest; the confirmed clear already cleans that smear (follow-up 21), so the ramp may buy nothing the clear does not; and `min` keeps a wall held after the vessel has passed it, which costs only if the plan uses the cells behind.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AB).**
* **AB1** the range at marking is the wrong range: under `R3D30`, `m23mk30k` reaches at least 10 fewer than `m23re30k`. (Follow-up 15's T1: a cell marked from 150 m is a far cell for ever.)
* **AB2** the nearest range ever read does as well as the range now: under `R3D30`, `m23mn30k` is within 10 of `m23re30k` (either side). (Follow-up 15's T2.)
* **AB3** the ramp does as well as the step: under `R3D30`, `m23rp30k` is within 10 of `m23re30k` (either side). (Follow-up 15's T3.)
* **AB4** the designated covers the long dropout: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30k` reaches at least 10 more than `inc`.
* **AB5** `m23rn30k` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AB6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AB7** the gate costs against a hold at every range under the joint stress: under `R3D30`, `m23e30k` reaches at least 5 more than `m23rn30k`. (Follow-up 18's J1 and follow-up 24's I1.)
* **AB8** on a sea with no faults `m23rn30k` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23re30k` against `m23rn30k` under `WD30L`, the per-kind and per-gap counts are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 200 m, 30, 0.8 and 2-of-3 are the right numbers; that a step and a linear ramp are the only shapes or that the minimum since the last clear is the right memory (follow-up 15's caveats stand); that the leak is a measured flicker. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs four detectors the registry does not have (`m23mk30k`, `m23mn30k`, `m23rp30k`, `m23rn30k`, each a registry row), the set HELD32 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD32 is (as follow-ups 25 and 26's are).


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 27 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD32 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers: the detector module with the five optional fields of this stretch (`clear.fNear`, `ghost.carry`, `ghost.zone`, `ghost.at`, `lock`) and 77 more registry rows (59 mutants of the new lines, 55 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 3 equivalent and said; 154 mechanical mutants of the 77 rows, all red); the table of sets (HELD30 to HELD63, seeds 3201 to 3260 for this one); the study engine with its new operation `noBar` and the 34 more specs (1,377 mechanical mutants of the specs, all red by the row that holds them to the registrations; 10 of the helpers they call and 2 of the list of operations; 12 of the engine's `noBar` line, 9 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 2 equivalent and said; each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against a literal copy of the registrations in the gate, and the table that generated both against the registered prose by a script (272 predictions' operations, thresholds, directions and arms mechanically, 14 by hand: no disagreement); the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `inc@R3D30` 14/18; `m23e30k@R3D30` 12/18; `m23re30k@R3D30` 13/18; `m23mk30k@R3D30` 16/18; `m23mn30k@R3D30` 14/18; `m23rp30k@R3D30` 12/18; `m23rn30k@R3D30` 12/18; `inc@WD30L` 17/18; `m23e30k@WD30L` 17/18; `m23re30k@WD30L` 13/18; `m23rn30k@WD30L` 16/18; `m23re30k@clean` 17/18; `m23rn30k@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `m23rn30k`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD32 = seeds 3201 to 3260 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 27 (2026-11-03; HELD32 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorHoldRangeConfirmHeld32.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (5 of 6 cells).** The designated `m23rn30k` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD; WD30L: islands HELD, gaps HELD, current HELD. **4 of 8 predictions held; AB1, AB3, AB5, AB7 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 170 (58/58/54); `long+fix+forget` 168 (58/56/54); `inc@R3D30` 129 (41/48/40); `m23e30k@R3D30` 141 (52/43/46); `m23re30k@R3D30` 131 (54/31/46); `m23mk30k@R3D30` 128 (50/35/43); `m23mn30k@R3D30` 128 (50/34/44); `m23rp30k@R3D30` 143 (50/45/48); `m23rn30k@R3D30` 145 (52/45/48); `inc@WD30L` 130 (42/55/33); `m23e30k@WD30L` 160 (53/55/52); `m23re30k@WD30L` 143 (47/49/47); `m23rn30k@WD30L` 159 (51/55/53); `m23re30k@clean` 166 (57/59/50); `m23rn30k@clean` 171 (58/59/54).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AB1 MISSED**: the range at marking is the wrong range: under R3D30, m23mk30k reaches at least 10 fewer than m23re30k. m23re30k@R3D30 131, m23mk30k@R3D30 128: +3 against the 10 asked.
* **AB2 HELD**: the nearest range ever read does as well as the range now: under R3D30, m23mn30k is within 10 of m23re30k. m23mn30k@R3D30 128, m23re30k@R3D30 131: 3 apart, within 10 asked.
* **AB3 MISSED**: the ramp does as well as the step: under R3D30, m23rp30k is within 10 of m23re30k. m23rp30k@R3D30 143, m23re30k@R3D30 131: 12 apart, within 10 asked.
* **AB4 HELD**: the designated covers the long dropout: under R3D30, m23rn30k reaches at least 10 more than inc. m23rn30k@R3D30 145, inc@R3D30 129: +16 against the 10 asked.
* **AB5 MISSED**: m23rn30k holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. m23rn30k@R3D30: 52/46.4 45/46.4 48/43.2 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AB6 HELD**: and under WD30L. m23rn30k@WD30L: 51/46.4 55/46.4 53/43.2 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AB7 MISSED**: the gate costs against a hold at every range under the joint stress: under R3D30, m23e30k reaches at least 5 more than m23rn30k. m23e30k@R3D30 141, m23rn30k@R3D30 145: -4 against the 5 asked.
* **AB8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults m23rn30k is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. m23rn30k@clean 171, long+fix+forget 168: 3 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated ramp holds five of six bar cells, and it is the step at 80 m that pays, not the range it goes by.** The designated `m23rn30k` (a 2-of-3 mark, the evidence hold of cap 30 gated by a ramp from 80 m to 200 m on the nearest range ever read, the clear confirmed at 0.8) holds all three kinds under WD30L (51, 55, 53 against 46.4, 46.4 and 43.2 needed) and islands and current under R3D30 (52 and 48); the gaps kind under R3D30 is 45 against the 46.4 needed (1.4 short): **PARTLY SUPPORTED**; four of eight predictions held (AB2, AB4, AB6, AB8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `m23rn30k` **145**, `m23rp30k` (the ramp on the range at the ping) 143, `m23e30k` (the hold at every range) 141, `m23re30k` (the step on the range at the ping: follow-up 24's designated) 131, `inc` 129, `m23mk30k` (the step on the range at marking) 128, `m23mn30k` (the step on the nearest range) 128 (exact 170, clean `long+fix+forget` 168); under WD30L: `m23e30k` 160, `m23rn30k` 159, `m23re30k` 143, `inc` 130.
* **The step costs 12 to 17 flights and the ramp costs nothing.** AB3 missed: `m23rp30k` is **12 above** `m23re30k` under R3D30 (within 10 was asked), and the designated is 14 above it (145, 131). AB7 missed the other way: the hold at every range is not above the ramp (`m23e30k` 141, 4 **below** `m23rn30k` where at least 5 above was asked); under WD30L they are level (160, 159). POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `m23rn30k` against `m23re30k` under R3D30 +26 / +12 (p 0.034), against `m23mk30k` +29 / +12 (p 0.012), against `m23mn30k` +31 / +14 (p 0.016), against `m23e30k` +13 / +9 (p 0.52), against `m23rp30k` +11 / +9 (p 0.82); under WD30L against `m23re30k` +25 / +9 (p 0.0090) and against `m23e30k` +6 / +7 (p 1.0). The step's cost is in the gaps kind: gaps reached under R3D30 (of 58) are `m23re30k` 31, `m23mn30k` 34, `m23mk30k` 35 against `m23rn30k` 45, `m23rp30k` 45, `m23e30k` 43 and `inc` 48; the 24 m gaps reached (of 20; exact 18) are 2, 3 and 3 for the three steps and 9, 11 and 8 for the three that do not step. The median number of occupied cells at the last plan under R3D30 is 62 to 66 for the steps and 86 to 87 for the ramps and the hold at every range (`inc` 137); under WD30L 62 for the step and 82 to 84 for the other two. That is follow-up 24's observation (a gate thins the map by about 30% and the reach goes with it) with the shape of the gate now varied: the thinning is the step's, and the ramp does not do it.
* **Which range the gate goes by is not the question.** AB1 missed and AB2 held: the range at marking (`m23mk30k` 128) is 3 below the range at the ping (131), not 10 below, and the nearest range ever read (`m23mn30k` 128) is level with both; under the ramp the two ranges are 2 apart (`m23rp30k` 143, `m23rn30k` 145; POST HOC +11 / +9, p 0.82). Follow-up 15's bet that a cell marked from 150 m stays a far cell for ever is not seen: the cost is in whether the gate is a step, not in the range it reads.
* **The ramp covers the long dropout, and the cost of the confirmed clear is small.** AB4 held: `m23rn30k` is **16 above** `inc` under R3D30 (10 asked; POST HOC +33 / +17, p 0.033) and 29 above it under WD30L (159 against 130; +41 / +12, p 0.0001). A sea with no faults costs nothing (AB8 held): `m23rn30k@clean` 171 against the clean map's 168 (POST HOC +6 / +3, p 0.51); `m23re30k@clean` 166.
* **The gaps kind decides the bar again, by 1.4 flights for the designated and by more for every other 2-of-3 arm under R3D30** (`m23e30k` 43, `m23rp30k` 45, the steps 31 to 35 against 46.4). Collisions under R3D30: `m23rn30k` 33 and `m23rp30k` 33, `m23e30k` 36, `m23re30k` 44, `m23mk30k` 45, `m23mn30k` 48, `inc` 51.

**What it does not say.** That a ramp is better than a step in general: one set, in which one arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios (follow-up 22); the step's cost here is 12 to 17, which is above that and below the number of tests made unadjusted. That the ramp's 80 to 200 m, the cap of 30 and 0.8 are the right numbers, or that the leak (0.15, made up) is a measured flicker. That the range does not matter under a step with other thresholds: the steps here were all at 80 m. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 28: THE NEAR ZONE CONFIRMED TOO, GRADED BY RANGE (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-confirm-range-confirm", asked while the DEV runs of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ANY SET OF 8 TO 28 IS FLOWN, so nothing below was shaped by a result)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 17's name (`detector-hold-confirm-range`: the whole detector, every end knowing how far) with `-confirm` on the end. The trailing confirmation is **not** taken as the hold's confirmed clear: that is follow-up 25 (`range-confirm-hold-confirm`), whose words say it. It is taken as **a second confirmation, in the other zone**: every range-aware mark in the notes (follow-ups 9, 10, 14, 17, 18, 19, 26) marks within 80 m at the **first** reading, and the price of that bet is registered each time (follow-up 9's K4, 14's S5, 17's Q5, 26's AA6: the near zone takes a uniform false return into the plan at once). This round **confirms the near zone as well**, with a rule lighter than the far one, so the confirmation is graded by range: strict far, light near. If it meant the hold's confirmed clear, that is follow-up 25; if it meant something else, say so before the build.

**The question.** A false return marked within 80 m is the dearest kind (the vessel meets it), and it is the kind the range-aware mark lets through when the false rate does not fall near the vessel (follow-up 7's `W`, a uniform 0.01). A near rule of two readings of three costs a ping or two of delay on a real wall and cuts a uniform false return to its square. Does the near confirmation repair the uniform sea without costing the range-law sea?

**The rule** (follow-up 26's detector with a near rule other than one reading): a free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked when at least `M` of its last `N` readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) when at least `Mn` of its last `Nn` were; a marked cell is cleared after `min(30, occ)` free readings in a row at every range (the evidence hold of follow-up 12, as in follow-up 26).

**Conditions** (everything else as follow-ups 3 to 27): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings and the range law) and **`WD30`** (follow-up 16's: the false rate **uniform** at 0.01 with the same dropouts): the sea where the law holds and the sea where it does not; and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `R3D30` and `WD30` the six detectors **`m11e30`** (a mark of one everywhere: the hold alone), **`m23e30`** (2-of-3 everywhere: follow-up 16's), **`m34e30`** (3-of-4 everywhere: follow-up 20's), **`rm34e30`** (3-of-4 beyond 80 m, one reading within: follow-up 26's designated), **`n22f34e30`** (3-of-4 beyond, 2 in a row within) and **`n23f34e30`** (**the designated: 3-of-4 beyond 80 m, 2-of-3 within**) (12 arms); and `n23f34e30` on a sea with no faults. Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD33 = seeds 3301 to 3360 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `n23f34e30` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30`**: six cells, both seas. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise.

**The tension, said before the data.** The near zone is where a delay costs a collision, and a dropout that begins before the near confirmation hides the obstacle from the hold altogether (follow-up 16's tension); the light rule is bought against the uniform sea and paid for in the range-law sea, where near false returns are rare and the delay buys little.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AC).**
* **AC1** a near confirmation repairs the uniform sea the range-aware mark could not: under `WD30`, `n23f34e30` reaches at least 10 more than `rm34e30`. (Follow-up 26's AA6 is the price it removes.)
* **AC2** and costs little where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `n23f34e30` is within 8 of `rm34e30` (either side).
* **AC3** graded beats uniform strictness where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `n23f34e30` reaches at least 5 more than `m34e30`. (No strictness near.)
* **AC4** and beats a uniform light rule: under `R3D30`, `n23f34e30` reaches at least 5 more than `m23e30`. (A strict rule far, where the false returns are.)
* **AC5** `n23f34e30` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AC6** and under `WD30`.
* **AC7** the memory in the near rule is worth little: under `R3D30`, `n22f34e30` is within 8 of `n23f34e30` (either side). (The near zone is a sixth of the sector's area.)
* **AC8** on a sea with no faults `n23f34e30` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m11e30` against `m34e30` under each sea, the per-kind and per-gap counts are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 3-of-4 far and 2-of-3 near are the right numbers or that the grading should be a step and not a smooth function of range; that the hold at every range and a run-clear are the right hold (follow-ups 13 to 25 are that table); that the range law is true of a real sonar. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs two detectors the registry does not have (`n22f34e30` and `n23f34e30`, each a registry row; `rm34e30` is follow-up 26's row), the set HELD33 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD33 is.

* **AC2 MISSED**: and costs little where the law holds: under R3D30, n23f34e30 is within 8 of rm34e30. n23f34e30@R3D30 132, rm34e30@R3D30 119: 13 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AC3 MISSED**: graded beats uniform strictness where the law holds: under R3D30, n23f34e30 reaches at least 5 more than m34e30. n23f34e30@R3D30 132, m34e30@R3D30 136: -4 against the 5 asked.
* **AC4 MISSED**: and beats a uniform light rule: under R3D30, n23f34e30 reaches at least 5 more than m23e30. n23f34e30@R3D30 132, m23e30@R3D30 129: +3 against the 5 asked.
* **AC5 MISSED**: n23f34e30 holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. n23f34e30@R3D30: 51/48.0 34/47.2 47/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AC6 MISSED**: and under WD30. n23f34e30@WD30: 49/48.0 38/47.2 44/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AC7 HELD**: the memory in the near rule is worth little: under R3D30, n22f34e30 is within 8 of n23f34e30. n22f34e30@R3D30 130, n23f34e30@R3D30 132: 2 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AC8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults n23f34e30 is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. n23f34e30@clean 166, long+fix+forget 168: 2 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The graded rule holds three of six bar cells and misses the gaps kind in both seas: NOT SUPPORTED.** The designated `n23f34e30` (3 of 4 beyond 80 m, 2 of 3 within, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range) holds islands and current under R3D30 (51 and 47 against 48.0 and 45.6 needed) and islands under WD30 (49); it misses gaps in both (34 under R3D30, 38 under WD30, against the 47.2 needed) and current under WD30 by 2 (44 against 45.6). Three of eight predictions held (AC1, AC7, AC8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `m11e30` (a mark of one: the hold alone) **137**, `m34e30` 136, `n23f34e30` 132, `n22f34e30` 130, `m23e30` 129, `rm34e30` 119 (exact 176, clean `long+fix+forget` 168); under WD30: `m34e30` **140**, `n22f34e30` 134, `n23f34e30` 131, `m23e30` 130, `m11e30` 105, `rm34e30` 80.
* **The near confirmation repairs the uniform sea the range-aware mark could not, by a wide margin (AC1 held).** Under WD30 `n23f34e30` is **51 above** `rm34e30` (131 against 80; 10 asked): the range-aware 3-of-4 with a first reading within 80 m collides in 77 flights of 180, times out in 12 and leaves the arena in 11, with a median minimum clearance of 3.8 m (the designated: 41 collisions, 8.6 m). POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `n23f34e30` against `rm34e30` under WD30 +61 / +10 (p below 0.0001), and against the mark of one (`m11e30`) +44 / +18 (p 0.0013).
* **Where the law holds the graded rule is not at the range-aware mark's level, it is 13 above it, and it is level with every uniform rule (AC2, AC3 and AC4 missed).** Under R3D30 `n23f34e30` is 13 above `rm34e30` (within 8 asked), 4 below `m34e30` (5 above asked) and 3 above `m23e30` (5 above asked). Five of the six arms lie within 8 of each other (129 to 137; `rm34e30`, the one with a first-reading mark within 80 m, is the outlier at 119). POST HOC `n23f34e30` against `m34e30` +16 / +20 (p 0.62), against `m23e30` +24 / +21 (p 0.77), against `m11e30` +28 / +33 (p 0.61), against `rm34e30` +30 / +17 (p 0.079). Nothing here separates a strict rule far and a light one near from a uniform rule, in a set where one arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios (follow-up 22).
* **The gaps kind decides the bar again, and the mark of one is the arm that keeps it.** Under R3D30 `m11e30` reaches 49 of 59 in the gaps kind (the bar's 47.2) and 37 in the current kind (45.6): it holds the gaps cell and misses the current cell, and the confirming rules do the opposite (gaps 30 to 38 and current 41 to 47 for the five that confirm within or beyond 80 m). The 24 m gaps reached under R3D30 (of 20; exact 19, clean `long+fix+forget` 20): `m11e30` 11, `m34e30` 8, `n22f34e30` 6, `n23f34e30` 5, `m23e30` 4, `rm34e30` 2; under WD30 `m11e30` 7, `n22f34e30` 5, `n23f34e30` 4, `m23e30` 4, `m34e30` 3, `rm34e30` 3. That confirming a mark costs the narrow gaps is what follow-ups 16, 17, 20, 21 and 22 found with the faults they were built against; this set adds that grading the confirmation by range does not recover them.
* **The memory in the near rule is worth nothing here (AC7 held), and a sea with no faults costs nothing (AC8 held).** `n22f34e30` 130 against `n23f34e30` 132 under R3D30 (134 and 131 under WD30; POST HOC +15 / +13, p 0.85 and +12 / +15, p 0.70). `n23f34e30@clean` 166 against the clean map's 168 (POST HOC +5 / +7, p 0.77).

**What it does not say.** That a graded confirmation is worse than a uniform one: nothing here separates them, and the designated misses its bar in the same kind the uniform confirming rules miss it in. That the first-reading mark within 80 m is what sinks `rm34e30` under WD30: `n22f34e30` and `n23f34e30` differ from it in the near rule and are 54 and 51 flights above it, but no arm changes the far rule alone. That 80 m, 30 and the rules are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 29: THE SECOND HOLD, ON THE CONFIRMED HOLD (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-confirm-hold", asked while the DEV runs of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ANY SET OF 8 TO 29 IS FLOWN, so nothing below was shaped by a result)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 24's name (`detector-range-hold-confirm`: the range-gated hold with the clear confirmed) with `-hold` on the end, the move follow-up 18 made on follow-up 17. Follow-up 18's reading is kept: **a second hold, on what has been confirmed**, in the two ways it can be done: take the range gate off the hold (the confirmed evidence hold at every range) and add **the ghost** (a cell cleared after at least 4 occupied readings is re-marked at one reading for 60 readings of that cell; no neighbour test: that is follow-up 23's). The four combinations are flown. The tension it answers is registered in both predecessors: the gate lets a dropout that begins far away take the cell (follow-up 17, follow-up 24's I1), and the confirmed clear clears a leaking dropout sooner (follow-up 21's A4). If it meant only one of the two, or something else, say so before the build.

**The rule** (follow-up 24's detector with two switches). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings and `occ`. A free cell is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range; a marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, where `L` is 30 for a marked cell **within 80 m** and 1 **beyond** it in the **gated** form, and 30 at every range in the **ungated** form; **with the ghost**, a cleared cell with `occ` of at least 4 keeps its window and evidence for 60 readings, and a reading of it as occupied in that time marks it at once with `occ` put back to 4.

**Conditions** (everything else as follow-ups 3 to 28): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the false rate 0.01 uniform, dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where the ghost is tested, its false returns repeating by design) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `R3D30` and `WD30L` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23e30k`** (the confirmed hold at every range: follow-up 24's), **`m23re30k`** (the gated confirmed hold: follow-up 24's designated), **`m23re30kg`** (gated, with the ghost) and **`m23e30kg`** (**the designated: at every range, with the ghost**) (10 arms); under `P` `m23re30k` and `m23re30kg` (2); and `m23e30kg` on a sea with no faults. Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD34 = seeds 3401 to 3460 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23e30kg` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise.

**The tension, said before the data.** The hold at every range is the hold follow-up 13 bet against (it brings back the fix smear far from the vessel; the confirmed clear is the smear's cure, so this may be cheap here); and the ghost re-marks a persistent phantom at its first reading, which the persistent mix is built to find. The ghost is also the one stage that can undo the confirmed clear's own price: a leaking dropout cleared sooner is a ghost cell that the next occupied reading restores.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AD).**
* **AD1** the gate costs under the joint stress: under `R3D30`, `m23e30k` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30k`. (Follow-up 18's J1 and follow-up 24's I1, asked of the confirmed hold.)
* **AD2** the ghost helps the gated hold: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kg` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30k`. (Follow-up 18's J3.)
* **AD3** and little when the hold is everywhere: under `R3D30`, `m23e30kg` is within 8 of `m23e30k` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J4.)
* **AD4** the ghost answers the leak: under `WD30L`, `m23e30kg` reaches at least 5 more than `m23e30k`. (The confirmed clear clears a leaking dropout sooner; the ghost restores it at one reading.)
* **AD5** and the ghost has a price under the persistent mix: under `P`, `m23re30kg` reaches at least 5 fewer than `m23re30k`. (A persistent false return that passed the confirmation is re-marked at its first reading each time it is cleared.)
* **AD6** `m23e30kg` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AD7** and under `WD30L`.
* **AD8** on a sea with no faults `m23e30kg` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23e30kg` against `m23re30k` under each sea, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers for the ghost, that the ghost should be a count of readings and not a length of time, that 80 m, 2-of-3, 30 and 0.8 are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs two detectors the registry does not have (`m23e30kg` and `m23re30kg`, each a registry row), the set HELD34 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD34 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 29 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD34 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers: the detector module with the five optional fields of this stretch (`clear.fNear`, `ghost.carry`, `ghost.zone`, `ghost.at`, `lock`) and 77 more registry rows (59 mutants of the new lines, 55 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 3 equivalent and said; 154 mechanical mutants of the 77 rows, all red); the table of sets (HELD30 to HELD63, seeds 3401 to 3460 for this one); the study engine with its new operation `noBar` and the 34 more specs (1,377 mechanical mutants of the specs, all red by the row that holds them to the registrations; 10 of the helpers they call and 2 of the list of operations; 12 of the engine's `noBar` line, 9 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 2 equivalent and said; each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against a literal copy of the registrations in the gate, and the table that generated both against the registered prose by a script (272 predictions' operations, thresholds, directions and arms mechanically, 14 by hand: no disagreement); the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `inc@R3D30` 14/18; `m23e30k@R3D30` 12/18; `m23re30k@R3D30` 13/18; `m23re30kg@R3D30` 11/18; `m23e30kg@R3D30` 13/18; `inc@WD30L` 17/18; `m23e30k@WD30L` 17/18; `m23re30k@WD30L` 13/18; `m23re30kg@WD30L` 16/18; `m23e30kg@WD30L` 18/18; `m23re30k@P` 13/18; `m23re30kg@P` 15/18; `m23e30kg@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `m23e30kg`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD34 = seeds 3401 to 3460 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 29 (2026-11-03; HELD34 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorRangeHoldConfirmHoldHeld34.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (5 of 6 cells).** The designated `m23e30kg` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD; WD30L: islands HELD, gaps HELD, current HELD. **4 of 8 predictions held; AD2, AD4, AD5, AD6 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 176 (59/60/57); `long+fix+forget` 172 (60/59/53); `inc@R3D30` 137 (49/43/45); `m23e30k@R3D30` 139 (51/33/55); `m23re30k@R3D30` 134 (53/30/51); `m23re30kg@R3D30` 133 (53/34/46); `m23e30kg@R3D30` 139 (53/34/52); `inc@WD30L` 145 (42/57/46); `m23e30k@WD30L` 164 (57/56/51); `m23re30k@WD30L` 155 (57/50/48); `m23re30kg@WD30L` 156 (55/52/49); `m23e30kg@WD30L` 165 (58/57/50); `m23re30k@P` 146 (56/39/51); `m23re30kg@P` 145 (55/43/47); `m23e30kg@clean` 173 (58/60/55).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AD1 HELD**: the gate costs under the joint stress: under R3D30, m23e30k reaches at least 5 more than m23re30k. m23e30k@R3D30 139, m23re30k@R3D30 134: +5 against the 5 asked.
* **AD2 MISSED**: the ghost helps the gated hold: under R3D30, m23re30kg reaches at least 5 more than m23re30k. m23re30kg@R3D30 133, m23re30k@R3D30 134: -1 against the 5 asked.
* **AD3 HELD**: and little when the hold is everywhere: under R3D30, m23e30kg is within 8 of m23e30k. m23e30kg@R3D30 139, m23e30k@R3D30 139: 0 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AD4 MISSED**: the ghost answers the leak: under WD30L, m23e30kg reaches at least 5 more than m23e30k. m23e30kg@WD30L 165, m23e30k@WD30L 164: +1 against the 5 asked.
* **AD5 MISSED**: and the ghost has a price under the persistent mix: under P, m23re30kg reaches at least 5 fewer than m23re30k. m23re30k@P 146, m23re30kg@P 145: +1 against the 5 asked.
* **AD6 MISSED**: m23e30kg holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. m23e30kg@R3D30: 53/47.2 34/48.0 52/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AD7 HELD**: and under WD30L. m23e30kg@WD30L: 58/47.2 57/48.0 50/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AD8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults m23e30kg is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. m23e30kg@clean 173, long+fix+forget 172: 1 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated ghost on the ungated confirmed hold holds five of six bar cells, and the ghost does nothing measurable: PARTLY SUPPORTED.** `m23e30kg` (a 2-of-3 mark, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range, the clear confirmed at 0.8, the ghost) holds all three kinds under WD30L (58, 57, 50 against 47.2, 48.0 and 45.6 needed) and islands and current under R3D30 (53 and 52); the gaps kind under R3D30 is 34 against 48.0. Four of eight predictions held (AD1, AD3, AD7, AD8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `m23e30k` (the same without the ghost: follow-up 24's) **139**, `m23e30kg` **139**, `inc` 137, `m23re30k` (gated) 134, `m23re30kg` (gated, with the ghost) 133 (exact 176, clean `long+fix+forget` 172); under WD30L: `m23e30kg` **165**, `m23e30k` 164, `m23re30kg` 156, `m23re30k` 155, `inc` 145; under P: `m23re30k` 146, `m23re30kg` 145.
* **The ghost is active and idle (AD2, AD4 and AD5 missed): five comparisons, each within 1 flight.** With the ghost against without it: gated under R3D30 133 against 134 (−1; AD2 asked +5), ungated under R3D30 139 against 139 (0; AD3 held), ungated under WD30L 165 against 164 (+1; AD4 asked +5), gated under WD30L 156 against 155 (+1), gated under P 145 against 146 (−1; AD5 asked a price of 5). It is not idle because it never fires: the mean number of ghost re-marks a flight is 19 (gated) and 24 (ungated) under R3D30, 23 and 31 under WD30L, 30 under P and 67 on a sea with no faults; no re-mark was refused for want of a neighbour (the arms carry no neighbour test). POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `m23e30kg` against `m23e30k` +8 / +8 (p 1.0) under R3D30 and +8 / +7 (p 1.0) under WD30L. Follow-up 23 found the ghost worth about 3 flights and follow-up 18's J3 registered +9 for it on the gated hold (its measured value is in that note's result); here it earns nothing.
* **The gate costs 5 to 9 (AD1 held, at its edge).** Under R3D30 `m23e30k` is exactly **5 above** `m23re30k` (139, 134; 5 asked); under WD30L 9 above (164, 155) and the 24 m gaps reached are 17 against 13 (of 20; exact 20). The gated arms collide more under WD30L (23 against 11 and 13 ungated). POST HOC the designated against the gated arms: `m23re30k` +18 / +13 (p 0.47) and `m23re30kg` +17 / +11 (p 0.34) under R3D30, +19 / +9 (p 0.087) and +15 / +6 (p 0.078) under WD30L. That is follow-up 24's finding (taking the gate off pays about ten) at a size inside this set's noise.
* **The hold is the cure and the gaps kind decides the bar.** Under WD30L the designated is 20 above the map that forgets (165 against 145; POST HOC +30 / +10, p 0.0022); under R3D30 it is 2 above (139, 137; +24 / +22, p 0.88). Gaps reached under R3D30 (of 60): `inc` 43, `m23e30kg` 34, `m23e30k` 33, `m23re30kg` 34, `m23re30k` 30 against 48.0 needed; the 24 m gaps (of 20): `inc` 9, `m23e30kg` 5, `m23e30k` 4, `m23re30kg` 2, `m23re30k` 0.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing (AD8 held).** `m23e30kg@clean` 173 against the clean map's 172 (POST HOC +5 / +4, p 1.0).

**What it does not say.** That a ghost cannot help: it is idle here, in a set where one arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios (follow-up 22), against a hold that already cures the dropouts, and the neighbour test (which would refuse a ghost's re-mark) is not in these arms. That the ghost's life of 60 readings, the cap of 30 and 0.8 are the right numbers, or that the leak (0.15, made up) is a measured flicker. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 30: THE GHOST FOR THE STRICT FAR MARK (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-confirm-hold-range-hold", asked while the DEV runs of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ANY SET OF 8 TO 30 IS FLOWN, so nothing below was shaped by a result)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 26's name (`detector-confirm-hold-range`: which range-aware mark when the evidence hold is in place) with `-hold` on the end, the move follow-ups 18 and 29 made on 17 and 24. Their reading is kept: **a second hold, on what has been confirmed**, which for a detector whose hold already runs at every range is **the ghost** (a cell cleared after at least 4 occupied readings is re-marked at one reading for 60 readings of that cell; no neighbour test: that is follow-up 23's). The reason it belongs with follow-up 26's family: a **strict far rule is slow to re-mark**. An evidence hold clears a cell watched for ten readings after ten free ones, and a dropout of 30 pings does that to every cell that had not been watched for thirty; the cell then has to be confirmed again, and 3 of 4 is the slowest of the family to do it. The ghost is the hold on the confirmation. If it meant the hold range-gated (follow-ups 17, 19, 24) or something else, say so before the build.

**The rule** (follow-up 26's detector with a switch). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked when at least `M` of its last `N` readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is cleared after `min(30, occ)` free readings in a row at every range; **with the ghost**, a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 keeps its window and evidence for 60 readings and a reading of it as occupied in that time marks it at once with `occ` put back to 4.

**Conditions** (everything else as follow-ups 3 to 29): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings), **`WD30`** (follow-up 16's: the false rate uniform at 0.01 with the same dropouts: the law NOT holding), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`: where the ghost is tested, its false returns repeating by design) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`m11e30`** (the hold alone), **`rm23e30`** (2-of-3 far, one reading near: follow-up 18's), **`rm23e30g`** (the same with the ghost: follow-up 18's designated), **`rm34e30`** (3-of-4 far: follow-up 26's designated), **`rm34e30g`** (**the designated: 3-of-4 far, with the ghost**) and **`m34e30`** (3-of-4 everywhere: follow-up 20's) (6 arms); under `WD30` `m11e30`, `rm34e30`, `rm34e30g` and `m34e30` (4); under `P` `rm34e30` and `rm34e30g` (2); and `rm34e30g` on a sea with no faults. Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD35 = seeds 3501 to 3560 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm34e30g` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30`**: three cells. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`WD30`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The ghost re-marks a persistent phantom at its first reading (follow-up 18's tension, follow-up 29's AD5), and a strict far rule is the stage most likely to let a persistent phantom through the confirmation only rarely, so the price may be small here. And the ghost does nothing for the first marking, which is where a strict far rule is slowest; a dropout that begins before the confirmation hides the obstacle from the hold altogether.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AE).**
* **AE1** the ghost helps a strict far rule: under `R3D30`, `rm34e30g` reaches at least 5 more than `rm34e30`. (Re-marking after a dropout at one reading and not at 3 of 4.)
* **AE2** and helps it more than a light one: `rm34e30g` + `rm23e30` reaches at least 3 more than `rm34e30` + `rm23e30g` under `R3D30` (the ghost's gain to the strict rule exceeds its gain to the light rule by at least 3).
* **AE3** strict beats light with the ghost where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `rm34e30g` reaches more than `rm23e30g`.
* **AE4** `rm34e30g` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AE5** the ghost has a price under the persistent mix: under `P`, `rm34e30g` reaches at least 5 fewer than `rm34e30`.
* **AE6** the bet on the law has a price: under `WD30`, `rm34e30g` reaches at least 10 fewer than `m34e30`. (Follow-up 26's AA6.)
* **AE7** the pair still cures the false returns: under `R3D30`, `rm34e30g` reaches at least 15 more than `m11e30`. (Follow-up 26's AA2.)
* **AE8** on a sea with no faults `rm34e30g` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm34e30` against `m11e30` under `WD30`, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 3-of-4, 80 m, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that a ghost with the neighbour test of follow-up 23 would not change the price under `P`; that the range law is true of a real sonar. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs one detector the registry does not have (`rm34e30g`; `rm34e30` is follow-up 26's row), the set HELD35 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD35 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 30 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD35 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)
