### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 34 (2026-11-03; HELD39 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorHoldRangeConfirmHoldHeld39.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (5 of 6 cells).** The designated `m23rn30kg` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD; WD30L: islands HELD, gaps HELD, current HELD. **6 of 8 predictions held; AI2, AI6 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 169 (58/59/52); `long+fix+forget` 169 (57/58/54); `inc@R3D30` 139 (45/49/45); `m23e30kg@R3D30` 134 (51/39/44); `m23rn30k@R3D30` 128 (47/38/43); `m23rn30kg@R3D30` 134 (50/39/45); `m23rp30kg@R3D30` 135 (49/40/46); `inc@WD30L` 126 (33/56/37); `m23e30kg@WD30L` 153 (49/57/47); `m23rn30k@WD30L` 149 (47/56/46); `m23rn30kg@WD30L` 153 (49/57/47); `inc@P` 144 (51/46/47); `m23rn30k@P` 147 (52/46/49); `m23rn30kg@P` 146 (51/48/47); `m23rn30kg@clean` 171 (59/60/52).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AI1 HELD**: the ghost helps the ramp hold: under R3D30, m23rn30kg reaches at least 5 more than m23rn30k. m23rn30kg@R3D30 134, m23rn30k@R3D30 128: +6 against the 5 asked.
* **AI2 MISSED**: and answers the leak: under WD30L, m23rn30kg reaches at least 5 more than m23rn30k. m23rn30kg@WD30L 153, m23rn30k@WD30L 149: +4 against the 5 asked.
* **AI3 HELD**: the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under P, m23rn30kg is within 8 of m23rn30k. m23rn30kg@P 146, m23rn30k@P 147: 1 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AI4 HELD**: the ramp needs no hold at every range once there is a ghost: under R3D30, m23rn30kg is within 8 of m23e30kg. m23rn30kg@R3D30 134, m23e30kg@R3D30 134: 0 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AI5 HELD**: which range the ramp goes by still does not matter: under R3D30, m23rp30kg is within 10 of m23rn30kg. m23rp30kg@R3D30 135, m23rn30kg@R3D30 134: 1 apart, within 10 asked.
* **AI6 MISSED**: m23rn30kg holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. m23rn30kg@R3D30: 50/46.4 39/47.2 45/41.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AI7 HELD**: and under WD30L. m23rn30kg@WD30L: 49/46.4 57/47.2 47/41.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AI8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults m23rn30kg is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. m23rn30kg@clean 171, long+fix+forget 169: 2 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated ghost on the ramp hold holds five of six bar cells, and it earns 4 to 6 flights on the ramp that is not separable from nothing: PARTLY SUPPORTED.** `m23rn30kg` (a 2-of-3 mark, the evidence hold of cap 30 gated by a ramp on the nearest range, the ghost) holds all three kinds under WD30L (49, 57, 47 against 46.4, 47.2 and 41.6 needed) and islands and current under R3D30 (50 and 45); the gaps kind under R3D30 is 39 against 47.2. Six of eight predictions held (AI1, AI3, AI4, AI5, AI7, AI8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `inc` (the map that forgets) **139**, `m23rp30kg` (the ramp on the range at the ping) 135, `m23e30kg` (the hold at every range, follow-up 29's) 134, `m23rn30kg` **134**, `m23rn30k` (no ghost: follow-up 27's) 128 (exact 169, clean `long+fix+forget` 169); under WD30L: `m23e30kg` 153, `m23rn30kg` **153**, `m23rn30k` 149, `inc` 126; under P: `m23rn30k` 147, `m23rn30kg` 146, `inc` 144.
* **The ghost earns 4 to 6 on the ramp hold under the dropouts, and nothing under the persistent mix (AI1 held, AI2 missed, AI3 held).** `m23rn30kg` against `m23rn30k`: +6 under R3D30 (5 asked), +4 under WD30L (5 asked), −1 under P. POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) +15 / +9 (p 0.31), +11 / +7 (p 0.48) and +16 / +17 (p 1.0). It fires: a mean of 23 re-marks a flight under R3D30, 30 under WD30L, 32 under P and 77 on a sea with no faults. This is larger than the 0 to 2 follow-ups 29 and 30 found for the ghost behind an ungated or a strict hold, and the size follow-up 18's J3 and J4 registered (+9 and +5); it is inside the 7 to 9 flights one arm moves between two sets of scenarios (follow-up 22).
* **With the ghost the gate is free, and the range it goes by does not matter (AI4 and AI5 held).** `m23rn30kg` and `m23e30kg` are 134 and 134 under R3D30 and 153 and 153 under WD30L (POST HOC +12 / +12, p 1.0 and +5 / +5, p 1.0); `m23rp30kg` is 135 (+10 / +11, p 1.0). Follow-ups 24 and 29 found a step gate costing 5 to 10 without a ghost; the ramp's own cost without one is not measured against an ungated hold in this set (no arm).
* **Under range-law false returns and 30-ping dropouts the map that forgets beats every detector in the set, and under the leak it is 27 below.** R3D30: `inc` 139 against 134 for the designated (POST HOC +23 / +28, p 0.58); `inc` holds the gaps kind (49 against 47.2) and current (45) and misses islands (45 against 46.4); the designated holds islands and current and misses gaps (39). The 24 m gaps reached (of 20; exact 19): `inc` 12, `m23rp30kg` 7, `m23e30kg` 6, `m23rn30k` 6, `m23rn30kg` 4. WD30L: the designated is 153 against `inc`'s 126 (+39 / +12, p 0.0002), with 20 collisions against 39. Collisions under R3D30: `m23rp30kg` 38, `inc` 39, `m23e30kg` 39, `m23rn30kg` 40, `m23rn30k` 47.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing (AI8 held).** `m23rn30kg@clean` 171 against the clean map's 169 (POST HOC +7 / +5, p 0.77).

**What it does not say.** That the ghost makes the ramp as good as the hold at every range by itself: the two are level here with the ghost, and the evidence for the ghost is +6 and +4 in a set that cannot tell them from 0 or 9. That the map that forgets is the better detector under range-law false returns with 30-ping dropouts (it wins the gaps kind and loses islands; it loses to every hold under the leak). That 30, the ramp's 80 to 200 m, the ghost's life of 60 and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 35: THE GHOST BEHIND THE MARK RULE, WITH THE CLEAR CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-confirm-hold-confirm-hold", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 22's name (`detector-confirm-hold-confirm`: which mark rule when the hold is there and its clear is confirmed) with `-hold` on the end, the move follow-ups 18, 29, 30, 32 and 34 made: **a second hold, on what has been confirmed**, which is **the ghost** (a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings keeps its window and evidence for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time it is marked again at that reading, with its evidence put back to 4; no neighbour test: that is follow-up 23's). Follow-up 22's detector is kept as it was (the mark rule varied; the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range with the confirmed clear of follow-up 21, `K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(30, occ)` readings free), and its question gets a second half: **the ghost re-marks at one reading whatever the mark rule says, so with a ghost the mark rule matters only at a cell's first marking**, and the rules that are slowest to mark (a run of three, 3 of 4) are the ones the ghost should help most. The name is also the four stages of follow-up 23 (`hold-confirm-hold-confirm`) in another order; follow-up 23 takes each stage out of **one** detector with the **neighbour** test on the ghost, and this round varies the mark rule with the plain ghost. If it meant follow-up 23's detector or something else, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range, its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals, the top four detectors were level) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirmations are one rule, spread 6 of 180; strictness cost where the misses are many). **The confirmed clear (follow-up 21), the mark rule with it (follow-up 22) and the confirmed gated hold (follow-up 24) are unread or unflown**, so what is predicted here about the confirmed clear is made without their results. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 22's detector with a switch). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` (occupied readings since last cleared) and a ghost counter. A free cell is **marked** when at least `M` of its last `N` readings were occupied (`occ` then starts at that count); a marked cell is **cleared** when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, at every range. **With the ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time with `occ = 4`, whatever the mark rule; after 60 it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (exactly follow-up 22's, everything else as follow-ups 3 to 34): **`WD30`** (follow-up 4's white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, with whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (the same with the dropouts leaking, each reading of a dropped-out obstacle still a normal return with chance 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where the ghost is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the clear of `f80`): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `WD30` the six detectors **`m11f80`** (a mark of one: the control that repairs nothing, and on which a ghost does nothing), **`m23f80`** (follow-up 21's designated) and **`m33f80`** (follow-up 22's designated), without the ghost, and **`m23f80g`**, **`m33f80g`** (**the designated: a run of three with the ghost**) and **`m34f80g`** with it; under `WD30L` `m23f80`, `m33f80`, `m23f80g` and `m33f80g` (4); under `P` `m33f80` and `m33f80g` (2); and `m33f80g` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD40 = seeds 4001 to 4060 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m33f80g` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `WD30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**Why `m33f80g` is the designated, and the tension, said before the data.** It is follow-up 22's designated (the rule that separates a wall from the smear best, and the slowest to mark a real cell) with the stage that repairs slowness; if the ghost works as designed it removes the run's price at every re-marking and keeps its separating power at the first marking. Against it: the ghost re-marks a smear edge cell that once earned 4 readings at one reading, which is the thing the run of three and the confirmed clear were together keeping out; and the ghost does nothing for the first marking, where a dropout that begins before the third reading hides an obstacle from the hold altogether.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AJ).**
* **AJ1** the ghost helps the strict run where cells are cleared: under `WD30L`, `m33f80g` reaches at least 5 more than `m33f80`. (A leaking dropout clears cells and a run of three takes three clean readings to bring them back; follow-up 22's X2 and follow-up 30's AE1.)
* **AJ2** and helps it more than the light rule: under `WD30L`, (`m33f80g` minus `m33f80`) exceeds (`m23f80g` minus `m23f80`) by at least 3. (Follow-up 30's AE2.)
* **AJ3** the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `m33f80g` is within 8 of `m33f80` (either side). (Follow-up 18: `rm23e30g@P` 159 against `inc` 160.)
* **AJ4** with a ghost the mark rule is one rule: under `WD30`, the three ghost arms (`m23f80g`, `m33f80g`, `m34f80g`) have pooled counts within 10 of each other (largest minus smallest). (The ghost re-marks whatever the rule says; follow-up 20's Y1 for the first marking.)
* **AJ5** every confirmation still repairs the white false returns: under `WD30`, each of the three ghost arms reaches at least 20 more than `m11f80`. (Follow-up 22's X4.)
* **AJ6** `m33f80g` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `WD30`.
* **AJ7** and under `WD30L`.
* **AJ8** on a sea with no faults `m33f80g` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
The ghost's effect under `WD30` (`m33f80g` against `m33f80`, `m23f80g` against `m23f80`), the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 0.8, 30, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that a run of three is the best of the family or that the family is the only thing a ghost could be asked of (a 4-of-5, a likelihood sum, a neighbour test are untried or follow-up 23's); that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, three registry rows (`m23f80g`, `m33f80g` and `m34f80g`; `m23f80g` is the detector follow-up 23 calls `gU`; the other arms are follow-ups 21 and 22's rows), the set HELD40 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD40 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 35 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD40 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `m11f80@WD30` 12/18; `m23f80@WD30` 13/18; `m33f80@WD30` 14/18; `m23f80g@WD30` 14/18; `m33f80g@WD30` 15/18; `m34f80g@WD30` 14/18; `m23f80@WD30L` 17/18; `m33f80@WD30L` 16/18; `m23f80g@WD30L` 18/18; `m33f80g@WD30L` 16/18; `m33f80@P` 18/18; `m33f80g@P` 17/18; `m33f80g@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `m33f80g`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD40 = seeds 4001 to 4060 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 35 (2026-11-03; HELD40 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorConfirmHoldConfirmHoldHeld40.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (5 of 6 cells).** The designated `m33f80g` against 80% of exact's count: WD30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD; WD30L: islands HELD, gaps HELD, current HELD. **5 of 8 predictions held; AJ1, AJ2, AJ6 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 174 (60/57/57); `long+fix+forget` 170 (56/57/57); `m11f80@WD30` 99 (34/32/33); `m23f80@WD30` 135 (51/39/45); `m33f80@WD30` 138 (49/40/49); `m23f80g@WD30` 139 (54/38/47); `m33f80g@WD30` 139 (51/40/48); `m34f80g@WD30` 136 (54/34/48); `m23f80@WD30L` 154 (53/52/49); `m33f80@WD30L` 140 (49/41/50); `m23f80g@WD30L` 161 (54/55/52); `m33f80g@WD30L` 144 (49/47/48); `m33f80@P` 148 (57/43/48); `m33f80g@P` 144 (51/46/47); `m33f80g@clean` 174 (59/59/56).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AJ1 MISSED**: the ghost helps the strict run where cells are cleared: under WD30L, m33f80g reaches at least 5 more than m33f80. m33f80g@WD30L 144, m33f80@WD30L 140: +4 against the 5 asked.
* **AJ2 MISSED**: and helps it more than the light rule: under WD30L, (m33f80g minus m33f80) exceeds (m23f80g minus m23f80) by at least 3. m33f80g@WD30L+m23f80@WD30L 298, m33f80@WD30L+m23f80g@WD30L 301: -3 against the 3 asked.
* **AJ3 HELD**: the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under P, m33f80g is within 8 of m33f80. m33f80g@P 144, m33f80@P 148: 4 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AJ4 HELD**: with a ghost the mark rule is one rule: under WD30, the three ghost arms have pooled counts within 10 of each other. m23f80g 139, m33f80g 139, m34f80g 136: 3 apart (within 10 asked).
* **AJ5 HELD**: every confirmation still repairs the white false returns: under WD30, each of the three ghost arms reaches at least 20 more than m11f80. m23f80g 139, m33f80g 139, m34f80g 136 against m11f80@WD30 99 (20 asked).
* **AJ6 MISSED**: m33f80g holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under WD30. m33f80g@WD30: 51/48.0 40/45.6 48/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AJ7 HELD**: and under WD30L. m33f80g@WD30L: 49/48.0 47/45.6 48/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AJ8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults m33f80g is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. m33f80g@clean 174, long+fix+forget 170: 4 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated ghost on the run of three holds five of six bar cells, and the ghost earns 1 to 4 flights: PARTLY SUPPORTED.** `m33f80g` (a run of three to mark, the evidence hold of cap 30, the clear confirmed at 0.8, the ghost) holds all three kinds under WD30L (49, 47, 48 against 48.0, 45.6 and 45.6 needed) and islands and current under WD30 (51 and 48); the gaps kind under WD30 is 40 against 45.6. Five of eight predictions held (AJ3, AJ4, AJ5, AJ7, AJ8). Counts of 180 under WD30: `m23f80g` **139**, `m33f80g` **139**, `m33f80` (no ghost: follow-up 22's designated) 138, `m34f80g` 136, `m23f80` (follow-up 21's designated) 135, `m11f80` (a mark of one) 99 (exact 174, clean `long+fix+forget` 170); under WD30L: `m23f80g` **161**, `m23f80` 154, `m33f80g` **144**, `m33f80` 140; under P: `m33f80` 148, `m33f80g` 144.
* **The ghost helps the run of three by 1 to 4 and the light rule by more under the leak (AJ1 and AJ2 missed).** AJ1 asked `m33f80g` 5 above `m33f80` under WD30L; it is **4** above (144, 140); under WD30 1 above (139, 138). AJ2 asked the strict rule's gain to exceed the light rule's by 3; it is **3 below** it: the ghost adds 7 to the light rule under WD30L (`m23f80g` 161, `m23f80` 154) and 4 to the run of three. POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `m33f80g` against `m33f80` +16 / +15 (p 1.0) under WD30, +16 / +12 (p 0.57) under WD30L and +8 / +12 (p 0.50) under P; against `m23f80g` under WD30L +11 / +28 (p 0.0095). The registered reason for AJ1 (a run of three takes three clean readings to bring a cell back) is not what the data show: the ghost does not repair the run of three's slowness, and the run of three under the leak is **17 below** the light rule with the same ghost (144, 161), with the 24 m gaps reached 8 against 15 of 20 (exact 17) and the gaps kind 47 against 55.
* **A run of three marks few cells, ghost or not.** Mean marks a flight under WD30: `m33f80` 61, `m33f80g` 70, `m34f80g` 100, `m23f80` 202, `m23f80g` 206; median plans 44 to 59 for the strict rules and 87 to 89 for the 2-of-3. That is follow-up 22's observation (a cell has to be seen three times in a row to enter the plan at all) again; the ghost re-marks 13 to 22 cells a flight under WD30, 18 and 30 under WD30L, 22 under P and 44 on a sea with no faults, with no count moving by more than 7.
* **With a ghost the mark rule is one rule, and every confirmation still repairs the white false returns (AJ4 and AJ5 held).** The three ghost arms are 136 to 139 under WD30 (3 apart; 10 asked) and 37 to 40 above `m11f80`'s 99 (20 asked); POST HOC `m33f80g` against `m23f80g` +20 / +20 (p 1.0), against `m34f80g` +22 / +19 (p 0.76), against `m11f80` +55 / +15 (p below 0.0001). The gaps kind decides the WD30 bar (34 to 40 against 45.6 for the confirming arms).
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing and the persistent mix a small price (AJ3 and AJ8 held).** `m33f80g@clean` 174 against the clean map's 170 (POST HOC +8 / +4, p 0.39); `m33f80g@P` is 4 below `m33f80`. Collisions under WD30L: `m23f80g` 14, `m23f80` 18, `m33f80g` 35, `m33f80` 39.

**What it does not say.** That a ghost cannot help a strict run: it is worth 4 flights under the leak, and this set cannot tell 4 from 0 or from 8 (one arm moves 7 to 9 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios, follow-up 22). That a run of three is no good: it holds the bar's islands and current in both seas. That the ghost's life of 60, 0.8 and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 36: WHICH RANGE THE PLAIN LOCK GOES BY (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-confirm-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 19's name (`detector-range-confirm-hold`: the range-aware 2-of-3 confirmation with the plain range-gated lock, `rm23lk`) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-up 15 made on follow-up 13 and follow-up 27 on follow-up 24: **which range the lock goes by, and whether its gate is a step or a ramp**. Follow-up 19's detector is kept (2-of-3 beyond 80 m, a first reading within; a **plain** lock, `thr = g(r)` free readings in a row with no evidence: it is the lock of follow-up 8, not the evidence hold) and the lock's gate is varied as follow-up 15 varied the evidence hold's: `g` a **step** (30 within 80 m, 1 beyond: `rm23lk`) or a **ramp** `max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))`, and `r` the range at the **ping** or the **nearest** the cell has been read occupied from. If it meant the range-aware mark's range (follow-up 14's, already here) or the evidence hold's range (follow-ups 15 and 17), or the confirmed clear's fraction by range (follow-up 31), say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and what it asks and predicts is shaped by them. **Follow-up 10's result is the one that matters**: `rd` (a run of three far, a first reading near, the plain lock) reached 18 of 180 under range-law false returns, 36 under the uniform false rate, 99 under the persistent mix, 130 under dropouts and **152 on a sea with no faults against the clean map's 170**, and follow-up 17's set measured `rd` at 14 of 180 under range-law false returns with dropouts. The mechanism its note gives (a near false return is marked at the first reading and held thirty readings) does not depend on the far mark, so **this round is registered expecting the plain lock to be poor whatever its gate**, and asks only whether the ramp (24 readings at 40 m, 18 at 80 m, against the step's 30) or the nearest range moves it. Follow-up 19's set (the same lock with the step, `rm23lk`) is unread or unflown. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The question.** A plain lock holds every marked cell for the whole of its gate whatever the cell's history, so a near false return is held as long as a wall. The evidence hold (follow-up 12) was made to fix that and does. The ramp shortens the lock near the vessel and lengthens it far from it; the nearest range ever read keeps a wall held after the vessel has passed it. Neither touches the problem, which is the lock's length for a cell read once. The round asks whether the shape or the range changes the size of a collapse, not whether a lock can win.

**The rule** (follow-up 19's detector with the gate chosen). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading. A marked cell is cleared after `g(r)` consecutive free readings, `g` the **step** (30 if `r` is at most 80 m, else 1) or the **ramp** (`max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))`), `r` the range **at the ping** (`ping`) or the **least range the cell has been read occupied from since it was last cleared** (`min`).

**Conditions** (exactly follow-up 19's, everything else as follows-ups 3 to 35): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law: the bar's), **`W`** (the uniform false rate of 0.01, the law NOT holding), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`rm23re30`** (follow-up 17's designated: the same mark with the evidence hold, here the control that does not collapse), **`rm23lk`** (follow-up 19's designated: step, `ping`), **`rm23lkmn`** (step, `min`), **`rm23lkrp`** (ramp, `ping`) and **`rm23lkrn`** (**the designated: ramp, `min`**, as follow-up 15's); `inc`, `rm23lk` and `rm23lkrn` under `W` (3) and under `P` (3); `rm23lkrn` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD41 = seeds 4101 to 4160 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm23lkrn` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30`**: three cells, follow-up 19's bar. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`W`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The ramp is the one gate that could matter: at 40 m it holds 24 readings where the step holds 30, at 60 m 21, at 80 m 18, so the near phantoms are held a fifth to two-fifths less; and it holds a far cell (12 at 120 m) where the step lets it go at once, which is the thing follow-up 15 found carries the narrow gaps. Against it: a lock of 18 to 24 readings is still a lock, and follow-up 10's collapse has no threshold in it.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AK).**
* **AK1** the near phantom problem remains: under `R3D30`, `rm23lkrn` reaches at least 30 fewer than `rm23re30`. (Follow-up 17: `rm23re30` 118, `rd` 14.)
* **AK2** which range and shape the lock goes by does not rescue it: under `R3D30`, the four lock arms (`rm23lk`, `rm23lkmn`, `rm23lkrp`, `rm23lkrn`) have pooled counts within 20 of each other (largest minus smallest). (All four hold a near false return for 18 to 30 readings.)
* **AK3** a lock is worse than the map that forgets: under `R3D30`, `rm23lkrn` reaches at least 20 fewer than `inc`.
* **AK4** `rm23lkrn` does not hold the bar: under `R3D30`, it is below 80% of exact in each of the three kinds.
* **AK5** where the law fails it is worse: under `W`, `rm23lkrn` reaches at least 20 fewer than `inc`. (Follow-up 10's `rd@W` 36 against `inc` 123.)
* **AK6** under the persistent mix `P`, `rm23lkrn` reaches fewer than `inc`. (Follow-up 10's `rd@P` 99 against 148.)
* **AK7** the ramp shortens the damage a little: under `R3D30`, `rm23lkrn` reaches more than `rm23lk`. (18 to 24 readings within 80 m against thirty, over the first 80 m.)
* **AK8** a lock costs even on a sea with no faults: `rm23lkrn@clean` reaches at least 8 fewer than the clean `long+fix+forget`. (Follow-up 10's `rd@clean` 152 against 170: the fix's flicker is held.)
`rm23lk` against `rm23lkrn` per kind and per gap, and the pooled occupied cells at the last plan (the phantom count) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 200 m, 30 and 2-of-3 are the right numbers; that the ramp and the step are the only gates (a gate that falls faster near the vessel is untried); that a plain lock cannot be made to work (a lock that is short for a cell read once is the evidence hold, which is follow-ups 12 to 17); that the range law is true of a real sonar. The round is expected to confirm a collapse, and says so before the data. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, three registry rows (`rm23lkmn`, `rm23lkrp` and `rm23lkrn`; `rm23lk`, `rm23re30` and `inc` exist), the set HELD41 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD41 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 36 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD41 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers: the detector module with the five optional fields of this stretch (`clear.fNear`, `ghost.carry`, `ghost.zone`, `ghost.at`, `lock`) and 77 more registry rows (59 mutants of the new lines, 55 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 3 equivalent and said; 154 mechanical mutants of the 77 rows, all red); the table of sets (HELD30 to HELD63, seeds 4101 to 4160 for this one); the study engine with its new operation `noBar` and the 34 more specs (1,377 mechanical mutants of the specs, all red by the row that holds them to the registrations; 10 of the helpers they call and 2 of the list of operations; 12 of the engine's `noBar` line, 9 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 2 equivalent and said; each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against a literal copy of the registrations in the gate, and the table that generated both against the registered prose by a script (272 predictions' operations, thresholds, directions and arms mechanically, 14 by hand: no disagreement); the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `inc@R3D30` 14/18; `rm23re30@R3D30` 13/18; `rm23lk@R3D30` 2/18; `rm23lkmn@R3D30` 1/18; `rm23lkrp@R3D30` 2/18; `rm23lkrn@R3D30` 3/18; `inc@W` 14/18; `rm23lk@W` 4/18; `rm23lkrn@W` 4/18; `inc@P` 16/18; `rm23lk@P` 10/18; `rm23lkrn@P` 15/18; `rm23lkrn@clean` 17/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**What DEV showed that the registration did not say, and is said here so that it is not found afterwards and called a surprise:** `rm23lk@R3D30` reached 2 of 18, `rm23lkmn@R3D30` reached 1 of 18, `rm23lkrp@R3D30` reached 2 of 18, `rm23lkrn@R3D30` reached 3 of 18, `rm23lk@W` reached 4 of 18, `rm23lkrn@W` reached 4 of 18 (the other arms 10 to 18). It is **not read as a result** (18 scenarios) and it moves **no prediction and no verdict rule**; it means the registered predictions and bar cells that need such an arm to reach 80% of exact are likely to be missed, and the study will say so.

**One operation the registration used in words.** AK4 (*`rm23lkrn` does not hold the bar: under `R3D30`, it is below 80% of exact in each of the three kinds*) is the engine's new `noBar` operation (true when the arm is flown in every kind and misses the 80% line in every kind), which is neither `bar` nor `notBar` (`notBar` is true when the arm misses in at least one kind). It is gated (rows with the edges 47/48 of 60, a kind with no flights and a kind with no `exact`) and sabotaged (12 mutants).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `rm23lkrn`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD41 = seeds 4101 to 4160 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

## FOLLOW-UP 37: THE SECOND HOLD THAT KNOWS HOW FAR (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 32's name (`detector-hold-range-hold`: follow-up 15's ramp hold with the carried evidence, the second hold) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-up 17 made on follow-up 16, follow-up 31 on follow-up 24 and follow-up 33 on follow-up 13: **the second hold knowing how far**. Follow-up 32's carry restores a cleared cell's evidence (to 4) when the cell is next read occupied within 60 readings, at every range. Here the carry is **judged by the range at the ping that re-marks the cell**: **only beyond 80 m** (`far`), **only within it** (`near`), or at every range (`all`: follow-up 32's). The reason a range can matter to a carry is the ramp: the hold `min(ev, g(r))` is 12 readings at 120 m, 6 at 160 m and 4 at 175 m, so a far cell that a dropout clears has little to lose, and restoring its evidence to 4 gains most there; a near cell was held up to 30 readings and cleared after that many free ones, which looks like a real clear, and a near persistent phantom is the one the planner cannot afford. If it meant the carry's *amount* depending on range, or the range of follow-up 15's gate varied again, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 15's (the ramp carries; `rn30` held the bar), follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 17's (a first-reading mark within 80 m is where the harm is done). Follow-up 32, which this one rests on, is **registered and unbuilt**: its carry is a field the module does not have yet and its prediction (AG1, the carry changes little under the dropout) is made without any result. The sets of follow-ups 19 and 21 to 24 are unread or unflown. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The question, and what to expect.** If follow-up 32's carry changes little under the dropout, as that round registered, then restricting it to a zone changes little too, and this round measures a small effect more finely; it is registered with that said. What it can show that follow-up 32 cannot is whether the effect, if there is one, lives in the far zone (where the hold is short) or the near zone (where the phantoms are).

**The rule** (follow-up 32's detector with a zone). Mark: one occupied reading. Clear: after `thr = min(ev, g(r))` consecutive free readings, `ev` the occupied readings since last cleared, `g(r) = max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))`, `r` the nearest range the cell has been read occupied from (`min`). **Carry with a zone**: a cell cleared with `ev` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time **at a ping from beyond 80 m (`far`), within 80 m inclusive (`near`) or from any range (`all`)** it is marked with `ev = 4`; a ghost read occupied from the other zone is marked as an ordinary cell (`ev` 1). After 60 readings it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 36): **`D30`** (whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings with the range law and none of follow-up 4's white faults: follow-up 15's and 32's; the bar's), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 4's four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a carried phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, a mark of one and the ramp hold on the nearest range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `D30` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`rn30`** (follow-up 15's designated, no carry), **`rn30c`** (follow-up 32's designated: the carry at every range), **`rn30cf`** (**the designated: the carry beyond 80 m only**) and **`rn30cn`** (the carry within 80 m only); under `WD30L` `rn30`, `rn30c` and `rn30cf` (3); under `P` `inc`, `rn30`, `rn30c` and `rn30cf` (4); `rn30cf` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD42 = seeds 4201 to 4260 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rn30cf` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `D30`**: three cells, follow-up 15's bar on a fresh set. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`WD30L`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar; `rn30` held this bar in follow-up 15, so a verdict here speaks of the carry only through the predictions.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The far carry restores a cell to a hold of 4, and the ramp's hold at 160 m is 6: the restoration can only matter between about 80 m and 175 m, a band in which a dropout of 30 pings has to start and end within the ghost's life for the carry to act at all; the near carry restores a cell that was held up to 30 readings to 4, shorter than what it had. Both effects are small by construction. The persistent mix returns the same false returns, and a far one carried is a phantom the planner sees from a distance for 4 readings.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AL).**
* **AL1** the far carry does what the carry at every range does: under `D30`, `rn30cf` is within 8 of `rn30c` (either side). (The far band is where the ramp's hold is short.)
* **AL2** and the near carry does nothing: under `D30`, `rn30cn` is within 8 of `rn30` (either side). (A near cell is held for what it earned.)
* **AL3** the far carry is cheaper under the persistent mix: under `P`, `rn30cf` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30c`. (The persistent false returns that matter are near.)
* **AL4** under the leak the zone does not matter: under `WD30L`, `rn30cf` is within 8 of `rn30c` (either side). (Follow-up 32's AG2.)
* **AL5** `rn30cf` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `D30`.
* **AL6** the designated beats the map that forgets under the dropout: under `D30`, `rn30cf` reaches more than `inc`. (Follow-up 15's T4 asked 10 more and missed at 4 more.)
* **AL7** under the persistent mix `P`, `rn30cf` is within 10 of `rn30` (either side). (Follow-up 32's AG3, asked of the far carry.)
* **AL8** on a sea with no faults `rn30cf` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rn30cf` against `rn30c` per kind and per gap (the 24 m gaps especially) and the number of carried re-marks by zone (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the zone should be a step in range and not a smooth weight; that the range at the re-marking ping is the right range for the carry (the range at the clearing ping, or the nearest the cell was read from, are untried); that the dropouts are like a real sonar's (they are made up, and so is the leak). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **two new fields in the module**, follow-up 32's `ghost.carry` and a zone for it (`ghost.zone`: `all` by default, `far` or `near`; validation, the mark path, `describe`, gate rows and mutants), two registry rows (`rn30cf` and `rn30cn`; `rn30`, `rn30c` and `inc` exist after follow-up 32's), the set HELD42 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD42 is.

Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 37 (2026-11-04; HELD42 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorHoldRangeHoldRangeHeld42.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (2 of 3 cells).** The designated `rn30cf` against 80% of exact's count: D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD. **6 of 8 predictions held; AL3, AL5 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 174 (59/58/57); `long+fix+forget` 170 (59/57/54); `inc@D30` 150 (58/37/55); `rn30@D30` 157 (58/44/55); `rn30c@D30` 157 (56/45/56); `rn30cf@D30` 157 (57/45/55); `rn30cn@D30` 158 (57/45/56); `rn30@WD30L` 124 (30/57/37); `rn30c@WD30L` 121 (32/58/31); `rn30cf@WD30L` 119 (30/57/32); `inc@P` 155 (59/49/47); `rn30@P` 159 (51/53/55); `rn30c@P` 158 (50/54/54); `rn30cf@P` 159 (51/54/54); `rn30cf@clean` 173 (56/59/58).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AL1 HELD**: the far carry does what the carry at every range does: under D30, rn30cf is within 8 of rn30c. rn30cf@D30 157, rn30c@D30 157: 0 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AL2 HELD**: and the near carry does nothing: under D30, rn30cn is within 8 of rn30. rn30cn@D30 158, rn30@D30 157: 1 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AL3 MISSED**: the far carry is cheaper under the persistent mix: under P, rn30cf reaches at least 3 more than rn30c. rn30cf@P 159, rn30c@P 158: +1 against the 3 asked.
* **AL4 HELD**: under the leak the zone does not matter: under WD30L, rn30cf is within 8 of rn30c. rn30cf@WD30L 119, rn30c@WD30L 121: 2 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AL5 MISSED**: rn30cf holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under D30. rn30cf@D30: 57/47.2 45/46.4 55/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AL6 HELD**: the designated beats the map that forgets under the dropout: under D30, rn30cf reaches more than inc. rn30cf@D30 157 against inc@D30 150.
* **AL7 HELD**: under the persistent mix P, rn30cf is within 10 of rn30. rn30cf@P 159, rn30@P 159: 0 apart, within 10 asked.
* **AL8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults rn30cf is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. rn30cf@clean 173, long+fix+forget 170: 3 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated far carry holds two of three bar cells (the gaps kind misses by 1.4 flights), and the carry, far, near or everywhere, is the same detector: PARTLY SUPPORTED.** `rn30cf` (a mark of one, the ramp hold of cap 30 on the nearest range, the carry beyond 80 m only) reaches 57, 45 and 55 under D30 against 47.2, 46.4 and 45.6 needed (exact 59, 58, 57): islands and current held, gaps one flight short. Six of eight predictions held (AL1, AL2, AL4, AL6, AL7, AL8). Counts of 180 under D30: `rn30cn` (the carry within 80 m only) **158**, `rn30` (no carry), `rn30c` (the carry at every range) and `rn30cf` **157**, **157**, **157**, `inc` (the map that forgets) 150 (exact 174, clean `long+fix+forget` 170); under WD30L: `rn30` 124, `rn30c` 121, `rn30cf` 119; under P: `rn30` 159, `rn30cf` 159, `rn30c` 158, `inc` 155.
* **Four carries are one number, and the carry is active (AL1, AL2 and AL4 held, AL3 missed).** Under D30 the four hold arms are within 1 of each other; under WD30L the three flown are within 5; under P the three flown are within 1. AL3 asked the far carry to be 3 above the carry everywhere under P; it is 1 above (159, 158). POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `rn30cf` against `rn30` +2 / +2 (p 1.0), against `rn30c` +1 / +1 (p 1.0), against `rn30cn` +1 / +2 (p 1.0) under D30; against `rn30` +4 / +9 (p 0.27) and against `rn30c` +2 / +4 (p 0.69) under WD30L; +6 / +6 and +2 / +1 under P. The carry fires: a mean of 73 carried re-marks a flight for `rn30c` under D30, 53 for `rn30cf` and 17 for `rn30cn` (the far band is where most of them are), 16 and 11 under WD30L, 21 and 15 under P, 31 on a sea with no faults. Follow-up 32 found the carry at every range changing no count by more than 2; this set finds the same for the far and near zones apart.
* **The hold is 7 above the map that forgets here, and it was 1 above in follow-up 32 with the same arm.** `rn30cf` is 7 above `inc` under D30 (157, 150; AL6 held; POST HOC +14 / +7, p 0.19), `rn30c` 7 above, `rn30` 7 above; follow-up 32's HELD37 had `rn30c` 148 and `inc` 147. The same arms moved between the two sets: `rn30` 146 to 157, `rn30c` 148 to 157, `inc` 147 to 150 under D30, and under P `rn30` 147 to 159, `rn30c` 145 to 158 and `inc` 151 to 155, while under WD30L `rn30` 123 to 124 and `rn30c` 125 to 121. A single arm moved up to 12 flights of 180 between two sets of scenarios, more than the 7 to 9 follow-up 22 measured. What the hold buys is in the gaps kind, 37 for `inc` against 44 to 45 for the four holds (the 24 m gaps reached, of 20, exact 18: 5 against 10 to 11).
* **Under the leaking dropouts the hold misses two kinds by a wide margin.** WD30L: islands 30, 32 and 30 of 59 (47.2 needed) and current 37, 31 and 32 of 57 (45.6) for `rn30`, `rn30c` and `rn30cf`; the gaps kind is 57 or 58 for all. A sea with no faults costs nothing (AL8 held): `rn30cf@clean` 173 against the clean map's 170 (POST HOC +8 / +5, p 0.58). Collisions under D30: `inc` 28, `rn30c` and `rn30cf` 21, `rn30` and `rn30cn` 20.

**What it does not say.** That the carry is useless: with 30-ping dropouts a cell is cleared once and the carry acts at the second clear, so the dropout alone cannot show it (as follow-up 32 said), and the persistent mix, which could, shows 1 to 2 flights. That the far band is where the ramp is short and the near band where it is not is not what separates anything here. That 30, 80 m and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 38: THE GHOST THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, ON THE CONFIRMED GATED HOLD (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-confirm-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 29's name (`detector-range-hold-confirm-hold`: the second hold, the ghost, on the confirmed hold, gated and ungated) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-up 37 made on follow-up 32 and follow-up 17 on follow-up 16: **the second hold knowing how far**. Follow-up 29's ghost re-marks a cleared cell (one that had at least 4 occupied readings) at **one** occupied reading, whatever the mark rule, at every range, for 60 readings of that cell. Here the ghost is **judged by the range at the ping that re-marks the cell**: **only beyond 80 m** (`far`), **only within it** (`near`), or at every range (`all`: follow-up 29's). The hold under it is the name's `range-hold`: the **gated** confirmed hold (follow-up 24: the evidence hold of cap 30 within 80 m and one free reading beyond, cleared by 0.8 of the window). The reason a range can matter is the gate itself: **beyond 80 m the gated hold lets a dropout take a cell at once**, so the far zone is where there is something to restore at one reading, and it is also where the range law puts the most false returns (0.06 at 200 m against 0.0054 at 60 m), so the far zone is where a restored cell is least safe. If it meant the confirmed clear's fraction depending on range (follow-up 31's) with the ghost, or the zone judged by the range at the clearing ping, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; removing the gate paid +10; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 17's (a first-reading mark within 80 m is where the harm is done). **The confirmed clear (follow-up 21), the confirmed gated hold (follow-up 24) and the ghost behind the confirmed hold (follow-up 29) are unread or unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about the confirmed clear is made without their results. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 29's detector with a zone on the ghost). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` and a ghost counter. A free cell is **marked** when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range; a marked cell is **cleared** when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(g, occ)` readings were free, `g` = 30 for a cell **within 80 m** of the sonar at the ping and 1 **beyond** it (the gated hold). A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time **at a ping from beyond 80 m (`far`), within 80 m inclusive (`near`) or from any range (`all`)** it is marked at that reading with `occ = 4`, whatever the mark rule; read occupied from the other zone it is judged by the ordinary 2-of-3 rule. After 60 readings it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (exactly follow-up 29's, everything else as follows-ups 3 to 37): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the false rate 0.01 uniform, dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a restored phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23re30k`** (follow-up 24's designated: the gated confirmed hold, no ghost), **`m23re30kg`** (follow-up 29's: the ghost at every range), **`m23re30kgf`** (**the designated: the ghost beyond 80 m only**), **`m23re30kgn`** (the ghost within 80 m only) and **`m23e30kg`** (follow-up 29's designated: the confirmed hold at every range with the ghost); under `WD30L` `m23re30k`, `m23re30kg` and `m23re30kgf` (3); under `P` `m23re30k`, `m23re30kg` and `m23re30kgf` (3); `m23re30kgf` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD43 = seeds 4301 to 4360 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23re30kgf` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The far ghost is the one stage that answers the gate's cost (a dropout that begins far away takes the cell at once; the ghost brings it back at one reading) and the one that meets the range law's worst false returns; the near ghost restores a cell that was held up to 30 readings and is safe under the law and not under the uniform rate. A ghost that re-marks at one reading a far cell once confirmed is as good as the cell's evidence, which for a far false return that passed a 2-of-3 and four readings is small.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AM).**
* **AM1** the far ghost is the one that matters: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgf` is within 8 of `m23re30kg` (either side). (The gate is where there is something to restore.)
* **AM2** and the near ghost does nothing: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgn` is within 8 of `m23re30k` (either side). (A near cell is held for what it earned.)
* **AM3** the far ghost helps the gated hold: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgf` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30k`. (Follow-up 18's J3, +9, asked of the far zone.)
* **AM4** under the leak the zone does not matter: under `WD30L`, `m23re30kgf` is within 8 of `m23re30kg` (either side).
* **AM5** `m23re30kgf` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AM6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AM7** the far ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `m23re30kgf` is within 8 of `m23re30k` (either side). (Follow-up 18: `rm23e30g@P` 159 against `inc` 160.)
* **AM8** on a sea with no faults `m23re30kgf` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23re30kgf` against `m23e30kg` under `R3D30` (does the far ghost make the gate unnecessary: follow-up 18's J1 and J4), the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks by zone (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 2-of-3, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the zone should be a step in range and not a smooth weight; that the range at the re-marking ping is the right range for the ghost; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, a zone for the ghost (`ghost.zone`: `all` by default, `far` or `near`; validation, the re-marking path, `describe`, gate rows and mutants; the same field follow-up 37 needs, built once), two registry rows (`m23re30kgf` and `m23re30kgn`; `m23re30k`, `m23re30kg` and `m23e30kg` are follow-ups 24 and 29's), the set HELD43 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD43 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 38 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD43 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `inc@R3D30` 14/18; `m23re30k@R3D30` 13/18; `m23re30kg@R3D30` 11/18; `m23re30kgf@R3D30` 12/18; `m23re30kgn@R3D30` 12/18; `m23e30kg@R3D30` 13/18; `m23re30k@WD30L` 13/18; `m23re30kg@WD30L` 16/18; `m23re30kgf@WD30L` 16/18; `m23re30k@P` 13/18; `m23re30kg@P` 15/18; `m23re30kgf@P` 16/18; `m23re30kgf@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `m23re30kgf`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD43 = seeds 4301 to 4360 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 38 (2026-11-04; HELD43 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorRangeHoldConfirmHoldRangeHeld43.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (4 of 6 cells).** The designated `m23re30kgf` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD; WD30L: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD. **5 of 8 predictions held; AM3, AM5, AM6 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 177 (59/59/59); `long+fix+forget` 174 (58/58/58); `inc@R3D30` 129 (45/43/41); `m23re30k@R3D30` 133 (50/31/52); `m23re30kg@R3D30` 132 (51/31/50); `m23re30kgf@R3D30` 132 (50/32/50); `m23re30kgn@R3D30` 138 (51/34/53); `m23e30kg@R3D30` 141 (50/41/50); `m23re30k@WD30L` 144 (51/39/54); `m23re30kg@WD30L` 144 (49/41/54); `m23re30kgf@WD30L` 147 (50/45/52); `m23re30k@P` 139 (52/41/46); `m23re30kg@P` 142 (57/37/48); `m23re30kgf@P` 137 (53/38/46); `m23re30kgf@clean` 169 (52/59/58).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AM1 HELD**: the far ghost is the one that matters: under R3D30, m23re30kgf is within 8 of m23re30kg. m23re30kgf@R3D30 132, m23re30kg@R3D30 132: 0 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AM2 HELD**: and the near ghost does nothing: under R3D30, m23re30kgn is within 8 of m23re30k. m23re30kgn@R3D30 138, m23re30k@R3D30 133: 5 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AM3 MISSED**: the far ghost helps the gated hold: under R3D30, m23re30kgf reaches at least 5 more than m23re30k. m23re30kgf@R3D30 132, m23re30k@R3D30 133: -1 against the 5 asked.
* **AM4 HELD**: under the leak the zone does not matter: under WD30L, m23re30kgf is within 8 of m23re30kg. m23re30kgf@WD30L 147, m23re30kg@WD30L 144: 3 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AM5 MISSED**: m23re30kgf holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. m23re30kgf@R3D30: 50/47.2 32/47.2 50/47.2 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AM6 MISSED**: and under WD30L. m23re30kgf@WD30L: 50/47.2 45/47.2 52/47.2 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AM7 HELD**: the far ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under P, m23re30kgf is within 8 of m23re30k. m23re30kgf@P 137, m23re30k@P 139: 2 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AM8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults m23re30kgf is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. m23re30kgf@clean 169, long+fix+forget 174: 5 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated far ghost holds four of six bar cells, misses the gaps kind in both seas, and is level with no ghost: PARTLY SUPPORTED.** `m23re30kgf` (a 2-of-3 mark, the gated evidence hold of cap 30, the clear confirmed at 0.8, the ghost for cells cleared beyond 80 m) holds islands and current under R3D30 (50 and 50 against 47.2 needed in each kind; exact 59, 59, 59) and under WD30L (50 and 52); the gaps kind is 32 under R3D30 and 45 under WD30L (47.2). Five of eight predictions held (AM1, AM2, AM4, AM7, AM8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `m23e30kg` (the hold at every range with the ghost) **141**, `m23re30kgn` (the ghost within 80 m only) 138, `m23re30k` (no ghost: follow-up 24's) 133, `m23re30kg` (the ghost at every range) 132, `m23re30kgf` **132**, `inc` 129 (exact 177, clean `long+fix+forget` 174); under WD30L: `m23re30kgf` **147**, `m23re30k` 144, `m23re30kg` 144; under P: `m23re30kg` 142, `m23re30k` 139, `m23re30kgf` 137.
* **The far ghost does what no ghost does (AM3 missed), and the zone does not matter (AM1, AM4 held).** `m23re30kgf` against `m23re30k`: −1 under R3D30 (5 more asked), +3 under WD30L and −2 under P; against the ghost at every range: 0, +3 and −5. POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `m23re30kgf` against `m23re30k` +12 / +13 (p 1.0) under R3D30, +12 / +9 (p 0.66) under WD30L, +9 / +11 (p 0.82) under P; against `m23re30kg` +6 / +6 (p 1.0), +7 / +4 (p 0.55) and +5 / +10 (p 0.30). The ghost fires (a mean of 19 re-marks a flight for the ghost at every range under R3D30, 14 for the far ghost and 5 for the near one; 23 and 17 under WD30L; 30 and 21 under P; 105 on a sea with no faults) and the far zone holds three quarters of them under R3D30.
* **The near ghost is the highest of the gated arms, by 5, and is not separable (AM2 held, within 8).** `m23re30kgn` 138 against `m23re30k` 133 and `m23re30kgf` 132 (POST HOC +6 / +12 against `m23re30kgf`, p 0.24). The registered reason (a near cell is held for what it earned, so a near ghost has nothing to restore) is not contradicted or confirmed by 5 flights in a set where one arm moves 7 to 12 between two sets of scenarios (follow-ups 22 and 37). In follow-ups 29, 30, 34 and 35 a ghost earned between −2 and +6 flights; here it does the same.
* **The gate is still where the loss is.** The hold at every range with the ghost (`m23e30kg`) is 9 above the far ghost on the gated hold under R3D30 (141, 132; POST HOC +13 / +22, p 0.18); gaps reached under R3D30 (of 59; 47.2 needed): `inc` 43, `m23e30kg` 41, the four gated arms 31 to 34; the 24 m gaps reached (of 20; exact 19): `inc` 8, `m23e30kg` 8, `m23re30kgn` 5, `m23re30kg` and `m23re30kgf` 4, `m23re30k` 2. Under WD30L the far ghost has the best gaps kind (45; 39 without a ghost, 41 with the ghost everywhere) and 10 of 20 at 24 m (7 and 6): the largest ghost effect on a kind in the set (4 to 6 flights), and it does not reach the 47.2 the bar needs.
* **A sea with no faults costs 5, within the 8 asked (AM8 held).** `m23re30kgf@clean` 169 against the clean map's 174 (POST HOC +4 / +9, p 0.27); collisions under R3D30: `m23e30kg` 37, `m23re30kgn` 42, `m23re30k` 45, `m23re30kg` and `m23re30kgf` 47, `inc` 49.

**What it does not say.** That a ghost cannot restore a cleared cell: the arms here clear a cell, drop it, and the ghost brings it back tens of times a flight, with no count moving by more than 6. That the near ghost earns 5 (one set cannot tell). That 80 m, the ghost's life of 60 and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 39: WHICH RANGE-AWARE CONFIRMATION WHEN THE CLEAR IS CONFIRMED TOO (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-confirm-hold-range-confirm", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 26's name (`detector-confirm-hold-range`: which `M`-of-`N` mark beyond 80 m, one reading within, when the evidence hold is there) with `-confirm` on the end, the move follow-up 25 made on follow-up 19, follow-up 24 on follow-up 13 and follow-up 27 on follow-up 15. The confirmation after the hold is the hold's, as in each of those: **the confirmed clear of follow-up 21** (`K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(30, occ)` readings free). Taken as **follow-up 22's question asked of the range-aware mark**, as follow-up 26 asked follow-up 20's: with the evidence hold at every range and its clear confirmed, which rule should the mark use beyond 80 m, with one reading within it? Follow-up 26 ends by saying that "a confirmed clear (follow-up 21) ... changes the order of the rules" is untested; this is that test. If it meant the confirmed clear's fraction depending on range (follow-up 31's) or the whole range-aware detector with the clear confirmed (follow-up 25's), say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 20's (under a hold the five confirming rules are one rule, spread 6 of 180; strictness cost where the misses are many), follow-up 17's and 18's (the range-aware mark with a hold was below the uniform pair when the hold was gated, level with it when the hold was at every range: `rm23e30` 134, `m23e30` 135 under R3D30; a first-reading mark within 80 m costs under the uniform false rate). **The confirmed clear itself (follow-up 21) and the family under it (follow-up 22) are unread or unflown**, and follow-up 22 registered the opposite of the first prediction below (that the family does *not* collapse under a confirmed clear) before any result; this one is made with follow-up 20 in view. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 26's detector with the clear confirmed). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** from the sonar at the ping is marked when at least `M` of its last `N` readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is **cleared** when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, at every range. With the mark of one at every range it is follow-up 21's `m11f80`.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 38): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law) and **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, the law NOT holding, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the confirmed clear's price is), and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range and the clear of `f80`): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the seven detectors **`m11f80`** (a mark of one everywhere: the control that repairs nothing), **`rm22f80`**, **`rm33f80`**, **`rm23f80`** and **`rm34f80`** (**the designated: 3-of-4 beyond 80 m, one reading within**), and, for the comparison with the same rule everywhere, **`m23f80`** (follow-up 21's designated) and **`m34f80`** (follow-up 22's); under `WD30L` `m11f80`, `rm23f80`, `rm34f80`, `m23f80` and `m34f80` (5); and `rm34f80` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD44 = seeds 4401 to 4460 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm34f80` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (The clean sea is in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The two registered effects of the confirmed clear (it cleans the fix smear that a long hold thickens; it clears a leaking dropout sooner) are felt in both zones, and the far zone is where the range law puts the false returns and the strict rule is cheap. A strict far rule is slow to re-mark a cell the clear takes (follow-up 22's X2); the near zone, marked at the first reading, takes a near false return at once, which `WD30L` (the law NOT holding) is built to find, with the leak on top.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AN).**
* **AN1** the family collapses under a confirmed clear too: under `R3D30`, the four range-aware confirming rules (`rm22f80`, `rm33f80`, `rm23f80`, `rm34f80`) have pooled counts within 20 of each other (largest minus smallest). (Follow-up 20's Y1 with the near zone alike in all four.)
* **AN2** every far confirmation repairs the range-law false returns: under `R3D30`, each of the four reaches at least 15 more than `m11f80`. (Follow-up 26's AA2.)
* **AN3** with the hold at every range the range-aware mark is level with the uniform one: under `R3D30`, `rm34f80` is within 8 of `m34f80` (either side). (`rm23e30` 134 and `m23e30` 135 in follow-up 18.)
* **AN4** strict and light are one rule under the leak: under `WD30L`, `rm34f80` is within 8 of `rm23f80` (either side). (Follow-up 20's Y1.)
* **AN5** `rm34f80` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AN6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AN7** the bet on the law has a price: under `WD30L`, `rm34f80` reaches at least 10 fewer than `m34f80`. (Follow-up 26's AA6; follow-up 17's Q5.)
* **AN8** on a sea with no faults `rm34f80` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm34f80` against `rm23f80` per kind and per gap, `m23f80` and `m34f80` against the range-aware rules under `WD30L`, and the pooled occupied cells at the last plan are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 3-of-4, 0.8 and a cap of 30 are the right numbers or that the far rule should be a step in range and not a smooth function; that the readings of a cell are independent; that the range law is true of a real sonar; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that a gated hold would not change the order. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, three registry rows (`rm22f80`, `rm33f80` and `rm34f80`; `rm23f80` is follow-up 25's row and `m11f80`, `m23f80` and `m34f80` are follow-ups 21 and 22's), the set HELD44 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD44 is.
