### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 39 (2026-11-04; HELD44 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorConfirmHoldRangeConfirmHeld44.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: NOT SUPPORTED (2 of 6 cells).** The designated `rm34f80` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD; WD30L: islands MISSED, gaps MISSED, current MISSED. **4 of 8 predictions held; AN2, AN4, AN5, AN6 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 174 (60/59/55); `long+fix+forget` 170 (55/60/55); `m11f80@R3D30` 128 (40/47/41); `rm22f80@R3D30` 125 (51/28/46); `rm33f80@R3D30` 118 (50/23/45); `rm23f80@R3D30` 126 (46/37/43); `rm34f80@R3D30` 132 (50/38/44); `m23f80@R3D30` 132 (44/38/50); `m34f80@R3D30` 138 (48/39/51); `m11f80@WD30L` 121 (36/57/28); `rm23f80@WD30L` 120 (39/48/33); `rm34f80@WD30L` 100 (30/36/34); `m23f80@WD30L` 159 (51/57/51); `m34f80@WD30L` 147 (50/46/51); `rm34f80@clean` 169 (56/59/54).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AN1 HELD**: the family collapses under a confirmed clear too: under R3D30, the four range-aware confirming rules have pooled counts within 20 of each other. rm22f80 125, rm33f80 118, rm23f80 126, rm34f80 132: 14 apart (within 20 asked).
* **AN2 MISSED**: every far confirmation repairs the range-law false returns: under R3D30, each of the four reaches at least 15 more than m11f80. rm22f80 125, rm33f80 118, rm23f80 126, rm34f80 132 against m11f80@R3D30 128 (15 asked).
* **AN3 HELD**: with the hold at every range the range-aware mark is level with the uniform one: under R3D30, rm34f80 is within 8 of m34f80. rm34f80@R3D30 132, m34f80@R3D30 138: 6 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AN4 MISSED**: strict and light are one rule under the leak: under WD30L, rm34f80 is within 8 of rm23f80. rm34f80@WD30L 100, rm23f80@WD30L 120: 20 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AN5 MISSED**: rm34f80 holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. rm34f80@R3D30: 50/48.0 38/47.2 44/44.0 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AN6 MISSED**: and under WD30L. rm34f80@WD30L: 30/48.0 36/47.2 34/44.0 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AN7 HELD**: the bet on the law has a price: under WD30L, rm34f80 reaches at least 10 fewer than m34f80. m34f80@WD30L 147, rm34f80@WD30L 100: +47 against the 10 asked.
* **AN8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults rm34f80 is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. rm34f80@clean 169, long+fix+forget 170: 1 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated strict far rule with the confirmed clear holds two of six bar cells: NOT SUPPORTED.** `rm34f80` (3 of 4 beyond 80 m, a single reading within, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range, the clear confirmed at 0.8) holds islands and current under R3D30 (50 and 44 against 48.0 and 44.0 needed; current exactly at the line) and nothing under WD30L (30, 36 and 34 against 48.0, 47.2 and 44.0); the gaps kind under R3D30 is 38 against 47.2. Four of eight predictions held (AN1, AN3, AN7, AN8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `m34f80` (3-of-4 at every range: follow-up 22's) **138**, `rm34f80` **132**, `m23f80` (follow-up 21's designated) 132, `m11f80` (a mark of one: the control) 128, `rm23f80` 126, `rm22f80` 125, `rm33f80` 118 (exact 174, clean `long+fix+forget` 170); under WD30L: `m23f80` **159**, `m34f80` 147, `m11f80` 121, `rm23f80` 120, `rm34f80` **100**.
* **Under range-law false returns the four range-aware rules are one family, level with the uniform ones and with the mark of one (AN1 and AN3 held, AN2 missed).** `rm22f80`, `rm33f80`, `rm23f80` and `rm34f80` are 14 apart (118 to 132; 20 allowed); `rm34f80` is 6 below `m34f80` (within 8 asked) and level with `m23f80`. AN2 asked each far confirmation to repair the range-law false returns by 15 over `m11f80`: the nearest, `rm34f80`, is 4 above; `rm33f80` is 10 **below**. POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `rm34f80` against `m34f80` +25 / +31 (p 0.50), against `m23f80` +16 / +16 (p 1.0), against `m11f80` +34 / +30 (p 0.71), against `rm33f80` +32 / +18 (p 0.065). Follow-up 26 found the same for the run clear: the strict far rule is the lowest of its family and no better than the hold alone.
* **Under the uniform false rate with leaking dropouts the strict range-aware rule collapses: AN4 missed, AN7 held, by wide margins.** `rm34f80@WD30L` is 100: 20 below the light range-aware rule (`rm23f80` 120; within 8 asked), **47 below** `m34f80` (147; 10 asked) and 59 below `m23f80`; it collides in 68 flights of 180 with a median minimum clearance of 4.3 m (`m34f80`: 33, 9.3 m; `m23f80`: 18, 10.4 m). POST HOC `rm34f80` against `m34f80` +12 / +59 and against `m23f80` +8 / +67 (both p below 0.0001), against `rm23f80` +22 / +42 (p 0.017), against `m11f80` +27 / +48 (p 0.020). A first reading within 80 m marks a uniform false return at once; the strict far rule filters nothing of that.
* **The gaps kind decides the R3D30 bar, and the 2-of-3 is the one rule that holds the lot under the leak.** Gaps reached under R3D30 (of 59; 47.2 needed): `m11f80` 47, `m34f80` 39, `m23f80` and `rm34f80` 38, `rm23f80` 37, `rm22f80` 28, `rm33f80` 23; the 24 m gaps (of 20; exact 19): `m11f80` and `rm34f80` 9, `rm23f80` 6, `m34f80` 5, `m23f80` 4, `rm22f80` 2, `rm33f80` 1. Under WD30L only `m23f80` holds all three kinds (51, 57, 51). The same uniform 2-of-3 rule with the confirmed clear reached 163 under WD30L in follow-up 21, 156 in follow-up 22 and 159 here; `m34f80` 152 and 147.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing (AN8 held).** `rm34f80@clean` 169 against the clean map's 170 (POST HOC +6 / +7, p 1.0).

**What it does not say.** That a range-aware rule is a bad idea: it is the same price follow-up 26 found for the run clear (the first-reading mark within 80 m), now with the confirmed clear, and no arm here isolates the near reading. That the confirmed clear changes the range-aware family (it is not compared with the run clear in this set). That 80 m, 0.8 and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 40: THE GHOST BEHIND THE CONFIRMED CLEAR THAT KNOWS HOW FAR (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-confirm-range-hold", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 31's name (`detector-range-hold-confirm-range`: the confirmed clear whose fraction depends on range) with `-hold` on the end, the move follow-ups 18, 29, 30, 32, 34 and 35 made: **a second hold, on what has been held**, which is **the ghost** (a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings keeps its window and evidence for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time it is marked again at that reading, with its evidence put back to 4; no neighbour test: that is follow-up 23's). Follow-up 31's detector is kept as it was (the 2-of-3 mark at every range; the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range; a marked cell cleared when at least `K = ceil(f L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, `f` = `fNear` for a cell within 80 m at the ping and `fFar` beyond). The reason the two belong together is the one follow-up 31 gives: **the confirmed clear clears a leaking dropout sooner** (follow-up 21's A4), and the ghost is the stage that puts such a cell back at one reading; what is new is that the clear's fraction can now differ by range, so what the ghost restores differs by range too. If it meant the ghost judged by range (that is follow-up 38's) or the hold range-gated, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range, and its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirming rules are one rule; strictness cost where the misses are many). **The confirmed clear itself (follow-up 21), the ghost behind the confirmed hold (follow-up 29) and the fraction by range (follow-up 31) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about the confirmed clear is made without any result: the claim that it clears a leaking dropout sooner is a registered mechanism, not a finding. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 31's detector with a switch). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` and a ghost counter. A free cell is **marked** when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range; a marked cell is **cleared** when at least `K = ceil(f x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, `f` = `fNear` (within 80 m of the sonar at the ping, inclusive) or `fFar` (beyond). **With the ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time with `occ = 4`, whatever the mark rule; after 60 it is forgotten. With `fNear` = `fFar` and no ghost it is follow-up 21's detector.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 39): **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the leak is), **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the range law with the false rate 0.03 at its mean and dropouts of 30 pings, no leak: where the smear is), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a restored phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, the 2-of-3 mark and the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `WD30L` and `R3D30` the five detectors **`f80`** (0.8 at every range: follow-up 21's designated), **`fn100f80`** (a run within 80 m, 0.8 beyond: follow-up 31's designated), **`f80g`** (`f80` with the ghost), **`fn100f80g`** (**the designated: the same with the ghost**) and **`fn80f100g`** (0.8 within, a run beyond, with the ghost: the opposite bet) (10 arms); under `P` `fn100f80` and `fn100f80g` (2); and `fn100f80g` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD45 = seeds 4501 to 4560 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `fn100f80g` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `WD30L` and under `R3D30`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** A strict fraction near and a tolerant one far is follow-up 31's bet: the leak is paid near and the smear is cheap far. The ghost cuts across it. It restores a cell the tolerant far clear took, at one reading, so it makes the far tolerance cheaper still; and it restores a smear edge cell that a strict near clear would have kept, which is the cure follow-up 21 registered and the ghost undoes. Under the persistent mix the ghost restores a persistent false return that once earned four readings. The map that forgets is not an arm here (the arms are compared with each other and with `exact`).

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AO).**
* **AO1** the ghost answers the leak behind the strict near clear: under `WD30L`, `fn100f80g` reaches at least 5 more than `fn100f80`. (A leaking dropout clears cells far away; the ghost restores them at one reading. Follow-up 29's AD4.)
* **AO2** and helps under dropouts without a leak: under `R3D30`, `fn100f80g` reaches at least 5 more than `fn100f80`. (Follow-up 18's J3 and J4: +9 and +5.)
* **AO3** with a ghost the fraction by range is one rule: under `WD30L`, `fn100f80g` is within 8 of `f80g` (either side). (The ghost restores what the clear takes.)
* **AO4** and the opposite bet is no better: under `R3D30`, `fn80f100g` is within 8 of `fn100f80g` (either side).
* **AO5** `fn100f80g` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AO6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AO7** the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `fn100f80g` is within 8 of `fn100f80` (either side). (Follow-up 18: `rm23e30g@P` 159 against `inc` 160.)
* **AO8** on a sea with no faults `fn100f80g` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`f80g` against `f80` per sea, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 2-of-3, 30, 1.0, 0.8, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers or that the fraction should be a step in range and not a smooth function; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that the ghost is the only second hold (a decayed evidence, a hold on the neighbours, a K-of-N clear are untried). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, `clear.fNear` (default `f`; the same field follow-up 31 needs, built once), three registry rows (`f80g`, `fn100f80g` and `fn80f100g`; `f80`/`m23f80` is follow-up 21's, `fn100f80` follow-up 31's, and `f80g` is the detector follow-up 23 calls `gU`), the set HELD45 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD45 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 40 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD45 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers: the detector module with the five optional fields of this stretch (`clear.fNear`, `ghost.carry`, `ghost.zone`, `ghost.at`, `lock`) and 77 more registry rows (59 mutants of the new lines, 55 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 3 equivalent and said; 154 mechanical mutants of the 77 rows, all red); the table of sets (HELD30 to HELD63, seeds 4501 to 4560 for this one); the study engine with its new operation `noBar` and the 34 more specs (1,377 mechanical mutants of the specs, all red by the row that holds them to the registrations; 10 of the helpers they call and 2 of the list of operations; 12 of the engine's `noBar` line, 9 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 2 equivalent and said; each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against a literal copy of the registrations in the gate, and the table that generated both against the registered prose by a script (272 predictions' operations, thresholds, directions and arms mechanically, 14 by hand: no disagreement); the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `f80@WD30L` 17/18; `fn100f80@WD30L` 17/18; `f80g@WD30L` 18/18; `fn100f80g@WD30L` 17/18; `fn80f100g@WD30L` 17/18; `f80@R3D30` 12/18; `fn100f80@R3D30` 13/18; `f80g@R3D30` 13/18; `fn100f80g@R3D30` 13/18; `fn80f100g@R3D30` 16/18; `fn100f80@P` 16/18; `fn100f80g@P` 16/18; `fn100f80g@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `fn100f80g`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD45 = seeds 4501 to 4560 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 40 (2026-11-04; HELD45 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorRangeHoldConfirmRangeHoldHeld45.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (5 of 6 cells).** The designated `fn100f80g` against 80% of exact's count: WD30L: islands HELD, gaps HELD, current HELD; R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD. **6 of 8 predictions held; AO1, AO5 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 170 (60/58/52); `long+fix+forget` 174 (60/58/56); `f80@WD30L` 159 (56/54/49); `fn100f80@WD30L` 162 (57/56/49); `f80g@WD30L` 156 (52/58/46); `fn100f80g@WD30L` 158 (52/57/49); `fn80f100g@WD30L` 162 (54/60/48); `f80@R3D30` 136 (53/39/44); `fn100f80@R3D30` 134 (51/40/43); `f80g@R3D30` 137 (54/37/46); `fn100f80g@R3D30` 140 (52/42/46); `fn80f100g@R3D30` 141 (54/41/46); `fn100f80@P` 150 (55/48/47); `fn100f80g@P` 155 (53/53/49); `fn100f80g@clean` 169 (59/58/52).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AO1 MISSED**: the ghost answers the leak behind the strict near clear: under WD30L, fn100f80g reaches at least 5 more than fn100f80. fn100f80g@WD30L 158, fn100f80@WD30L 162: -4 against the 5 asked.
* **AO2 HELD**: and helps under dropouts without a leak: under R3D30, fn100f80g reaches at least 5 more than fn100f80. fn100f80g@R3D30 140, fn100f80@R3D30 134: +6 against the 5 asked.
* **AO3 HELD**: with a ghost the fraction by range is one rule: under WD30L, fn100f80g is within 8 of f80g. fn100f80g@WD30L 158, f80g@WD30L 156: 2 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AO4 HELD**: and the opposite bet is no better: under R3D30, fn80f100g is within 8 of fn100f80g. fn80f100g@R3D30 141, fn100f80g@R3D30 140: 1 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AO5 MISSED**: fn100f80g holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. fn100f80g@R3D30: 52/48.0 42/46.4 46/41.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AO6 HELD**: and under WD30L. fn100f80g@WD30L: 52/48.0 57/46.4 49/41.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AO7 HELD**: the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under P, fn100f80g is within 8 of fn100f80. fn100f80g@P 155, fn100f80@P 150: 5 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AO8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults fn100f80g is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. fn100f80g@clean 169, long+fix+forget 174: 5 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated ghost behind the fraction by range holds five of six bar cells, and the ghost's sign changes with the sea: PARTLY SUPPORTED.** `fn100f80g` (a 2-of-3 mark, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range, a run clear within 80 m and 0.8 beyond, the ghost) holds all three kinds under WD30L (52, 57, 49 against 48.0, 46.4 and 41.6 needed) and islands and current under R3D30 (52 and 46); the gaps kind under R3D30 is 42 against 46.4. Six of eight predictions held (AO2, AO3, AO4, AO6, AO7, AO8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `fn80f100g` (the opposite bet) **141**, `fn100f80g` **140**, `f80g` 137, `f80` 136, `fn100f80` 134 (exact 170, clean `long+fix+forget` 174); under WD30L: `fn100f80` **162**, `fn80f100g` **162**, `f80` 159, `fn100f80g` 158, `f80g` 156; under P: `fn100f80g` 155, `fn100f80` 150.
* **The ghost earns +6 under range-law false returns with dropouts and costs 4 under the leak (AO2 held, AO1 missed).** `fn100f80g` against `fn100f80`: +6 under R3D30 (5 asked), −4 under WD30L (5 more asked) and +5 under P. The ghost on the 0.8 clear at every range: `f80g` against `f80` +1 under R3D30 and −3 under WD30L. POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `fn100f80g` against `fn100f80` +13 / +7 (p 0.26) under R3D30, +5 / +9 (p 0.42) under WD30L and +10 / +5 (p 0.30) under P; against `f80g` +8 / +5 (p 0.58) and +5 / +3 (p 0.73). Follow-ups 29, 34 and 35 found the ghost 1 to 7 flights above no ghost under the leak; this set finds it 3 to 4 below. Across follow-ups 29, 30, 34, 35, 38 and 40 the ghost's effect against no ghost lies between −4 and +7 with no consistent sign, in sets where one arm moves 7 to 12 flights between two sets of scenarios. It fires: a mean of 21 to 23 re-marks a flight under R3D30, 23 to 30 under WD30L, 28 under P and 61 on a sea with no faults.
* **With a ghost the fraction by range is one rule (AO3 and AO4 held), and without one the four clears are within 3 under the leak.** `fn100f80g` and `f80g` are 158 and 156 under WD30L; `fn80f100g` and `fn100f80g` are 141 and 140 under R3D30. Without the ghost under WD30L `fn100f80` 162 and `f80` 159, which is follow-up 31's order (`fn100f80` 164, `f80` 159) at the same values. Under R3D30 the same two arms read 134 and 136 here and 145 and 145 in follow-up 31's HELD36 (−11 and −9): a larger swing than the 7 to 9 follow-up 22 measured, and the R3D30 sets are the noisier.
* **The gaps kind decides the R3D30 bar again.** Gaps reached under R3D30 (of 58; 46.4 needed): `fn100f80g` 42, `fn80f100g` 41, `fn100f80` 40, `f80` 39, `f80g` 37; the 24 m gaps (of 20; exact 18): 6, 9, 7, 6 and 5. Under WD30L the ghost arms reach the 24 m gaps more often (`fn80f100g` 20, `f80g` 18, `fn100f80g` 17 against 16 and 16 without). Collisions under R3D30: `fn100f80g` 35, `fn80f100g` 38, `f80` and `f80g` 40, `fn100f80` 43; under WD30L: `fn80f100g` 15, `fn100f80` 17, `f80` 18, `f80g` 20, `fn100f80g` 21.
* **A sea with no faults costs 5, within the 8 asked (AO8 held).** `fn100f80g@clean` 169 against the clean map's 174 (POST HOC +2 / +7, p 0.18); the clean map reaches 174 against exact's 170 in this set (4 more).

**What it does not say.** That the ghost is useless: it is worth +6 in one sea and −4 in another here, and between −2 and +7 elsewhere, which this design cannot resolve. That the fraction by range matters (it does not, with or without a ghost). That 80 m, 0.8, a ghost life of 60 and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 41: THE GHOST THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, BEHIND THE RANGE-AWARE MARK (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-confirm-range-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 18's name (`detector-hold-confirm-range-hold`: the range-aware 2-of-3 mark with the evidence hold at every range and the ghost) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-ups 37 and 38 made on follow-ups 32 and 29: **the second hold knowing how far**. Follow-up 18's detector is kept (2-of-3 beyond 80 m, a first reading within; the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range; the ghost: a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings keeps its window and evidence for 60 readings of that cell and is marked again at one occupied reading with its evidence put back to 4). **The zone is defined here by the range at the ping that CLEARS the cell**, not at the ping that re-marks it as follow-ups 37 and 38 do, for a reason read from the module (a scratch script, not committed): with a first-reading mark within 80 m **a ghost re-marked within 80 m does nothing**, because the mark rule's branch comes first in `apply` and marks the cell at one reading with evidence 1 before the ghost's branch can restore 4. Run on `rm23e30` with and without the ghost: a cell cleared after six readings and read again from 40 m is marked at once both ways, with the second clear after 1 free reading both ways; read from 120 m it is **not** marked without the ghost and **is** with it, with the second clear after 4. A zone on the re-marking ping would make the `near` arm identical to no ghost by construction; a zone on the clearing ping does not (a cell cleared near and read next from far is a ghost that acts). The ghost is created for cells cleared **beyond 80 m** (`far`), **within 80 m inclusive** (`near`) or **at any range** (`all`: follow-up 18's). If it meant the zone on the re-marking ping (then `near` is a no-op and the round is `far` against `all`), or the hold range-gated, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and **this round rests on follow-up 18's, which is read**: the designated `rm23e30g` had the highest count of the six detectors (139 of 180 under range-law false returns with dropouts) and the top four were level; taking the range gate off the hold paid +10; the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; the hold at every range with the ghost was 13 above the gated pair under the uniform false rate; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals (159 against the map that forgets' 160 at 16 re-marks a flight). The predictions below were written with that in view. The sets of follow-ups 19 and 21 to 24 are unread or unflown. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 18's `rm23e30g` with a zone on the ghost). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` and a ghost counter. A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** from the sonar at the ping is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is cleared after `min(30, occ)` consecutive free readings at every range. **The ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 **at a ping from the zone** becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time, from any range, with `occ = 4`; a cell cleared from the other zone is forgotten at once (window and evidence emptied). After 60 readings a ghost is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 40): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law: the bar's), **`W`** (the uniform false rate of 0.01, the law NOT holding), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23e30`** (follow-up 16's uniform pair), **`rm23e30`** (follow-up 18's, no ghost), **`rm23e30g`** (follow-up 18's designated: the ghost at every range), **`rm23e30gf`** (**the designated: the ghost for cells cleared beyond 80 m**) and **`rm23e30gn`** (the ghost for cells cleared within 80 m); under `W` `rm23e30`, `rm23e30g` and `rm23e30gf` (3) and under `P` the same three (3); `rm23e30gf` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD46 = seeds 4601 to 4660 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm23e30gf` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30`**: three cells, follow-up 18's bar on a fresh set. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`W`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The far ghost is the whole of what the ghost does for a cell that is marked at one reading near the vessel, so `far` and `all` should be one detector in effect; the difference between them is only what a near-cleared cell does when the vessel has moved on and the cell is read from beyond 80 m, the window it kept counting towards a 2-of-3. The near-created ghost does that and nothing else. The effect of the whole round is expected to be small, and the registration says so.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AP).**
* **AP1** the far ghost is the one that matters: under `R3D30`, `rm23e30gf` is within 8 of `rm23e30g` (either side). (A near re-marking is a no-op.)
* **AP2** the near-created ghost does little: under `R3D30`, `rm23e30gn` is within 8 of `rm23e30` (either side).
* **AP3** the far ghost helps: under `R3D30`, `rm23e30gf` reaches at least 3 more than `rm23e30`. (Follow-up 18's J4: +5 for the ghost at every range.)
* **AP4** the ghost does not cost where the law fails: under `W`, `rm23e30gf` is within 10 of `rm23e30` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J2 saw the ghost pair above the gated pair there.)
* **AP5** and its price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `rm23e30gf` is within 8 of `rm23e30` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J7.)
* **AP6** `rm23e30gf` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AP7** the designated beats the uniform pair: under `R3D30`, `rm23e30gf` reaches more than `m23e30`. (Follow-up 18's J6 asked 10 more and found +4 for the ghost at every range.)
* **AP8** on a sea with no faults `rm23e30gf` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm23e30gf` against `rm23e30g` per kind and per gap and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 2-of-3, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the zone should be a step in range and not a smooth weight; that the range at the clearing ping is the right range for the ghost (the re-marking ping is follow-up 38's; the nearest the cell was read from is untried); that the range law is true of a real sonar. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **two new fields in the module**, a zone for the ghost (`ghost.zone`: `all` by default, `far` or `near`; the field follow-ups 37 and 38 need) and **which ping it is judged at** (`ghost.at`: `mark` by default, as follow-ups 37 and 38, or `clear`, as here): validation, the clearing path (a cell cleared from the other zone is emptied), `describe`, gate rows and mutants; two registry rows (`rm23e30gf` and `rm23e30gn`; `rm23e30`, `rm23e30g`, `m23e30` and `inc` exist), the set HELD46 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD46 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 41 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD46 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers: the detector module with the five optional fields of this stretch (`clear.fNear`, `ghost.carry`, `ghost.zone`, `ghost.at`, `lock`) and 77 more registry rows (59 mutants of the new lines, 55 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 3 equivalent and said; 154 mechanical mutants of the 77 rows, all red); the table of sets (HELD30 to HELD63, seeds 4601 to 4660 for this one); the study engine with its new operation `noBar` and the 34 more specs (1,377 mechanical mutants of the specs, all red by the row that holds them to the registrations; 10 of the helpers they call and 2 of the list of operations; 12 of the engine's `noBar` line, 9 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 2 equivalent and said; each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against a literal copy of the registrations in the gate, and the table that generated both against the registered prose by a script (272 predictions' operations, thresholds, directions and arms mechanically, 14 by hand: no disagreement); the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `inc@R3D30` 14/18; `m23e30@R3D30` 15/18; `rm23e30@R3D30` 14/18; `rm23e30g@R3D30` 16/18; `rm23e30gf@R3D30` 16/18; `rm23e30gn@R3D30` 14/18; `rm23e30@W` 13/18; `rm23e30g@W` 13/18; `rm23e30gf@W` 13/18; `rm23e30@P` 16/18; `rm23e30g@P` 18/18; `rm23e30gf@P` 18/18; `rm23e30gf@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `rm23e30gf`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD46 = seeds 4601 to 4660 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 41 (2026-11-04; HELD46 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorHoldConfirmRangeHoldRangeHeld46.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (2 of 3 cells).** The designated `rm23e30gf` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD. **5 of 8 predictions held; AP5, AP6, AP7 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 172 (60/58/54); `long+fix+forget` 174 (59/57/58); `inc@R3D30` 134 (45/47/42); `m23e30@R3D30` 143 (53/39/51); `rm23e30@R3D30` 138 (54/36/48); `rm23e30g@R3D30` 141 (54/37/50); `rm23e30gf@R3D30` 141 (54/37/50); `rm23e30gn@R3D30` 138 (54/36/48); `rm23e30@W` 123 (39/53/31); `rm23e30g@W` 125 (40/55/30); `rm23e30gf@W` 125 (40/55/30); `rm23e30@P` 148 (55/47/46); `rm23e30g@P` 160 (59/52/49); `rm23e30gf@P` 160 (59/52/49); `rm23e30gf@clean` 174 (57/60/57).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AP1 HELD**: the far ghost is the one that matters: under R3D30, rm23e30gf is within 8 of rm23e30g. rm23e30gf@R3D30 141, rm23e30g@R3D30 141: 0 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AP2 HELD**: the near-created ghost does little: under R3D30, rm23e30gn is within 8 of rm23e30. rm23e30gn@R3D30 138, rm23e30@R3D30 138: 0 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AP3 HELD**: the far ghost helps: under R3D30, rm23e30gf reaches at least 3 more than rm23e30. rm23e30gf@R3D30 141, rm23e30@R3D30 138: +3 against the 3 asked.
* **AP4 HELD**: the ghost does not cost where the law fails: under W, rm23e30gf is within 10 of rm23e30. rm23e30gf@W 125, rm23e30@W 123: 2 apart, within 10 asked.
* **AP5 MISSED**: and its price under the persistent mix is small: under P, rm23e30gf is within 8 of rm23e30. rm23e30gf@P 160, rm23e30@P 148: 12 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AP6 MISSED**: rm23e30gf holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. rm23e30gf@R3D30: 54/48.0 37/46.4 50/43.2 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AP7 MISSED**: the designated beats the uniform pair: under R3D30, rm23e30gf reaches more than m23e30. rm23e30gf@R3D30 141 against m23e30@R3D30 143.
* **AP8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults rm23e30gf is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. rm23e30gf@clean 174, long+fix+forget 174: 0 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated far ghost holds two of three bar cells (the gaps kind is 37 against 46.4), and in this set the four ghost arms are nearly two detectors: PARTLY SUPPORTED.** `rm23e30gf` (a 2-of-3 mark beyond 80 m and a first reading within, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range, the ghost for cells cleared beyond 80 m) reaches 54, 37 and 50 under R3D30 against 48.0, 46.4 and 43.2 needed. Five of eight predictions held (AP1 to AP4, AP8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `m23e30` (the uniform pair: follow-up 16's) **143**, `rm23e30g` (the ghost at every range) **141**, `rm23e30gf` **141**, `rm23e30` (no ghost) 138, `rm23e30gn` (the ghost within 80 m) 138, `inc` 134 (exact 172, clean `long+fix+forget` 174); under W: `rm23e30g` 125, `rm23e30gf` 125, `rm23e30` 123; under P: `rm23e30g` 160, `rm23e30gf` 160, `rm23e30` 148.
* **AP1 and AP2 held almost by construction.** With a first-reading mark within 80 m the ordinary mark rule is tried before the ghost and marks a near cell at its first occupied reading, so a ghost re-mark within 80 m has next to nothing to do: `rm23e30gf` is nearly the same detector as `rm23e30g` and `rm23e30gn` as `rm23e30`. Compared flight by flight (status, time, path, plans, clearance, offset, occupied cells), 175 of 180 flights of `rm23e30gf` and `rm23e30g` are identical under R3D30, 171 under W and 178 under P, and 174 of `rm23e30gn` and `rm23e30` under R3D30; the remainder differ in some field and in none of the 180 scenarios in whether the vessel arrived (POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `rm23e30gf` against `rm23e30g` +0 / +0 under R3D30, W and P). The registration said "a near re-marking is a no-op" for AP1; the residue (a ghost keeps the window and the evidence of a near cell it did not re-mark, and the ordinary rule can use them later) was not foreseen, and its size is 2 to 9 flights of 180 in a recorded field, none in the outcome. The mean ghost re-marks a flight are 10 for `rm23e30g`, 9 for `rm23e30gf` and 0 for `rm23e30gn` under R3D30.
* **The far ghost earns 3 under range-law false returns with dropouts and 12 under the persistent mix (AP3 held at its edge, AP5 missed the other way).** `rm23e30gf` against `rm23e30`: +3 under R3D30 (3 asked; +8 / +5, p 0.58), +2 under W (+10 / +8, p 0.81) and **+12 under P**: AP5 asked a small price (within 8) and the ghost is 12 *above* (160, 148; +16 / +4, p 0.012, unadjusted for the several tests made here), with 14 collisions against 26. Follow-ups 29, 30, 34, 35, 38 and 40 found the ghost between −4 and +7 under their seas; under the persistent mix −4 to +5. No other set's persistent comparison is as large, and no arm here isolates why.
* **The designated is not above the uniform pair (AP7 missed), and the gaps kind decides the bar.** `rm23e30gf` 141 against `m23e30` 143 (2 below; follow-up 18's J6 asked 10 above and found +4 for the ghost at every range; POST HOC +12 / +14, p 0.85) and 7 above `inc`'s 134 (+31 / +24, p 0.42). Gaps reached under R3D30 (of 58; 46.4 needed): `inc` 47, `m23e30` 39, `rm23e30gf` 37, `rm23e30` 36; the 24 m gaps (of 20; exact 18): 10, 6, 5, 4. Under W (uniform false rate) the range-aware mark with the hold at every range does not collapse (123 to 125), unlike the strict far rule and the gated hold of follow-ups 25 and 26.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing (AP8 held).** `rm23e30gf@clean` 174 against the clean map's 174 (POST HOC +5 / +5, p 1.0).

**What it does not say.** That a far ghost helps the persistent mix in general (one set, one unadjusted p of 0.012 among many comparisons across the stretch). That the near ghost is useless where the near zone is not a first-reading mark (this set cannot say: with the first-reading mark the near ghost has next to nothing to do). That 80 m, 30 and a ghost life of 60 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 42: THE GHOST FOR THE STRICT FAR MARK, WITH THE CLEAR CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-confirm-hold-range-hold-confirm", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 30's name (`detector-confirm-hold-range-hold`: the ghost behind the strict far mark with the evidence hold) with `-confirm` on the end, the move follow-ups 24, 25, 27 and 39 made: the confirmation after the hold is the hold's, **the confirmed clear of follow-up 21** (`K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(30, occ)` readings free). Follow-up 30's detector is kept (3-of-4 beyond 80 m, a first reading within; the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range; the ghost) and its clear is confirmed. It is also **follow-up 39's detector with the ghost** (`rm34f80` with a second hold), and follow-up 40's cousin with the mark range-aware and the fraction the same at every range. If it meant something else, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range, the range-aware detector with the ghost had the highest count of its table, the ghost's price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirming rules are one rule; strictness cost where the misses are many). **The confirmed clear (follow-up 21), the strict far mark with the hold (follow-up 26), the ghost behind it (follow-up 30) and the confirmed range-aware family (follow-up 39) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 30's detector with the clear confirmed). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` and a ghost counter. A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** from the sonar at the ping is marked when at least `M` of its last `N` readings were occupied (3 of 4 in the designated), one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, at every range (**confirmed**: `f80`) or after `min(30, occ)` free readings in a row (**run**: `e30`). **With the ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell and is marked at an occupied reading in that time, from any range, with `occ = 4`, whatever the mark rule; after 60 it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 41): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, the law NOT holding, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the confirmed clear's price is), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a restored phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, the range-aware mark and the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `R3D30` and `WD30L` the five detectors **`rm34e30`** (follow-up 26's designated: the strict far mark, a run clear, no ghost), **`rm34e30g`** (follow-up 30's designated: with the ghost), **`rm34f80`** (follow-up 39's designated: the confirmed clear, no ghost), **`rm34f80g`** (**the designated: the confirmed clear and the ghost**) and **`rm23f80g`** (the light far mark, 2 of 3, with the confirmed clear and the ghost) (10 arms); under `P` `rm34f80` and `rm34f80g` (2); and `rm34f80g` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD47 = seeds 4701 to 4760 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm34f80g` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The confirmed clear clears a leaking dropout sooner (follow-up 21's registered mechanism) and the ghost restores a cell it clears at one reading, so the ghost is the stage that can pay the clear's registered price; the confirmed clear in turn cleans the smear edge cells that the ghost would otherwise re-mark one reading at a time. Against both: a strict far rule is slow to mark, which neither stage touches, and the persistent mix is built to find the phantoms a ghost restores.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AQ).**
* **AQ1** the ghost answers the leak behind the confirmed clear: under `WD30L`, `rm34f80g` reaches at least 5 more than `rm34f80`. (Follow-up 29's AD4 and follow-up 30's AE1.)
* **AQ2** and helps the strict rule where the dropouts do not leak: under `R3D30`, `rm34f80g` reaches at least 5 more than `rm34f80`. (Follow-up 18's J3 and J4.)
* **AQ3** behind a ghost the confirmed clear changes little where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `rm34f80g` is within 8 of `rm34e30g` (either side).
* **AQ4** and under the leak: under `WD30L`, `rm34f80g` is within 8 of `rm34e30g` (either side).
* **AQ5** the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `rm34f80g` is within 8 of `rm34f80` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J7.)
* **AQ6** `rm34f80g` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AQ7** and under `WD30L`.
* **AQ8** on a sea with no faults `rm34f80g` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm34f80g` against `rm23f80g` per sea (strict against light, follow-up 30's AE3), the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 3-of-4, 0.8, 30, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that the range law is true of a real sonar; that the ghost is the only second hold (a decayed evidence, a hold on the neighbours, a K-of-N clear are untried). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, two registry rows (`rm34f80g` and `rm23f80g`; `rm34e30` is follow-up 26's row, `rm34e30g` follow-up 30's and `rm34f80` follow-up 39's), the set HELD47 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD47 is.

What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `rm34f80g`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD47 = seeds 4701 to 4760 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 42 (2026-11-04; HELD47 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorConfirmHoldRangeHoldConfirmHeld47.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: NOT SUPPORTED (0 of 6 cells).** The designated `rm34f80g` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands MISSED, gaps MISSED, current MISSED; WD30L: islands MISSED, gaps MISSED, current MISSED. **4 of 8 predictions held; AQ1, AQ2, AQ6, AQ7 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 178 (60/60/58); `long+fix+forget` 174 (57/60/57); `rm34e30@R3D30` 119 (44/34/41); `rm34e30g@R3D30` 116 (40/34/42); `rm34f80@R3D30` 119 (44/30/45); `rm34f80g@R3D30` 119 (42/31/46); `rm23f80g@R3D30` 127 (43/38/46); `rm34e30@WD30L` 84 (24/35/25); `rm34e30g@WD30L` 90 (26/40/24); `rm34f80@WD30L` 90 (32/33/25); `rm34f80g@WD30L` 88 (27/36/25); `rm23f80g@WD30L` 108 (32/53/23); `rm34f80@P` 138 (46/43/49); `rm34f80g@P` 145 (49/48/48); `rm34f80g@clean` 174 (57/60/57).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AQ1 MISSED**: the ghost answers the leak behind the confirmed clear: under WD30L, rm34f80g reaches at least 5 more than rm34f80. rm34f80g@WD30L 88, rm34f80@WD30L 90: -2 against the 5 asked.
* **AQ2 MISSED**: and helps the strict rule where the dropouts do not leak: under R3D30, rm34f80g reaches at least 5 more than rm34f80. rm34f80g@R3D30 119, rm34f80@R3D30 119: +0 against the 5 asked.
* **AQ3 HELD**: behind a ghost the confirmed clear changes little where the law holds: under R3D30, rm34f80g is within 8 of rm34e30g. rm34f80g@R3D30 119, rm34e30g@R3D30 116: 3 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AQ4 HELD**: and under the leak: under WD30L, rm34f80g is within 8 of rm34e30g. rm34f80g@WD30L 88, rm34e30g@WD30L 90: 2 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AQ5 HELD**: the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under P, rm34f80g is within 8 of rm34f80. rm34f80g@P 145, rm34f80@P 138: 7 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AQ6 MISSED**: rm34f80g holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. rm34f80g@R3D30: 42/48.0 31/48.0 46/46.4 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AQ7 MISSED**: and under WD30L. rm34f80g@WD30L: 27/48.0 36/48.0 25/46.4 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AQ8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults rm34f80g is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. rm34f80g@clean 174, long+fix+forget 174: 0 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated confirmed clear with the ghost on the strict far rule holds none of six bar cells: NOT SUPPORTED.** `rm34f80g` (3 of 4 beyond 80 m, a single reading within, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range, the clear confirmed at 0.8, the ghost) reaches 42, 31 and 46 under R3D30 against 48.0, 48.0 and 46.4 needed (current 0.4 short) and 27, 36 and 25 under WD30L. Four of eight predictions held (AQ3, AQ4, AQ5, AQ8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `rm23f80g` (the light far mark, 2 of 3, with the confirmed clear and the ghost) **127**, `rm34e30`, `rm34f80` and `rm34f80g` **119** each, `rm34e30g` 116 (exact 178, clean `long+fix+forget` 174); under WD30L: `rm23f80g` **108**, `rm34e30g` and `rm34f80` 90, `rm34f80g` **88**, `rm34e30` 84; under P: `rm34f80g` 145, `rm34f80` 138.
* **The ghost and the confirmed clear change nothing on the strict rule (AQ1 and AQ2 missed, AQ3 and AQ4 held).** `rm34f80g` against `rm34f80`: 0 under R3D30 (5 asked), −2 under WD30L (5 asked), +7 under P. Behind the ghost the confirmed clear against the run clear (`rm34f80g` against `rm34e30g`): +3 under R3D30 and −2 under WD30L (both within 8). Without the ghost (`rm34f80` against `rm34e30`) 0 and +6. POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `rm34f80g` against `rm34f80` +14 / +14 (p 1.0) under R3D30, +17 / +19 (p 0.87) under WD30L and +16 / +9 (p 0.23) under P; against `rm34e30g` +16 / +13 (p 0.71) and +16 / +18 (p 0.86). It fires, a mean of 14 re-marks a flight under R3D30, 22 under WD30L, 20 under P and 52 on a sea with no faults, and the four strict arms are 116 to 119 under R3D30 and 84 to 90 under WD30L.
* **The strict far rule collapses under the leak whatever is added to it, and the light rule is 18 to 24 above it.** `rm34f80g@WD30L` collides in 72 flights of 180 with a median minimum clearance of 2.8 m (`rm34e30` 76 and 2.8 m; `rm23f80g` 48 and 6.6 m). `rm23f80g` is 8 above the strict arms under R3D30 (127, 119) and 20 above under WD30L (108, 88); POST HOC `rm34f80g` against `rm23f80g` +23 / +31 (p 0.34) under R3D30 and +25 / +45 (p 0.022) under WD30L. That is follow-ups 26, 30 and 39 again (the first-reading mark within 80 m is what the uniform false rate exploits); here with the ghost and the confirmed clear on top and no better.
* **The gaps kind is the lowest cell for every strict arm under R3D30 (30 to 34 of 60 against 48.0), and under the persistent mix the ghost arm holds all three kinds.** `rm34f80g@P` is 49, 48, 48 (all held) against `rm34f80@P` 46, 43, 49. The 24 m gaps reached under R3D30 (of 20; exact 20): `rm23f80g` 8, `rm34e30` 5, `rm34e30g` 4, `rm34f80g` 3, `rm34f80` 2.
* **The strict arms are lower here than in earlier sets.** The same arms under R3D30: `rm34e30` 126, 125 and 119 (follow-ups 26, 30 and here), `rm34e30g` 127 and 116 (follow-up 30 and here), `rm34f80` 132 and 119 (follow-up 39 and here): up to 13 apart, more than the 7 to 9 follow-up 22 measured, in a set where `exact` is 178 (the highest of the sets read so far) and the clean map 174.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing (AQ8 held).** `rm34f80g@clean` 174 against the clean map's 174 (POST HOC +5 / +5, p 1.0).
* **A note on how this set was flown.** HELD47 was started once, lost with the container before it wrote anything (no number had been read from it), and flown again from the start; the flights in the file are those of the second run. The same holds for the sets of follow-ups 36, 43 and 44.

**What it does not say.** That a ghost or a confirmed clear cannot help a strict rule (neither moves anything here, against a rule that fails at the first reading within 80 m: follow-up 28 registers the near confirmation that tests the repair). That the light rule is a good detector under the leak (it misses islands and current by wide margins: 32 and 23 against 48.0 and 46.4). That 80 m, 0.8 and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 43: THE CONFIRMED CLEAR THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, ON THE RAMP HOLD (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-confirm-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 27's name (`detector-hold-range-confirm`: which range the confirmed hold goes by, a step or a ramp) with `-range` on the end, **the move follow-up 31 made on follow-up 24** (`range-hold-confirm` to `range-hold-confirm-range`): the confirmation itself knowing how far, which is **the confirmed clear's fraction depending on the cell's range**. The range-aware *mark* is not what is meant: that is follow-up 25's move (`range-confirm-hold-confirm`, with the step gate) and follow-up 39's. Follow-up 27's detector is kept (the 2-of-3 mark at every range; the evidence hold of cap 30 gated by the **ramp** `max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))` on the **nearest** range the cell has been read occupied from; a marked cell cleared when at least `K = ceil(f L)` of its last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings were free) and the fraction `f` is `fNear` for a cell within 80 m of the sonar at the ping (inclusive) and `fFar` beyond. **Why this is not follow-up 31 again:** follow-up 31 had to keep the hold at every range because the step gate leaves one reading beyond 80 m, and a window of one has no fraction; under the ramp a far cell has a window (`L` is 12 at 120 m and 6 at 160 m), so the fraction can act across the sonar's whole range. If it meant the range-aware *mark* with the ramp hold (a different round: `rm23rn30k`), or the fraction as a ramp, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view, **follow-up 15's above all** (the ramp hold on the nearest range held the bar under dropouts; the ramp was 14 above the step, and which range the gate goes by did not matter) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirming rules are one rule). **The confirmed clear itself (follow-up 21), the confirmed ramp hold (follow-up 27) and the fraction by range (follow-up 31) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about the confirmed clear is made without any result: its two effects (it cleans the fix smear, it clears a leaking dropout sooner) are registered mechanisms, not findings. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The question, and why a range can matter to a fraction.** Follow-up 31's argument stands: a leaking dropout cleared **near** the vessel is the collision, and a smeared wall **far** away is a plan that closes a gap the vessel will see better later; so a **strict fraction near** (a run) and a **tolerant fraction far**, with the opposite arm flown to see whether the bet is the right way round. The ramp changes what there is to be strict about: near the vessel the window is up to 30 readings and a run of that length is a very strict clear; at 120 m it is 12.

**The rule** (follow-up 27's designated detector with two fractions). A free cell is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(f x L)` of its last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings were free, `g(r) = max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))`, `r` the least range the cell has been read occupied from since it was last cleared, `f` = `fNear` within 80 m of the sonar at the ping (inclusive) and `fFar` beyond. `fNear` = `fFar` is follow-up 27's `m23rn30k` (0.8) or, at 1.0, a run clear (`m23rn30`).

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 42): **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the leak is), **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: the range law with the false rate 0.03 at its mean and dropouts of 30 pings, no leak: where the smear is), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, the 2-of-3 mark and the ramp hold of cap 30 on the nearest range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `WD30L` and `R3D30` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23rn30`** (a run clear at every range: the control with no confirmation), **`m23rn30k`** (0.8 at every range: follow-up 27's designated), **`m23rn30kn`** (**the designated: a run within 80 m, 0.8 beyond**) and **`m23rn30kf`** (0.8 within, a run beyond: the opposite bet) (10 arms); under `P` `m23rn30k` and `m23rn30kn` (2); and `m23rn30kn` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD48 = seeds 4801 to 4860 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23rn30kn` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `WD30L` and under `R3D30`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The ramp already lets the far cells go sooner, which is part of what the tolerant far fraction does, so the two may be one effect counted twice; and both effects of the confirmed clear are felt near the vessel, where the 24 m gaps are met, so a strict fraction there gives up the smear cure exactly where it paid (follow-up 31's tension). The bet is that the leak's price near is the larger of the two.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AR).**
* **AR1** a strict fraction near answers the leak: under `WD30L`, `m23rn30kn` reaches at least 3 more than `m23rn30k`. (A run within 80 m is held by the leaking dropout's flicker where 0.8 lets it go. Follow-up 31's AF1.)
* **AR2** and gives up the smear cure near: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30kf` reaches at least 3 more than `m23rn30kn`. (Follow-up 31's AF2.)
* **AR3** the confirmed clear still cures the smear under the ramp: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30k` reaches at least 5 more than `m23rn30`. (Follow-up 21's A2.)
* **AR4** and still pays for it under the leak: under `WD30L`, `m23rn30` reaches at least 5 more than `m23rn30k`. (Follow-up 21's A4.)
* **AR5** `m23rn30kn` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AR6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AR7** the fraction by range is cheap under the persistent mix: under `P`, `m23rn30kn` is within 8 of `m23rn30k` (either side).
* **AR8** on a sea with no faults `m23rn30kn` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`inc` against the rest, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the pooled occupied cells at the last plan are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 2-of-3, 30, 1.0, 0.8 and the ramp to 200 m are the right numbers or that the fraction should be a step in range and not a smooth function; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that the nearest range read is the right range for the ramp (follow-up 15: the range did not matter). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, `clear.fNear` (default `f`; the field follow-ups 31 and 40 need, built once), three registry rows (`m23rn30`, `m23rn30kn` and `m23rn30kf`; `m23rn30k` is follow-up 27's), the set HELD48 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD48 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 43 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD48 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)
