What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `m23rn30kn`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD48 = seeds 4801 to 4860 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 43 (2026-11-04; HELD48 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorHoldRangeConfirmRangeHeld48.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: PARTLY SUPPORTED (5 of 6 cells).** The designated `m23rn30kn` against 80% of exact's count: WD30L: islands HELD, gaps HELD, current HELD; R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current HELD. **3 of 8 predictions held; AR1, AR2, AR3, AR4, AR5 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 174 (59/58/57); `long+fix+forget` 169 (57/59/53); `inc@WD30L` 126 (36/60/30); `m23rn30@WD30L` 160 (55/56/49); `m23rn30k@WD30L` 159 (52/57/50); `m23rn30kn@WD30L` 157 (52/55/50); `m23rn30kf@WD30L` 159 (53/58/48); `inc@R3D30` 136 (48/44/44); `m23rn30@R3D30` 136 (53/35/48); `m23rn30k@R3D30` 139 (54/37/48); `m23rn30kn@R3D30` 138 (51/37/50); `m23rn30kf@R3D30` 137 (55/35/47); `m23rn30k@P` 145 (51/48/46); `m23rn30kn@P` 143 (51/43/49); `m23rn30kn@clean` 170 (58/59/53).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AR1 MISSED**: a strict fraction near answers the leak: under WD30L, m23rn30kn reaches at least 3 more than m23rn30k. m23rn30kn@WD30L 157, m23rn30k@WD30L 159: -2 against the 3 asked.
* **AR2 MISSED**: and gives up the smear cure near: under R3D30, m23rn30kf reaches at least 3 more than m23rn30kn. m23rn30kf@R3D30 137, m23rn30kn@R3D30 138: -1 against the 3 asked.
* **AR3 MISSED**: the confirmed clear still cures the smear under the ramp: under R3D30, m23rn30k reaches at least 5 more than m23rn30. m23rn30k@R3D30 139, m23rn30@R3D30 136: +3 against the 5 asked.
* **AR4 MISSED**: and still pays for it under the leak: under WD30L, m23rn30 reaches at least 5 more than m23rn30k. m23rn30@WD30L 160, m23rn30k@WD30L 159: +1 against the 5 asked.
* **AR5 MISSED**: m23rn30kn holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. m23rn30kn@R3D30: 51/47.2 37/46.4 50/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AR6 HELD**: and under WD30L. m23rn30kn@WD30L: 52/47.2 55/46.4 50/45.6 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AR7 HELD**: the fraction by range is cheap under the persistent mix: under P, m23rn30kn is within 8 of m23rn30k. m23rn30kn@P 143, m23rn30k@P 145: 2 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AR8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults m23rn30kn is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. m23rn30kn@clean 170, long+fix+forget 169: 1 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated fraction by range holds five of six bar cells, and on the ramp hold the four clears are one number: PARTLY SUPPORTED.** `m23rn30kn` (a 2-of-3 mark, the ramp hold of cap 30 on the nearest range, a run clear within 80 m and 0.8 beyond) holds all three kinds under WD30L (52, 55, 50 against 47.2, 46.4 and 45.6 needed) and islands and current under R3D30 (51 and 50); the gaps kind under R3D30 is 37 against 46.4. Three of eight predictions held (AR6, AR7, AR8). Counts of 180 under WD30L: `m23rn30` (a run clear at every range) **160**, `m23rn30k` (0.8 at every range: follow-up 27's designated) 159, `m23rn30kf` (0.8 within, a run beyond) 159, `m23rn30kn` **157**, `inc` (the map that forgets) 126; under R3D30: `m23rn30k` **139**, `m23rn30kn` 138, `m23rn30kf` 137, `m23rn30` 136, `inc` 136 (exact 174, clean `long+fix+forget` 169); under P: `m23rn30k` 145, `m23rn30kn` 143.
* **Neither the confirmed clear nor the fraction by range does anything here (AR1 to AR4 missed).** The four ramp-hold clears are within 3 of each other under each sea (157 to 160, 136 to 139). AR3 asked the confirmed clear to cure the smear by 5 under R3D30: `m23rn30k` is 3 above `m23rn30`; AR4 asked the run clear to be 5 above the confirmed one under WD30L: it is 1 above (160, 159); AR1 asked a strict fraction near to answer the leak by 3: `m23rn30kn` is 2 *below* `m23rn30k`; AR2 asked the opposite bet to be 3 above under R3D30: 1 below (137, 138). POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) `m23rn30kn` against `m23rn30` +9 / +7 (p 0.80) under R3D30 and +5 / +8 (p 0.58) under WD30L, against `m23rn30k` +3 / +4 (p 1.0) and +2 / +4 (p 0.69), against `m23rn30kf` +10 / +9 (p 1.0) and +4 / +6 (p 0.75); under P against `m23rn30k` +10 / +12 (p 0.83). Follow-ups 21, 24, 25 and 31 found the confirmed clear 1 to 7 below the run clear on other holds; this set finds it 1 below under WD30L and 3 above under R3D30 on the ramp hold.
* **The ramp hold is a cure under the leak and level with the map that forgets under range-law false returns.** WD30L: the four holds are 31 to 34 above `inc` (157 against 126; POST HOC +39 / +8, p below 0.0001), with 16 to 19 collisions against 41 and 12 timeouts for `inc`. R3D30: `m23rn30kn` 138 against `inc` 136 (+24 / +22, p 0.88). The gaps kind under R3D30 (of 58; 46.4 needed) is missed by every arm: `inc` 44, `m23rn30k` and `m23rn30kn` 37, `m23rn30` and `m23rn30kf` 35; the 24 m gaps reached (of 20; exact 18): `inc` 9, `m23rn30` and `m23rn30kf` 8, `m23rn30k` and `m23rn30kn` 7.
* **The same arm moves further between sets than the 7 to 9 follow-up 22 reported.** `m23rn30k` under R3D30 reached 145 in follow-up 27's HELD32, 128 in follow-up 34's HELD39 and 139 here (17 apart); under WD30L 159, 149 and 159. `inc` under R3D30 129, 139 and 136. That is the context in which a difference of 3 to 5 flights should be read.
* **A sea with no faults costs nothing (AR8 held).** `m23rn30kn@clean` 170 against the clean map's 169 (POST HOC +5 / +4, p 1.0).
* **A note on how this set was flown.** HELD48 was started once, lost with the container before it wrote anything (no number had been read from it), and flown again from the start; the flights in the file are those of the second run. The same holds for the sets of follow-ups 36, 42 and 44.

**What it does not say.** That a confirmed clear or a fraction by range could not matter under another hold (follow-ups 21, 24 and 31 found effects of 1 to 7 on other holds). That the ramp is level with the map that forgets in general (it loses the gaps kind and wins the sea with leaks). That 30, 80 m, 0.8 and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 44: THE PLAIN RUN WITH THE CLEAR CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-range-confirm", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 33's name (`detector-range-hold-range`: follow-up 10's range-aware run, three in a row beyond 80 m and one reading within, with follow-up 13's gated evidence hold) with `-confirm` on the end, the move follow-up 25 made on follow-up 19 and follow-up 24 on follow-up 13. The confirmation after the hold is the hold's, as in each of those: **the confirmed clear of follow-up 21** (`K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(30, occ)` readings free). So this is **follow-up 25's question asked of follow-up 33's detector**: follow-up 25 puts the confirmed clear on the range-aware mark *with a memory* (`rm23re30k`), this round puts it on the range-aware mark *without* one (`crre30k`), and the two differ only as follow-ups 17 and 33 do. It asks whether the confirmed clear pays in the plain-run detector, and whether the run and the memory are one rule behind it. Naming, from follow-up 33's correction: the detector follow-up 33 calls `r3re30` is follow-up 19's `crre30`, the registry's name for it, used here. If it meant something else, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 17's (the range-aware pair with the gated evidence hold reached 118 of 180 under range-law false returns with dropouts against 145 for the uniform pair; under the uniform false rate 97 against 163), follow-up 18's (taking the range gate off the hold paid +10) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirming rules are one rule). **The plain-run detector (follow-up 19's `crre30`, follow-up 33) and the confirmed clear (follow-up 21, follow-up 25) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 33's detector with the clear confirmed). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked after 3 consecutive occupied readings (**run**) or when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied (**memory**); one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading. A marked cell **within `near`** is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free (**confirmed**: `k`) or after `min(30, occ)` free readings in a row (**run clear**), and one **beyond `near`** after one free reading.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 43): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`W`** (the uniform false rate of 0.01, no dropouts: the law NOT holding) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `R3D30` and `WD30L` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`crre30`** (the run, a run clear: follow-up 19's), **`crre30k`** (**the designated: the run, the clear confirmed**), **`rm23re30`** (the memory, a run clear: follow-up 17's designated) and **`rm23re30k`** (the memory, the clear confirmed: follow-up 25's designated) (10 arms); under `W` `inc` and `crre30k` (2); and `crre30k` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD49 = seeds 4901 to 4960 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `crre30k` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`W` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The confirmed clear is registered to clean the fix smear (a long hold thickens a wall) and to clear a leaking dropout sooner; the gate already lets a far dropout take a cell at once, and a first-reading near mark already takes every near false return. Neither of those is a thing the clear touches, and follow-up 17's range-aware pair was well below the uniform pair; the bet is that the clear helps the plain-run detector as much as it helps the memory one, and no more.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AS).**
* **AS1** the confirmed clear pays in the plain-run detector: under `R3D30`, `crre30k` reaches at least 5 more than `crre30`. (Follow-up 21's A2 and follow-up 25's V1.)
* **AS2** and behind the gate it changes little under the leak: under `WD30L`, `crre30k` is within 8 of `crre30` (either side). (Follow-up 24's I2 and follow-up 25's V4.)
* **AS3** the run and the memory are one rule behind a confirmed clear: under `R3D30`, `crre30k` is within 10 of `rm23re30k` (either side). (Follow-up 20's Y1 and follow-up 33's AH2.)
* **AS4** and under the leak: under `WD30L`, `crre30k` is within 10 of `rm23re30k` (either side).
* **AS5** `crre30k` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AS6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AS7** the first-reading mark costs when the law fails: under `W`, `crre30k` reaches at least 10 fewer than `inc`. (Follow-up 17's Q5 and Q6.)
* **AS8** on a sea with no faults `crre30k` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm23re30k` against `rm23re30` under each sea, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the pooled occupied cells at the last plan are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, a run of three, 2-of-3, a cap of 30 and 0.8 are the right numbers; that the range law is true of a real sonar; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that the plain run and the memory are the only marks worth pairing with the confirmed clear (a K-of-N clear, a likelihood sum and the filtered fix are untried). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, one registry row (`crre30k`; `crre30` is follow-up 19's row, `rm23re30` follow-up 17's and `rm23re30k` follow-up 25's), the set HELD49 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD49 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 44 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD49 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers: the detector module with the five optional fields of this stretch (`clear.fNear`, `ghost.carry`, `ghost.zone`, `ghost.at`, `lock`) and 77 more registry rows (59 mutants of the new lines, 55 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 3 equivalent and said; 154 mechanical mutants of the 77 rows, all red); the table of sets (HELD30 to HELD63, seeds 4901 to 4960 for this one); the study engine with its new operation `noBar` and the 34 more specs (1,377 mechanical mutants of the specs, all red by the row that holds them to the registrations; 10 of the helpers they call and 2 of the list of operations; 12 of the engine's `noBar` line, 9 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 2 equivalent and said; each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against a literal copy of the registrations in the gate, and the table that generated both against the registered prose by a script (272 predictions' operations, thresholds, directions and arms mechanically, 14 by hand: no disagreement); the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `inc@R3D30` 14/18; `crre30@R3D30` 9/18; `crre30k@R3D30` 9/18; `rm23re30@R3D30` 13/18; `rm23re30k@R3D30` 13/18; `inc@WD30L` 17/18; `crre30@WD30L` 4/18; `crre30k@WD30L` 5/18; `rm23re30@WD30L` 8/18; `rm23re30k@WD30L` 6/18; `inc@W` 14/18; `crre30k@W` 5/18; `crre30k@clean` 17/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**What DEV showed that the registration did not say, and is said here so that it is not found afterwards and called a surprise:** `crre30@WD30L` reached 4 of 18, `crre30k@WD30L` reached 5 of 18, `crre30k@W` reached 5 of 18 (the other arms 6 to 18). It is **not read as a result** (18 scenarios) and it moves **no prediction and no verdict rule**; it means the registered predictions and bar cells that need such an arm to reach 80% of exact are likely to be missed, and the study will say so.

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `crre30k`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD49 = seeds 4901 to 4960 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.

### RESULT OF FOLLOW-UP 44 (2026-11-04; HELD49 flown ONCE, 2,700 flights, `brain/subSensedDetDetectorRangeHoldRangeConfirmHeld49.jsonl`; the verdict below is the one the study command printed; every number under "POST HOC" was computed after it was read and is labelled)

**VERDICT: NOT SUPPORTED (1 of 6 cells).** The designated `crre30k` against 80% of exact's count: R3D30: islands HELD, gaps MISSED, current MISSED; WD30L: islands MISSED, gaps MISSED, current MISSED. **4 of 8 predictions held; AS1, AS4, AS5, AS6 missed.**

**Flown (reached of 180; islands / gaps / current):** `exact` 172 (57/59/56); `long+fix+forget` 171 (59/59/53); `inc@R3D30` 130 (49/43/38); `crre30@R3D30` 110 (48/19/43); `crre30k@R3D30` 110 (49/19/42); `rm23re30@R3D30` 120 (49/26/45); `rm23re30k@R3D30` 118 (48/26/44); `inc@WD30L` 130 (41/59/30); `crre30@WD30L` 69 (31/10/28); `crre30k@WD30L` 69 (33/10/26); `rm23re30@WD30L` 96 (33/35/28); `rm23re30k@WD30L` 97 (33/34/30); `inc@W` 136 (39/58/39); `crre30k@W` 74 (34/16/24); `crre30k@clean` 163 (56/59/48).

**The predictions (each scored as registered):**
* **AS1 MISSED**: the confirmed clear pays in the plain-run detector: under R3D30, crre30k reaches at least 5 more than crre30. crre30k@R3D30 110, crre30@R3D30 110: +0 against the 5 asked.
* **AS2 HELD**: and behind the gate it changes little under the leak: under WD30L, crre30k is within 8 of crre30. crre30k@WD30L 69, crre30@WD30L 69: 0 apart, within 8 asked.
* **AS3 HELD**: the run and the memory are one rule behind a confirmed clear: under R3D30, crre30k is within 10 of rm23re30k. crre30k@R3D30 110, rm23re30k@R3D30 118: 8 apart, within 10 asked.
* **AS4 MISSED**: and under the leak: under WD30L, crre30k is within 10 of rm23re30k. crre30k@WD30L 69, rm23re30k@WD30L 97: 28 apart, within 10 asked.
* **AS5 MISSED**: crre30k holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under R3D30. crre30k@R3D30: 49/45.6 19/47.2 42/44.8 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AS6 MISSED**: and under WD30L. crre30k@WD30L: 33/45.6 10/47.2 26/44.8 (reached / needed of 0.8 exact, islands gaps current).
* **AS7 HELD**: the first-reading mark costs when the law fails: under W, crre30k reaches at least 10 fewer than inc. inc@W 136, crre30k@W 74: +62 against the 10 asked.
* **AS8 HELD**: on a sea with no faults crre30k is within 8 of the clean long+fix+forget. crre30k@clean 163, long+fix+forget 171: 8 apart, within 8 asked.

**What was found (the registered measures; the reading of them is post hoc where said).**
* **The designated run of three with the confirmed clear holds one of six bar cells: NOT SUPPORTED.** `crre30k` (a run of three beyond 80 m and a first reading within, the evidence hold of cap 30 gated by range, the clear confirmed at 0.8) reaches 49, 19 and 42 under R3D30 against 45.6, 47.2 and 44.8 needed (islands held) and 33, 10 and 26 under WD30L. Four of eight predictions held (AS2, AS3, AS7, AS8). Counts of 180 under R3D30: `inc` **130**, `rm23re30` (the memory, a run clear: follow-up 17's) 120, `rm23re30k` (the memory, confirmed: follow-up 25's) 118, `crre30` (the run, a run clear: follow-up 19's) and `crre30k` **110** each (exact 172, clean `long+fix+forget` 171); under WD30L: `inc` 130, `rm23re30k` 97, `rm23re30` 96, `crre30` and `crre30k` **69** each; under W: `inc` 136, `crre30k` 74.
* **The confirmed clear is idle, to the flight (AS1 missed, AS2 held).** `crre30k` and `crre30` are 110 and 110 under R3D30 and 69 and 69 under WD30L (POST HOC (3 kinds pooled, 180 paired scenarios, no correction for the number of tests) +1 / +1, p 1.0 and +3 / +3, p 1.0); `rm23re30k` and `rm23re30` 118 and 120, 97 and 96. The cure the registration borrowed from follow-ups 21 and 25 is not seen on a run of three, and its cost (1 to 7 elsewhere) is not seen either.
* **The run is 8 below the memory where the law holds and 28 below under the leak (AS3 held, AS4 missed).** `crre30k` against `rm23re30k`: −8 under R3D30 (within 10 asked; POST HOC +16 / +24, p 0.27) and −28 under WD30L (within 10 asked; +23 / +51, p 0.0015). Under the uniform false rate with leaking dropouts a run of three with a first reading within 80 m collapses: 95 collisions of 180, a median minimum clearance of 0 and 69 reached (`rm23re30k`: 69 collisions, 4.3 m). Under W `crre30k` reaches 74 against `inc`'s 136 (AS7 held, by 62; +17 / +79, p below 0.0001), 88 collisions.
* **The gaps kind is the failure under range-law false returns, as in follow-up 33.** Gaps reached under R3D30 (of 59; 47.2 needed): `inc` 43, `rm23re30` and `rm23re30k` 26, `crre30` and `crre30k` 19; the 32 m gaps (of 20): 16, 9, 9, 4, 4, and under WD30L 0 of 20 for both runs. `crre30k` is 20 below `inc` under R3D30 (POST HOC +23 / +43, p 0.019).
* **A sea with no faults costs 8 (AS8 held, at its edge).** `crre30k@clean` 163 against the clean map's 171 (POST HOC +4 / +12, p 0.077); follow-up 33 found 14 (`r3re30@clean` 160 against 174) for the same run without the confirmed clear.
* **The run of three is the stable arm across sets, the memory is not.** `crre30` under R3D30 reached 118 in follow-up 19's HELD24, 114 in follow-up 33's HELD38 (as `r3re30`) and 110 here; `rm23re30` 131, 115 and 120; `inc` 127, 127 and 130.
* **A note on how this set was flown.** HELD49 was started once, lost with the container before it wrote anything (no number had been read from it), and flown again from the start; the flights in the file are those of the second run. The same holds for the sets of follow-ups 36, 42 and 43.

**What it does not say.** That a confirmed clear cannot matter for a run: the run's mark of three takes few cells, a clear that is idle there is a statement about a detector that fails for another reason (its first reading within 80 m under a uniform false rate, which no arm here isolates). That the memory is the better far rule (the two are level where the law holds, 8 apart). That 30, 80 m, 0.8 and the made-up leak of 0.15 are the right numbers. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

## FOLLOW-UP 45: THE HOLD THAT REMEMBERS, WITH THE CLEAR CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-hold-confirm", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 32's name (`detector-hold-range-hold`: follow-up 15's ramp hold with the carried evidence) with `-confirm` on the end, the move follow-ups 24, 25, 27 and 39 made: the confirmation after the hold is the hold's, **the confirmed clear of follow-up 21** (`K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings free). Follow-up 32's detector is kept (a **mark of one**: the confirmation in the name is the clear's, not the mark's; the evidence hold with the ramp `g(r) = max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))` on the nearest range the cell has been read occupied from; the carry: a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings that is read occupied again within 60 readings of that cell comes back with its evidence put to 4) and its clear is confirmed. It is **not** follow-up 34 (`hold-range-confirm-hold`), which has the 2-of-3 *mark* and the ghost. If it meant the 2-of-3 mark as well, that is follow-up 34; say so before the build.

**One piece of arithmetic worth saying before the data.** With `K = ceil(0.8 L)`, a window of 1, 2, 3 or 4 readings tolerates **no** occupied reading (`K = L`) and a window of 5 tolerates one; so for a mark of one, whose false returns have `occ` of 1 and clear at their first free reading, the confirmed clear changes **nothing** until a cell has earned 5 occupied readings, and the carry restores 4, at which the clear is still a run. The two stages are therefore nearly orthogonal here by construction, and the round measures how far "nearly" goes.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 15's (the ramp hold on the nearest range held the bar under dropouts; which range did not matter) and follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 and +5 to a hold; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals). **The confirmed clear (follow-up 21) is unread, and the carry (follow-up 32) is registered and unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result: the confirmed clear's two effects (it cleans the fix smear, it clears a leaking dropout sooner) are registered mechanisms, not findings. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 32's detector with the clear confirmed). A free cell is marked at one occupied reading. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings were free (**confirmed**), `r` the least range it has been read occupied from since it was last cleared, or after `min(g(r), occ)` free readings in a row (**run clear**). **With the carry**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time with `occ = 4`.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 44): **`D30`** (whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings with the range law and none of follow-up 4's white faults: follow-up 15's and 32's), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the confirmed clear's price is), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, a mark of one and the ramp hold on the nearest range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `D30` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`rn30`** (follow-up 15's designated: a run clear, no carry), **`rn30k`** (the clear confirmed, no carry), **`rn30c`** (follow-up 32's designated: a run clear, the carry) and **`rn30ck`** (**the designated: the clear confirmed and the carry**); under `WD30L` `inc`, `rn30`, `rn30k`, `rn30c` and `rn30ck` (5); under `P` `rn30` and `rn30ck` (2); and `rn30ck` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD50 = seeds 5001 to 5060 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rn30ck` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** Both stages are expected to be small by the arithmetic above, and the carry's own registration expects it to change little under the dropout. The confirmed clear is registered to cost under the leak, where a cell with a long evidence tolerates an occupied reading in five and a leaking dropout clears it sooner; the carry puts a cleared cell back at 4, a window at which the clear tolerates nothing. If either stage does anything measurable it is in `WD30L`.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AT).**
* **AT1** the confirmed clear changes little for a mark of one under the dropout: under `D30`, `rn30k` is within 8 of `rn30` (either side). (A window of four or fewer tolerates no occupied reading.)
* **AT2** but it costs under the leak: under `WD30L`, `rn30` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30k`. (Follow-up 21's A4: a leaking dropout is cleared sooner.)
* **AT3** the carry changes little behind it: under `D30`, `rn30ck` is within 8 of `rn30k` (either side). (Follow-up 32's AG1.)
* **AT4** and under the leak: under `WD30L`, `rn30ck` is within 8 of `rn30c` (either side). (Follow-up 32's AG2.)
* **AT5** `rn30ck` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `D30`.
* **AT6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AT7** the pair's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `rn30ck` is within 10 of `rn30` (either side). (Follow-up 32's AG3 expected a price; follow-up 18's totals showed none.)
* **AT8** on a sea with no faults `rn30ck` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rn30ck` against `rn30c` per kind and per gap, `inc` against the rest under `WD30L` and the number of carried re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 4 occupied readings, a life of 60, 0.8, a cap of 30 and the ramp to 200 m are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that the confirmed clear and the carry are the only treatments worth pairing with the ramp hold. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, follow-up 32's `ghost.carry` (built once), two registry rows (`rn30k` and `rn30ck`; `rn30` is follow-up 15's row and `rn30c` follow-up 32's), the set HELD50 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD50 is.

Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

## FOLLOW-UP 46: THE GHOST THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, BEHIND THE STRICT FAR MARK (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-confirm-hold-range-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 30's name (`detector-confirm-hold-range-hold`: the ghost behind the strict far mark, 3 of 4 beyond 80 m and a first reading within, with the evidence hold at every range) with `-range` on the end, **the move follow-up 41 made on follow-up 18** (and follow-ups 37 and 38 on 32 and 29): the ghost knowing how far. It is follow-up 41's round with follow-up 30's mark in place of follow-up 18's. **The zone is judged at the ping that clears the cell**, as in follow-up 41, for the reason given there and read from the module: with a first-reading mark within 80 m a ghost re-marked within 80 m is a no-op (a re-marking zone would make a `near` arm identical to no ghost by construction). The ghost is created for cells cleared **beyond 80 m** (`far`), **within 80 m inclusive** (`near`) or **at any range** (`all`: follow-up 30's). What this round adds to follow-up 41's question is the strict mark: follow-up 30's tension is that a strict far rule is the slowest to re-mark a cell a dropout cleared, so the far ghost should matter more here than it does behind the 2-of-3. If it meant the zone on the re-marking ping, or the hold range-gated, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range, its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirming rules are one rule; strictness cost where the misses are many). **The strict far mark with the hold (follow-up 26), the ghost behind it (follow-up 30) and the ghost's zone (follow-up 41) are unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 30's detector with a zone on the ghost). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked when at least 3 of its last 4 readings were occupied (the **strict** rule; **light**: 2 of 3), one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is cleared after `min(30, occ)` consecutive free readings at every range. **The ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 **at a ping from the zone** becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time, from any range, with `occ = 4`; a cell cleared from the other zone is forgotten at once. After 60 readings a ghost is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 45): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law: the bar's), **`WD30`** (follow-up 16's: the false rate uniform at 0.01 with the same dropouts: the law NOT holding), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`rm34e30`** (follow-up 26's designated: the strict far mark, no ghost), **`rm34e30g`** (follow-up 30's designated: the ghost at every range), **`rm34e30gf`** (**the designated: the ghost for cells cleared beyond 80 m**), **`rm34e30gn`** (the ghost for cells cleared within 80 m), **`rm23e30`** (the light far mark, no ghost: follow-up 18's) and **`rm23e30gf`** (the light far mark with the far ghost: follow-up 41's designated); under `WD30` `rm34e30`, `rm34e30g` and `rm34e30gf` (3) and under `P` the same three (3); `rm34e30gf` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD51 = seeds 5101 to 5160 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm34e30gf` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30`**: three cells, follow-up 30's bar on a fresh set. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`WD30`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The far ghost is the whole of what the ghost does for a cell marked at one reading near the vessel, so `far` and `all` should be one detector in effect, and the near-created ghost acts only on a cell cleared near and read next from far. The strict rule is the one for which a re-marking at one reading instead of three of four is the largest saving, so if the ghost's far zone matters anywhere it should matter here. The persistent mix returns the same false returns, and a strict rule passes them rarely, so the ghost's price may be small.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AU).**
* **AU1** the far ghost is the one that matters: under `R3D30`, `rm34e30gf` is within 8 of `rm34e30g` (either side). (A near re-marking is a no-op.)
* **AU2** the near-created ghost does little: under `R3D30`, `rm34e30gn` is within 8 of `rm34e30` (either side).
* **AU3** the far ghost helps the strict rule: under `R3D30`, `rm34e30gf` reaches at least 5 more than `rm34e30`. (Follow-up 30's AE1.)
* **AU4** and helps it more than the light rule: under `R3D30`, (`rm34e30gf` minus `rm34e30`) exceeds (`rm23e30gf` minus `rm23e30`) by at least 3. (Follow-up 30's AE2.)
* **AU5** the ghost does not cost where the law fails: under `WD30`, `rm34e30gf` is within 10 of `rm34e30` (either side).
* **AU6** `rm34e30gf` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AU7** the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `rm34e30gf` is within 8 of `rm34e30` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J7.)
* **AU8** on a sea with no faults `rm34e30gf` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm34e30gf` against `rm23e30gf` (strict against light, follow-up 30's AE3), the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 3-of-4, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the zone should be a step in range and not a smooth weight; that the range at the clearing ping is the right range for the ghost; that the range law is true of a real sonar. The round is follow-up 41's with a different mark and is expected to find a small effect, and says so. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **two new fields in the module**, `ghost.zone` and `ghost.at` (the fields follow-ups 37, 38 and 41 need, built once), two registry rows (`rm34e30gf` and `rm34e30gn`; `rm34e30` is follow-up 26's row, `rm34e30g` follow-up 30's, `rm23e30` follow-up 18's and `rm23e30gf` follow-up 41's), the set HELD51 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD51 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 47: THE SECOND HOLD ON THE PLAIN RUN (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-range-hold", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 33's name (`detector-range-hold-range`: follow-up 10's plain range-aware run, three in a row beyond 80 m and one reading within, with follow-up 13's gated evidence hold) with `-hold` on the end, **the move follow-up 18 made on follow-up 17** and follow-up 29 on follow-up 24: **a second hold, on what has been held**, in the two ways it was done there and in all four combinations: **take the range gate off the hold** (the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range, the mark still range-aware) and add **the ghost** (a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings keeps its window and evidence for 60 readings of that cell and is marked again at one occupied reading, from any range, with its evidence put back to 4; no neighbour test: that is follow-up 23's). Follow-up 18's reading is kept without change; what is new is the mark: the plain run of three, which has no memory, in place of the 2-of-3. Naming, from follow-up 33's correction: the detector follow-up 33 calls `r3re30` is follow-up 19's `crre30`, used here, and the hold at every range with the run is the detector follow-up 33 calls `r3e30`, called `cre30` here. If it meant the ghost judged by range (follow-ups 37, 38, 41 and 46), say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and **this round rests on follow-up 18's, which is read**: removing the range gate from the hold paid +10 under range-law false returns with dropouts, the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range, under the uniform false rate the hold at every range with the ghost was 13 above the gated pair, and the ghost's price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals. The predictions below were written with that in view, and with follow-up 17's (the range-aware pair with the gated hold was 27 below the uniform pair on its set, 11 on follow-up 18's) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirming rules are one rule). The plain-run detector itself (follow-ups 19 and 33) is unread or unbuilt, so what is predicted about the run is made without a result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 18's detector with the run in place of the memory). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` and a ghost counter. A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** from the sonar at the ping is marked after 3 consecutive occupied readings, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is cleared after `min(clear, occ)` consecutive free readings, `clear` = 30 within `near` and 1 beyond (**gated**) or 30 at every range (**ungated**). **With the ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time with `occ = 4`; after 60 it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (exactly follow-up 33's, everything else as follows-ups 3 to 46): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law: the bar's), **`W`** (the uniform false rate of 0.01, the law NOT holding), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`crre30`** (the run, the gated hold: follow-up 19's), **`cre30`** (the run, the hold at every range), **`crre30g`** (**the designated: the run, the gated hold, the ghost**), **`cre30g`** (the run, the hold at every range, the ghost) and **`rm23re30g`** (the memory, the gated hold, the ghost: follow-up 18's, here the control for the run against the memory); under `W` `inc`, `crre30` and `crre30g` (3) and under `P` the same three (3); `crre30g` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD52 = seeds 5201 to 5260 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `crre30g` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30`**: three cells, follow-up 18's bar on a fresh set. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`W`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The gated hold is the hold follow-up 13 bet *for* (it keeps the fix smear off far walls) and follow-up 18 found cost about 10 under these faults; the ghost is the stage that restores what the gate lets a far dropout take. The run of three is slower to mark than the 2-of-3 by about a half at a hit rate of 0.8 (51% against 90% of three readings), which the ghost does not touch: it acts only on a cell that was marked and cleared. The persistent mix returns the same false returns, and a run of three passes them less often than a 2-of-3.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AV).**
* **AV1** the range gate on the hold costs under the joint stress: under `R3D30`, `cre30` reaches at least 5 more than `crre30`. (Follow-up 18's J1: +10; follow-up 33's AH3.)
* **AV2** the ghost helps the gated hold: under `R3D30`, `crre30g` reaches at least 5 more than `crre30`. (Follow-up 18's J3: +9.)
* **AV3** and little when the hold is everywhere: under `R3D30`, `cre30g` is within 8 of `cre30` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J4: +5.)
* **AV4** behind a ghost the run is no different from the memory: under `R3D30`, `crre30g` is within 10 of `rm23re30g` (either side). (Follow-up 20's Y1.)
* **AV5** `crre30g` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AV6** the first-reading mark costs when the law fails: under `W`, `crre30g` reaches at least 10 fewer than `inc`. (Follow-up 17's Q5 and Q6.)
* **AV7** the ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `crre30g` is within 8 of `crre30` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J7.)
* **AV8** on a sea with no faults `crre30g` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`crre30g` against `cre30g` and `rm23re30g` per kind and per gap and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, a run of three, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the ghost is the only second hold (a decayed evidence, a hold on the neighbours, a K-of-N clear are untried); that the range law is true of a real sonar. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, three registry rows (`cre30`, `crre30g` and `cre30g`; `cre30` is the detector follow-up 33 calls `r3e30`, one row between them; `crre30`, `inc` and `rm23re30g` exist), the set HELD52 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD52 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 48: THE GHOST THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, ON THE CONFIRMED RAMP HOLD (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-confirm-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 34's name (`detector-hold-range-confirm-hold`: the ghost on the ramp-gated confirmed hold) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-up 38 made on follow-up 29, follow-up 37 on follow-up 32 and follow-up 41 on follow-up 18: **the second hold knowing how far**. Follow-up 34's detector is kept (the 2-of-3 mark at every range; the confirmed clear of follow-up 21, `K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings free; `g` the ramp `max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))` on the nearest range the cell has been read occupied from; the ghost: a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings is marked again at one occupied reading for 60 readings of that cell, with its evidence put back to 4) and the ghost is **judged by the range at the ping that re-marks the cell**, as in follow-up 38: **beyond 80 m** (`far`), **within it** (`near`) or **at every range** (`all`: follow-up 34's). The zone is on the re-marking ping here, unlike follow-ups 41 and 46, because the mark is the 2-of-3 at every range: a near re-marking is not a no-op as it is behind a first-reading near mark. The reason a range can matter is the ramp, as in follow-up 34: a far cell is held 12 readings at 120 m and 6 at 160 m, so a dropout takes it and the far ghost has something to restore; a near cell was held up to 30. If it meant the zone on the clearing ping (follow-up 41's), or the zone with the step gate (follow-up 38's), say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 15's (the ramp hold held the bar; which range the gate goes by did not matter) and follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals). **The confirmed clear (follow-up 21), the confirmed ramp hold (follow-up 27) and the ghost on it (follow-up 34) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 34's detector with a zone on the ghost). A free cell is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings were free. A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time **at a ping from beyond 80 m (`far`), within 80 m inclusive (`near`) or from any range (`all`)** it is marked at that reading with `occ = 4`, whatever the mark rule; read occupied from the other zone it is judged by the ordinary 2-of-3 rule. After 60 readings it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 47): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a restored phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23rn30k`** (follow-up 27's designated: the confirmed ramp hold, no ghost), **`m23rn30kg`** (follow-up 34's designated: the ghost at every range), **`m23rn30kgf`** (**the designated: the ghost beyond 80 m only**) and **`m23rn30kgn`** (the ghost within 80 m only); under `WD30L` `m23rn30k`, `m23rn30kg`, `m23rn30kgf` and `m23rn30kgn` (4); under `P` `m23rn30k`, `m23rn30kg` and `m23rn30kgf` (3); `m23rn30kgf` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD53 = seeds 5301 to 5360 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23rn30kgf` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The ramp already holds far cells a few readings, so a far cell cleared by a dropout was held for little and has little evidence to restore; the ghost restores it at one reading, which is the far zone's whole use. The near ghost restores a cell that was confirmed cleared after up to 30 readings of evidence, which is a clear that was probably right. The persistent mix returns the same false returns, and the range law puts the most false returns far away, where the far ghost acts.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AW).**
* **AW1** the far ghost is the one that matters: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30kgf` is within 8 of `m23rn30kg` (either side). (The ramp's short far hold is where there is something to restore.)
* **AW2** and the near ghost does little: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30kgn` is within 8 of `m23rn30k` (either side). (A near clear was probably right.)
* **AW3** the far ghost helps the ramp hold: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30kgf` reaches at least 5 more than `m23rn30k`. (Follow-up 34's AI1; follow-up 18's J3 and J4.)
* **AW4** and answers the leak: under `WD30L`, `m23rn30kgf` reaches at least 5 more than `m23rn30k`. (Follow-up 34's AI2.)
* **AW5** `m23rn30kgf` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AW6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AW7** the far ghost's price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `m23rn30kgf` is within 8 of `m23rn30k` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J7.)
* **AW8** on a sea with no faults `m23rn30kgf` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23rn30kgf` against `m23rn30kg` per kind and per gap (the 24 m gaps especially) and the number of ghost re-marks by zone (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 200 m, 30, 0.8, 2-of-3, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the zone should be a step in range and not a smooth weight; that the range at the re-marking ping is the right range for the ghost; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, `ghost.zone` (the field follow-ups 37, 38, 41 and 46 need, built once; `ghost.at` stays at its default `mark`), two registry rows (`m23rn30kgf` and `m23rn30kgn`; `m23rn30k` is follow-up 27's row and `m23rn30kg` follow-up 34's), the set HELD53 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD53 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 49: THE GHOST THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, WITH ITS RE-MARK CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-confirm-hold-range-confirm", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 38's name (`detector-range-hold-confirm-hold-range`: the ghost, judged by the range at the ping that re-marks the cell, on the gated confirmed hold) with `-confirm` on the end. The earlier `-confirm` moves (follow-ups 24, 25, 27, 39, 44, 45) added **the confirmed clear** to a name that had none; follow-up 38's name already has it (`range-hold-confirm`, the gated hold with the clear of follow-up 21). **The word that follows a ghost is read as follow-up 23 read its fourth word** (`hold-confirm-hold-confirm`: the four stages, the last being the ghost with its re-marking confirmed): **the ghost's re-mark is confirmed by a neighbour**. A ghost cell read occupied is marked again only if one of its eight neighbours was also read occupied at the same ping or was marked when the ping began (follow-up 23's `nbr`, in the module already), so an isolated reading, which is what a false return and a leaking dropout's stray hits look like, does not restore it. The rest is follow-up 38's: the 2-of-3 mark at every range; the evidence hold of cap 30 gated at 80 m (one free reading beyond); the clear confirmed at 0.8; the ghost judged by the range at the re-marking ping, **beyond 80 m** (`far`), **within 80 m** (`near`) or **at every range** (`all`). If it meant the mark confirmed again (a stricter rule for the ghost's re-mark: two occupied readings in place of one) or something else, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range, and its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 17's (a first-reading mark is where harm is done). **The confirmed clear (follow-up 21), the ghost behind the confirmed hold (follow-up 29), the ghost's zone (follow-up 38) and the neighbour test inside the four stages (follow-up 23) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 38's detector with the re-mark confirmed). A free cell is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(g, occ)` readings were free, `g` = 30 within 80 m of the sonar at the ping and 1 beyond. A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time **at a ping from the zone** it is marked at that reading with `occ = 4` **if a neighbour (eight-connected) was also read occupied at that ping or was marked when the ping began** (**confirmed**: `c`) or without the test (**plain**); read occupied from the other zone, or refused by the test, it is judged by the ordinary 2-of-3 rule. After 60 readings it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 48): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where isolated stray hits are), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where a restored phantom is called) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the five detectors **`m23re30k`** (follow-up 24's designated: the gated confirmed hold, no ghost), **`m23re30kg`** (follow-up 29's: the plain ghost at every range), **`m23re30kgf`** (follow-up 38's designated: the plain ghost beyond 80 m only), **`m23re30kgfc`** (**the designated: the confirmed ghost beyond 80 m only**) and **`m23re30kgc`** (the confirmed ghost at every range); under `WD30L` `m23re30kg`, `m23re30kgf`, `m23re30kgfc` and `m23re30kgc` (4); under `P` `m23re30kgf`, `m23re30kgfc` and `m23re30kgc` (3); `m23re30kgfc` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD54 = seeds 5401 to 5460 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23re30kgfc` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The neighbour test is built to refuse an isolated reading, and the two faults this study is about put isolated readings in the sea: false returns (a free cell read occupied once) and the leak (a dropped-out obstacle returning a stray hit). It also refuses the restoring of a wall cell whose neighbours were not read at that ping, which a first reading of a restored wall after a dropout may be, since a whole-obstacle dropout takes all its cells at once (the cells not yet cleared stay marked and count as neighbours, so an obstacle's edge is restored, and the cells inside it follow). So the test may cheapen the ghost under the persistent mix and cost it under the leak, which is the ghost's best case.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AX).**
* **AX1** the confirmed ghost's price under the persistent mix is no different from the plain one's: under `P`, `m23re30kgfc` is within 8 of `m23re30kgf` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J7: the plain ghost's price was not visible.)
* **AX2** the test costs little where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgfc` is within 8 of `m23re30kgf` (either side).
* **AX3** the confirmed far ghost still helps the gated hold: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgfc` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30k`. (Follow-up 18's J3: +9 for the plain ghost.)
* **AX4** but the test refuses the leak's stray hits: under `WD30L`, `m23re30kgfc` reaches at least 3 fewer than `m23re30kgf`. (Follow-up 29's AD4 expects the ghost to answer the leak; a stray hit has no neighbour.)
* **AX5** `m23re30kgfc` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AX6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AX7** the zone still does not matter once the re-mark is confirmed: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgc` is within 8 of `m23re30kgfc` (either side). (Follow-up 38's AM1.)
* **AX8** on a sea with no faults `m23re30kgfc` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23re30kgc` against `m23re30kg` per sea, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the numbers of ghost re-marks and refusals (`detStats.ghostMarks`, `detStats.ghostRefused`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That eight neighbours at the same ping is the right confirmation (two readings, a neighbour pair, a likelihood sum are untried); that 80 m, 2-of-3, 0.8, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that the range law is true of a real sonar. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, `ghost.zone` (the field follow-ups 37, 38, 41, 46 and 48 need, built once; `ghost.nbr` exists), two registry rows (`m23re30kgfc` and `m23re30kgc`; `m23re30k`, `m23re30kg` and `m23re30kgf` are follow-ups 24, 29 and 38's), the set HELD54 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD54 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 50: THE CONFIRMED CLEAR THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, BEHIND THE CARRY (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-hold-confirm-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 45's name (`detector-hold-range-hold-confirm`: the ramp hold with the carried evidence, the clear confirmed, a mark of one) with `-range` on the end, **the move follow-up 31 made on follow-up 24 and follow-up 43 on follow-up 27**: the confirmation itself knowing how far, which is **the confirmed clear's fraction depending on the cell's range**. Follow-up 45's detector is kept (a mark of one; the evidence hold of cap 30 with the ramp `g(r) = max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))` on the nearest range the cell has been read occupied from; the carry, in follow-up 32's form: a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings that is read occupied again within 60 readings of that cell comes back with its evidence put to 4) and the clear is `K = ceil(f L)` of the last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings free, with `f` = `fNear` for a cell within 80 m of the sonar at the ping (inclusive) and `fFar` beyond. If it meant the carry's zone (that is follow-up 37's) or the mark confirmed (follow-up 34's), say so before the build.

**What the arithmetic says before the data.** Follow-up 45 notes that `K = ceil(0.8 L)` tolerates no occupied reading in a window of 4 or fewer and one in a window of 5 to 9. For a mark of one that means false returns (`occ` 1) are untouched by any fraction, and the carry restores 4, at which every fraction is a run. **The fraction can only matter for a cell that has earned 5 occupied readings or more, and under the ramp that is a near cell**: within 80 m the window is 18 to 30 readings, so 0.8 tolerates 3 to 6 occupied readings in it, and beyond 160 m it is 6 or fewer and tolerates at most one. So the registered "strict near, tolerant far" bet (follow-up 31's) changes the near behaviour almost entirely and the far fraction does little; the opposite arm is flown to see whether that is so. This is the reason the predictions below are not follow-up 31's.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 15's (the ramp hold held the bar under dropouts) and follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 and +5 to a hold). **The confirmed clear (follow-up 21), the fraction by range (follow-ups 31 and 43), the carry (follow-up 32) and the carry with the confirmed clear (follow-up 45) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result: the clear's two effects (it cleans the fix smear, it clears a leaking dropout sooner) are registered mechanisms, not findings. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 45's detector with two fractions). A free cell is marked at one occupied reading. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(f x L)` of its last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings were free, `r` the least range it has been read occupied from since it was last cleared, `f` = `fNear` within 80 m of the sonar at the ping (inclusive) and `fFar` beyond. **The carry**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time with `occ = 4`. `fNear` = `fFar` = 1 is follow-up 32's `rn30c`; both 0.8 is follow-up 45's `rn30ck`.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 49): **`D30`** (whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings with the range law and none of follow-up 4's white faults: follow-up 15's, 32's and 45's), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the leak is), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, a mark of one, the ramp hold on the nearest range and the carry): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `D30` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets, no carry), **`rn30c`** (a run clear at every range: follow-up 32's designated), **`rn30ck`** (0.8 at every range: follow-up 45's designated), **`rn30cfn100f80`** (**the designated: a run within 80 m, 0.8 beyond**) and **`rn30cfn80f100`** (0.8 within, a run beyond: the opposite bet); under `WD30L` `inc`, `rn30c`, `rn30ck`, `rn30cfn100f80` and `rn30cfn80f100` (5); under `P` `rn30ck` and `rn30cfn100f80` (2); and `rn30cfn100f80` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD55 = seeds 5501 to 5560 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rn30cfn100f80` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The confirmed clear is registered to cost under the leak (a leaking dropout is cleared sooner) and to clean the smear; a strict fraction near gives up the smear cure where the narrow gaps are met, and a tolerant fraction far gains nothing the ramp's short far window does not already give. By the arithmetic the designated is the run clear (`rn30c`) within 80 m and almost the run clear beyond it, so it should be level with `rn30c` and the round measures how level.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AY).**
* **AY1** a strict fraction near answers the leak: under `WD30L`, `rn30cfn100f80` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30ck`. (Follow-up 31's AF1 and follow-up 43's AR1.)
* **AY2** by the arithmetic the designated is the run clear: under `D30`, `rn30cfn100f80` is within 8 of `rn30c` (either side).
* **AY3** the confirmed clear costs under the leak: under `WD30L`, `rn30c` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30ck`. (Follow-up 21's A4; follow-up 45's AT2.)
* **AY4** and changes little under the dropout: under `D30`, `rn30ck` is within 8 of `rn30c` (either side). (Follow-up 45's AT1.)
* **AY5** `rn30cfn100f80` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `D30`.
* **AY6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AY7** the fraction by range is cheap under the persistent mix: under `P`, `rn30cfn100f80` is within 8 of `rn30ck` (either side).
* **AY8** on a sea with no faults `rn30cfn100f80` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rn30cfn80f100` against `rn30ck` per sea, `inc` against the rest under `WD30L`, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the number of carried re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, 4 occupied readings, a life of 60, 0.8, a cap of 30 and the ramp to 200 m are the right numbers or that the fraction should be a step in range and not a smooth function; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **two new fields in the module**, `clear.fNear` (default `f`; the field follow-ups 31, 40 and 43 need, built once) and `ghost.carry` (follow-up 32's, built once), two registry rows (`rn30cfn100f80` and `rn30cfn80f100`; `rn30c` is follow-up 32's row and `rn30ck` follow-up 45's), the set HELD55 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD55 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 50 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD55 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

## FOLLOW-UP 51: THE PLAIN RUN WITH THE SECOND HOLD AND THE CLEAR CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-range-hold-confirm", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 47's name (`detector-range-hold-range-hold`: the plain run, three in a row beyond 80 m and a first reading within, with the evidence hold gated or at every range and the ghost) with `-confirm` on the end. **The rule for the trailing word, kept the same in every round that has it:** the first `-confirm` added to a name that has none is **the confirmed clear of follow-up 21** (`K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(g, occ)` readings free; follow-ups 24, 25, 27, 39, 44 and 45 did this, and follow-up 45 did it to a name ending in a second hold, as here); a `-confirm` added to a name that already has one is **the ghost's re-mark confirmed by a neighbour** (follow-up 23's `nbr`; follow-up 49 did this). Follow-up 47's name has no `confirm`, so this round is **follow-up 47's detector with the clear confirmed**: it is follow-up 44's detector (`crre30k`) with the ghost, and follow-up 29's question (the ghost behind the confirmed hold, gated and at every range) asked of the plain run. If it meant the neighbour-confirmed ghost, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; removing the gate paid +10; the ghost's price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals), follow-up 17's (the range-aware pair with the gated evidence hold was below the uniform pair; under the uniform false rate far below) and follow-up 20's (under a hold the confirming rules are one rule). **The plain run (follow-ups 19 and 33), the confirmed clear (follow-up 21), its use with the run (follow-up 44) and the ghost behind the run (follow-up 47) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 47's detector with the clear confirmed). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked after 3 consecutive occupied readings, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(clear, occ)` readings were free, `clear` = 30 within `near` and 1 beyond (**gated**) or 30 at every range (**ungated**). **The ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time, from any range, with `occ = 4`; after 60 it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 50): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, the law NOT holding, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`W`** (the uniform false rate of 0.01, no dropouts) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `R3D30` and `WD30L` the five detectors **`crre30g`** (follow-up 47's designated: the run, the gated hold, the ghost, a run clear), **`crre30k`** (follow-up 44's designated: the same without the ghost, the clear confirmed), **`crre30kg`** (**the designated: the run, the gated hold, the clear confirmed, the ghost**), **`cre30kg`** (the same with the hold at every range) and **`rm23re30kg`** (the memory, the gated hold, the clear confirmed, the ghost: the control for the run against the memory) (10 arms); under `W` `inc` and `crre30kg` (2); and `crre30kg` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD56 = seeds 5601 to 5660 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `crre30kg` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`W` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The confirmed clear is registered to clear a leaking dropout sooner and the ghost to restore what a clear took, so the ghost is the stage that can pay the clear's price; the run is slower to mark than the memory and neither stage touches that; the gate is the hold follow-up 18 found cost about 10 and the ghost repaired part of it. The first-reading near mark takes a near false return at once whatever follows it.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters AZ because every other single or double letter before it is taken).**
* **AZ1** the ghost answers the leak behind the confirmed clear: under `WD30L`, `crre30kg` reaches at least 5 more than `crre30k`. (Follow-up 29's AD4 and follow-up 42's AQ1.)
* **AZ2** and helps where the dropouts do not leak: under `R3D30`, `crre30kg` reaches at least 5 more than `crre30k`. (Follow-up 18's J3: +9 for the gated hold.)
* **AZ3** behind a ghost the confirmed clear changes little where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `crre30kg` is within 8 of `crre30g` (either side).
* **AZ4** and under the leak: under `WD30L`, `crre30kg` is within 8 of `crre30g` (either side).
* **AZ5** `crre30kg` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **AZ6** and under `WD30L`.
* **AZ7** the first-reading mark costs when the law fails: under `W`, `crre30kg` reaches at least 10 fewer than `inc`. (Follow-up 17's Q5 and Q6.)
* **AZ8** on a sea with no faults `crre30kg` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`crre30kg` against `cre30kg` (the gate, with the clear confirmed and the ghost) and against `rm23re30kg` (the run against the memory), per kind and per gap, and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, a run of three, 0.8, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21); that the range law is true of a real sonar; that the ghost is the only second hold. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module**, three registry rows (`crre30kg`, `cre30kg` and `rm23re30kg`; `crre30g` is follow-up 47's row and `crre30k` follow-up 44's), the set HELD56 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD56 is.
