## FOLLOW-UP 52: THE GHOST ON THE CONFIRMED RAMP HOLD, WITH ITS RE-MARK CONFIRMED (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-confirm-hold-confirm", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 34's name (`detector-hold-range-confirm-hold`: the ghost on the ramp-gated confirmed hold) with `-confirm` on the end. Follow-up 34's name already has a `confirm` (the clear's), so the trailing word is read by the rule kept since follow-up 49: **a `-confirm` added to a name that already has one is the ghost's re-mark confirmed by a neighbour** (follow-up 23's `nbr`, in the module already): a ghost cell read occupied is marked again only if one of its eight neighbours was also read occupied at the same ping or was marked when the ping began, so an isolated reading does not restore it. Follow-up 34's detector is otherwise kept as it was (the 2-of-3 mark at every range; the confirmed clear, `K = ceil(0.8 L)` of the last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings free; `g` the ramp `max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))` on the nearest range the cell has been read occupied from; the ghost: a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings is re-marked for 60 readings of that cell, with its evidence put back to 4). It differs from follow-up 23 (the four stages taken out of one detector with the hold at every range) and follow-up 49 (the gated hold and the ghost's zone) only in the hold. If it meant the mark confirmed again, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18, 20 and 21 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 21's (**the confirmed clear did not beat the run clear it replaces, 142 against 147 under white false returns with dropouts and 163 against 168 with leaking dropouts, and every arm did better with the leak**), follow-up 15's (the ramp hold held the bar under dropouts) and follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals). **The confirmed ramp hold (follow-up 27), the ghost on it (follow-up 34) and the neighbour test in the four stages (follow-up 23) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 34's detector with the re-mark confirmed). A free cell is marked when at least 2 of its last 3 readings were occupied, at every range. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings were free, `r` the least range it has been read occupied from since it was last cleared. A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time, from any range, it is marked at that reading with `occ = 4` **if a neighbour (eight-connected) was also read occupied at that ping or was marked when the ping began** (**confirmed**: `c`) or without the test (**plain**); refused by the test it is judged by the ordinary 2-of-3 rule. After 60 readings it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 51): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the leak's stray hits are), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23rn30k`** (follow-up 27's designated: the confirmed ramp hold, no ghost), **`m23rn30kg`** (follow-up 34's designated: the plain ghost), **`m23rn30kgc`** (**the designated: the confirmed ghost**) and **`full`** (follow-up 23's: the confirmed hold at every range with the confirmed ghost: the control for the ramp); under `WD30L` `m23rn30k`, `m23rn30kg`, `m23rn30kgc` and `full` (4); under `P` `m23rn30k`, `m23rn30kg` and `m23rn30kgc` (3); `m23rn30kgc` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD57 = seeds 5701 to 5760 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23rn30kgc` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The neighbour test refuses an isolated reading, which is what a false return and the leak's stray hits look like, so it may cheapen the ghost under the persistent mix and cost it under the leak, where a stray hit through a dropped-out obstacle has no neighbour. Against the ramp's short far window (12 readings at 120 m) a restored far cell has little to lose and the test refuses more of them: the far cells are where an obstacle's neighbours are least likely to be read at the same ping.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters BA).**
* **BA1** the test costs little where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30kgc` is within 8 of `m23rn30kg` (either side). (Follow-up 49's AX2.)
* **BA2** but it refuses the leak's stray hits: under `WD30L`, `m23rn30kgc` reaches at least 3 fewer than `m23rn30kg`. (Follow-up 49's AX4.)
* **BA3** its price under the persistent mix is no different from the plain ghost's: under `P`, `m23rn30kgc` is within 8 of `m23rn30kg` (either side). (Follow-up 49's AX1.)
* **BA4** the confirmed ghost still helps the ramp hold: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30kgc` reaches at least 5 more than `m23rn30k`. (Follow-up 34's AI1; follow-up 18's J3.)
* **BA5** the ramp needs no hold at every range once the ghost is confirmed: under `R3D30`, `m23rn30kgc` is within 8 of `full` (either side). (Follow-up 34's AI4.)
* **BA6** `m23rn30kgc` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **BA7** and under `WD30L`.
* **BA8** on a sea with no faults `m23rn30kgc` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23rn30kgc` against `m23rn30k` per sea, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the numbers of ghost re-marks and refusals (`detStats.ghostMarks`, `detStats.ghostRefused`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That eight neighbours at the same ping is the right confirmation; that 0.8, 2-of-3, 4 occupied readings, a life of 60 and the ramp to 200 m are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21, and the data show it is the easier sea). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module** (`ghost.nbr` exists), one registry row (`m23rn30kgc`; `m23rn30k` is follow-up 27's row, `m23rn30kg` follow-up 34's and `full` follow-up 23's), the set HELD57 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD57 is.

A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time, from any range, it is marked at that reading with `occ = 4` **if a neighbour (eight-connected) was also read occupied at that ping or was marked when the ping began** (**confirmed**: `c`) or without the test (**plain**); refused by the test it is judged by the ordinary 2-of-3 rule. After 60 readings it is forgotten.

**Conditions** (exactly follow-up 29's, everything else as follows-ups 3 to 52): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the leak's stray hits are), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the 2-of-3 mark): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`m23re30k`** (follow-up 24's: gated, no ghost), **`m23re30kg`** (follow-up 29's: gated, the plain ghost), **`m23re30kgc`** (**the designated: gated, the confirmed ghost**), **`m23e30kg`** (follow-up 29's designated: at every range, the plain ghost) and **`full`** (follow-up 23's: at every range, the confirmed ghost); under `WD30L` `m23re30kg`, `m23re30kgc`, `m23e30kg` and `full` (4); under `P` `m23re30kg` and `m23re30kgc` (2); `m23re30kgc` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD58 = seeds 5801 to 5860 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `m23re30kgc` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The gate lets a dropout that begins far away take a cell at once, and the ghost restores it at one reading; the neighbour test refuses a restoring that has no neighbour read at that ping, which a far cell's neighbours, read less often, are likelier to lack. So the test may take back from the gated hold the very thing the ghost gave it, and cost less in the ungated one. Under the leak a stray hit through a dropout has no neighbour and is refused; under the persistent mix an isolated persistent false return is refused too.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters BB).**
* **BB1** the test costs little where the law holds: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgc` is within 8 of `m23re30kg` (either side). (Follow-up 49's AX2.)
* **BB2** but it refuses the leak's stray hits: under `WD30L`, `m23re30kgc` reaches at least 3 fewer than `m23re30kg`. (Follow-up 49's AX4.)
* **BB3** its price under the persistent mix is no different from the plain ghost's: under `P`, `m23re30kgc` is within 8 of `m23re30kg` (either side). (Follow-up 49's AX1.)
* **BB4** the gate costs under the joint stress whichever ghost it has: under `R3D30`, `full` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30kgc`. (Follow-up 18's J1, follow-up 29's AD1.)
* **BB5** the confirmed ghost still helps the gated hold: under `R3D30`, `m23re30kgc` reaches at least 5 more than `m23re30k`. (Follow-up 18's J3: +9 for the plain ghost.)
* **BB6** `m23re30kgc` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **BB7** and under `WD30L`.
* **BB8** on a sea with no faults `m23re30kgc` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`m23e30kg` against `full` (the test with the hold at every range) per sea, the per-kind and per-gap counts and the numbers of ghost re-marks and refusals (`detStats.ghostMarks`, `detStats.ghostRefused`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That eight neighbours at the same ping is the right confirmation; that 80 m, 2-of-3, 0.8, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21, and the data show it is the easier sea). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **no new field in the module** (`ghost.nbr` exists) and **no registry row of its own** (`m23re30k` is follow-up 24's, `m23re30kg` and `m23e30kg` follow-up 29's, `full` follow-up 23's and `m23re30kgc` follow-up 49's, one row between them), the set HELD58 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD58 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 53 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD58 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

## FOLLOW-UP 54: THE HOLD ON THE FREE STATE (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-confirm-hold-range-hold-confirm-hold", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first, and this is the least certain reading in the series.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 42's name (`detector-confirm-hold-range-hold-confirm`: the strict far mark, the evidence hold, the ghost and the confirmed clear) with `-hold` on the end. Every earlier trailing `-hold` (follow-ups 18, 29, 30, 32, 34, 35, 40, 47) was read as **the ghost**, and follow-up 42's name already has one, so the ghost cannot be the new word again (that would be follow-up 42). **It is read here as a hold on the opposite state: a lock on a cell that has been cleared and was never earned.** The ghost is a quick re-mark for a cleared cell that had earned at least 4 occupied readings. Its mirror: a cell cleared with fewer than 4 occupied readings (a false return, a flicker of the fix smear) is **locked free for 8 readings of that cell**: an occupied reading in that time is recorded in its window and cannot mark it. The two together say that a cell's past decides how fast it comes back, quickly if it was a wall and slowly if it was an accident. Follow-up 42's name stays as it was: the strict far mark (3 of 4 beyond 80 m, a first reading within), the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range, the clear confirmed at 0.8, the ghost. **If it meant something else, say so before the build; the readings I did not take are a decayed evidence, a hold on the neighbours and a K-of-N clear, each named untried in earlier "what it would not say" paragraphs, and the ghost again (which is follow-up 42 itself).**

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 to 22 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals), follow-up 20's and 22's (under a hold the mark rules are one rule, with the run clear and with the confirmed clear; the strict run was the lowest of the five under leaking dropouts) and follow-up 21's (the confirmed clear did not beat the run clear). **The strict far mark with the hold (follow-up 26), the ghost behind it (follow-up 30) and the confirmed clear on both (follow-up 42) are unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 42's detector with a lock). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` (occupied readings since last cleared), a ghost counter and a lock counter. A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked when at least 3 of its last 4 readings were occupied, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading, **unless it is locked**; a marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(30, occ)` readings were free, at every range. A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell and is marked at an occupied reading in that time with `occ = 4`; **a cell cleared with `occ` of fewer than 4 is locked for 8 readings of that cell** (`l`): its readings go into the window and its lock falls by one at each, and it can be marked only after the lock has run out. After the lock a cell is marked by the ordinary rule.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 53): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15: where the leak's stray hits and the uniform false returns are), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where an unearned phantom comes back) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, the evidence hold of cap 30 at every range and the clear of `f80`): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `R3D30` and `WD30L` the five detectors **`rm34f80`** (follow-up 39's designated: the strict far mark, no ghost), **`rm34f80g`** (follow-up 42's designated: with the ghost), **`rm34f80l`** (with the lock, no ghost), **`rm34f80gl`** (**the designated: the ghost and the lock**) and **`rm23f80gl`** (the light far mark, 2 of 3, with the ghost and the lock) (10 arms); under `P` `rm34f80g` and `rm34f80gl` (2); and `rm34f80gl` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD59 = seeds 5901 to 5960 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rm34f80gl` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The lock is a delay on re-marking, and a delay is the thing a strict far mark already costs: a cell cleared with fewer than 4 readings that is in fact a real wall (cleared by a dropout before it was earned, which the whole-obstacle dropouts do to every cell marked less than four readings ago) is kept out for 8 readings, and the vessel may be closing on it. The lock helps only where an unearned cell returns as a false return or as a flicker, and the strict rule already passes those rarely. The expected effect is small, and the lock may cost under dropouts.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters BC).**
* **BC1** where the law holds the lock adds little to the ghost: under `R3D30`, `rm34f80gl` is within 8 of `rm34f80g` (either side). (A strict mark passes few false returns.)
* **BC2** under the uniform false rate and the leak the lock costs: under `WD30L`, `rm34f80gl` reaches at least 3 fewer than `rm34f80g`. (A leaking dropout clears cells early, before they are earned, and the lock keeps them out.)
* **BC3** under the persistent mix the lock helps: under `P`, `rm34f80gl` reaches at least 3 more than `rm34f80g`. (An unearned persistent phantom returns after 8 readings instead of at once.)
* **BC4** the lock alone does nothing measurable: under `R3D30`, `rm34f80l` is within 8 of `rm34f80` (either side).
* **BC5** `rm34f80gl` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **BC6** and under `WD30L`.
* **BC7** strict beats light with both: under `R3D30`, `rm34f80gl` reaches more than `rm23f80gl`. (Follow-up 30's AE3.)
* **BC8** on a sea with no faults `rm34f80gl` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rm34f80gl` against `rm34f80g` per kind and per gap, the number of locked re-markings refused (`detStats.lockRefused`) and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 8 readings and a threshold of 4 are the right numbers for the lock, or that a count of readings and not a length of time is the right unit; that the lock is the third hold the name meant; that 80 m, 3-of-4, 0.8, 30, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, a lock (`lock`: `null` by default, or `{ life, below }` with life 8 and below 4: validation, the clearing path, the marking path, `describe`, `stats.lockRefused`, gate rows and mutants), four registry rows (`rm34f80l`, `rm34f80gl` and `rm23f80gl`, and `rm34f80g` if follow-up 42 has not added it; `rm34f80` is follow-up 39's row), the set HELD59 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD59 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 55: THE GHOST THAT KNOWS HOW FAR, BEHIND THE PLAIN RUN (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-range-hold-range", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 47's name (`detector-range-hold-range-hold`: the plain range-aware run, three in a row beyond 80 m and a first reading within, with the gated evidence hold and the ghost) with `-range` on the end, the move follow-ups 37, 38, 41, 46 and 48 made on their predecessors: **the second hold knowing how far**. It is follow-up 41's round with the plain run in place of the 2-of-3, and follow-up 46's with the run in place of the 3-of-4. **The zone is judged at the ping that clears the cell**, as in follow-ups 41 and 46, for the reason given in follow-up 41 and read from the module: with a first-reading mark within 80 m a ghost re-marked within 80 m does nothing, so a zone on the re-marking ping would make the `near` arm identical to no ghost by construction. The ghost is created for cells cleared **beyond 80 m** (`far`), **within 80 m inclusive** (`near`) or **at any range** (`all`: follow-up 47's). Naming, from follow-up 33's correction: the plain run with the gated evidence hold is follow-up 19's `crre30`. If it meant the zone on the re-marking ping, or the hold at every range, say so before the build.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 to 22 had been read when it was written, and **this round rests on follow-up 18's, which is read**: the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range, removing the gate paid +10, and the ghost's price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals (159 against the map that forgets' 160 at 16 re-marks a flight). The predictions were also written with follow-up 17's (the range-aware pair with the gated hold was below the uniform pair) and follow-up 20's and 22's (the mark rules are one rule under a hold) in view. **The plain run (follow-ups 19 and 33), the ghost behind it (follow-up 47) and the ghost's zone (follow-ups 41 and 46) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The rule** (follow-up 47's `crre30g` with a zone on the ghost). Each cell keeps a window of its last 32 readings, `occ` and a ghost counter. A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** from the sonar at the ping is marked after 3 consecutive occupied readings, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading; a marked cell is cleared after `min(clear, occ)` consecutive free readings, `clear` = 30 within `near` and 1 beyond (the gated hold). **The ghost**: a cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 **at a ping from the zone** becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time, from any range, with `occ = 4`; a cell cleared from the other zone is forgotten at once. After 60 readings a ghost is forgotten.

**Conditions** (exactly follow-up 33's, everything else as follows-ups 3 to 54): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law: the bar's), **`W`** (the uniform false rate of 0.01, the law NOT holding), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `R3D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`crre30`** (the run, the gated hold, no ghost: follow-up 19's), **`crre30g`** (the ghost at every range: follow-up 47's designated), **`crre30gf`** (**the designated: the ghost for cells cleared beyond 80 m**), **`crre30gn`** (the ghost for cells cleared within 80 m) and **`rm23re30gf`** (the 2-of-3 with the far ghost: the control for the run against the memory); under `W` `crre30`, `crre30g` and `crre30gf` (3) and under `P` the same three (3); `crre30gf` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD60 = seeds 6001 to 6060 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `crre30gf` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30`**: three cells, follow-up 47's bar on a fresh set. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`W`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The far ghost is the whole of what the ghost does for a cell marked at one reading near the vessel, so `far` and `all` should be one detector in effect; the near-created ghost acts only on a cell cleared near and read next from far. The plain run is the slowest of the marks to enter a far cell (51% of three readings at a hit rate of 0.8, against 90% for a 2-of-3), and the ghost is the stage that makes a re-entry fast, so if the far ghost matters anywhere it should matter here. The effect of the zone itself is expected to be small, as in follow-up 41.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters BD).**
* **BD1** the far ghost is the one that matters: under `R3D30`, `crre30gf` is within 8 of `crre30g` (either side). (A near re-marking is a no-op.)
* **BD2** the near-created ghost does little: under `R3D30`, `crre30gn` is within 8 of `crre30` (either side).
* **BD3** the far ghost helps the gated run: under `R3D30`, `crre30gf` reaches at least 5 more than `crre30`. (Follow-up 47's AV2; follow-up 18's J3: +9.)
* **BD4** the ghost does not cost where the law fails: under `W`, `crre30gf` is within 10 of `crre30` (either side). (Follow-up 46's AU5.)
* **BD5** and its price under the persistent mix is small: under `P`, `crre30gf` is within 8 of `crre30` (either side). (Follow-up 18's J7.)
* **BD6** `crre30gf` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **BD7** behind the far ghost the run is no different from the memory: under `R3D30`, `crre30gf` is within 10 of `rm23re30gf` (either side). (Follow-up 20's Y1.)
* **BD8** on a sea with no faults `crre30gf` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`crre30gf` against `crre30g` per kind and per gap and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 80 m, a run of three, 4 occupied readings and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the zone should be a step in range and not a smooth weight; that the range at the clearing ping is the right range for the ghost (the re-marking ping is follow-up 38's and 48's); that the range law is true of a real sonar. Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **two new fields in the module**, `ghost.zone` and `ghost.at` (the fields follow-ups 37, 38, 41, 46 and 48 need, built once), three registry rows (`crre30gf`, `crre30gn` and `rm23re30gf`; `crre30` is follow-up 19's row, `crre30g` follow-up 47's and `inc` exists), the set HELD60 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD60 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 55 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD60 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

It is **not read as a result** (18 scenarios) and it moves **no prediction and no verdict rule**; it means the registered predictions and bar cells that need such an arm to reach 80% of exact are likely to be missed, and the study will say so.

## FOLLOW-UP 56: THE HOLD ON THE FREE STATE, BEHIND THE CARRY AND THE FRACTION BY RANGE (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-hold-range-hold-confirm-range-hold", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first, and this shares the doubt of follow-up 54.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 50's name (`detector-hold-range-hold-confirm-range`: the ramp hold with the carried evidence, the clear confirmed with its fraction depending on range, a mark of one) with `-hold` on the end. Follow-up 50's name already has a second hold (the carry), so the trailing word is read by the rule follow-up 54 set for a name that already has one: **a lock on the free state**, the mirror of the ghost. A cell cleared with fewer than 4 occupied readings (a false return, a smear flicker: it was never earned) is **locked free for 8 readings of that cell**: an occupied reading in that time is recorded in its window and cannot mark it. Follow-up 50's detector is kept as it was (a mark of one; the evidence hold of cap 30 with the ramp `g(r) = max(1, round(30 (1 - r / 200 m)))` on the nearest range the cell has been read occupied from; the carry: a cell cleared with at least 4 occupied readings that is read occupied again within 60 readings comes back with its evidence put to 4; the clear `K = ceil(f L)` of the last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings free, `f` = `fNear` within 80 m and `fFar` beyond). **Why the lock has more to do here than behind a strict mark (follow-up 54):** a mark of one takes every false return at its first reading, so the cells a lock keeps out are exactly the repeats, and the persistent mix is made of repeats. If it meant something else, say so before the build; the readings not taken are the carry's zone (follow-up 37), the ghost judged by range, and a decayed evidence.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 to 22 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 15's (the ramp hold held the bar under dropouts), follow-up 18's (the ghost's price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 21's (the confirmed clear did not beat the run clear; every arm did better with the leak). **The carry (follow-up 32), the confirmed clear with the carry (follow-up 45), the fraction by range (follow-up 50) and the lock (follow-up 54) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The arithmetic worth saying before the data.** For a mark of one a cell cleared with fewer than 4 occupied readings has `occ` of 1 to 3 and a window of `min(g, occ)` of 1 to 3 readings, so every fraction in follow-up 50 is a run for it; the lock is therefore independent of the fraction by range, and the carry is for the other cells (those with 4 or more), so **the lock and the carry act on disjoint sets of cleared cells**, which is the reason they are put in one detector.

**The rule** (follow-up 50's designated detector with a lock). A free cell is marked at one occupied reading, **unless it is locked**. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(f x L)` of its last `L = min(g(r), occ)` readings were free, `r` the least range it has been read occupied from since it was last cleared, `f` = `fNear` (1.0) within 80 m of the sonar at the ping (inclusive) and `fFar` (0.8) beyond. A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell and is marked at an occupied reading in that time with `occ = 4`; **a cell cleared with `occ` of fewer than 4 is locked for 8 readings of that cell** (`l`): its readings go into the window, its lock falls by one at each, and it can be marked only after the lock has run out.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 55): **`D30`** (whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings with the range law and none of follow-up 4's white faults: follow-ups 15's, 32's, 45's and 50's), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where the repeats are) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, a mark of one, the ramp hold on the nearest range and the carry): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `D30` and `WD30L` the five detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets, no carry), **`rn30c`** (a run clear at every range: follow-up 32's designated), **`rn30cl`** (the same with the lock), **`rn30cfn100f80`** (a run within 80 m, 0.8 beyond: follow-up 50's designated) and **`rn30cfn100f80l`** (**the designated: with the lock**) (10 arms); under `P` `rn30cfn100f80` and `rn30cfn100f80l` (2); and `rn30cfn100f80l` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD61 = seeds 6101 to 6160 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rn30cfn100f80l` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The lock is a delay on re-marking, and a whole-obstacle dropout clears every cell marked fewer than four readings ago, real or not: those cells are then locked for 8 readings after the dropout ends, while the vessel may be closing on them. Against that, the persistent false returns the mix repeats, which a mark of one takes at once, are refused for 8 readings each time. The white false returns of `WD30L` do not repeat at a cell (0.01 a reading), so the lock has nothing to refuse there; the expected effect is a cost under dropouts, a gain under the persistent mix and nothing in between.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters BE).**
* **BE1** the lock costs under the dropouts: under `D30`, `rn30cfn100f80l` reaches at least 3 fewer than `rn30cfn100f80`. (Cells cleared unearned by a dropout wait 8 readings after it.)
* **BE2** and does nothing against random false returns: under `WD30L`, `rn30cfn100f80l` is within 8 of `rn30cfn100f80` (either side). (A white false return does not repeat at its cell.)
* **BE3** and helps against repeats: under `P`, `rn30cfn100f80l` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30cfn100f80`. (The persistent mix is made of repeats.)
* **BE4** the fraction by range is still the run clear: under `D30`, `rn30cfn100f80l` is within 8 of `rn30cl` (either side). (Follow-up 50's AY2.)
* **BE5** `rn30cfn100f80l` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `D30`.
* **BE6** and under `WD30L`.
* **BE7** the lock's price under the dropout does not need the fraction: under `D30`, `rn30c` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30cl`. (BE1 without the fraction.)
* **BE8** on a sea with no faults `rn30cfn100f80l` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rn30cfn100f80l` against `inc` under `WD30L`, the per-kind and per-gap counts, the number of locked re-markings refused (`detStats.lockRefused`) and the number of carried re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 8 readings and a threshold of 4 are the right numbers for the lock, or that the lock is the hold the name meant; that 80 m, 4 occupied readings, a life of 60, 0.8, a cap of 30 and the ramp to 200 m are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **three new fields in the module**, `clear.fNear` (follow-ups 31, 40, 43 and 50), `ghost.carry` (follow-up 32's) and the lock (follow-up 54's `lock: { life, below }`), each built once, two registry rows (`rn30cl` and `rn30cfn100f80l`; `rn30c` is follow-up 32's row and `rn30cfn100f80` follow-up 50's), the set HELD61 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD61 is.

## FOLLOW-UP 57: THE HOLD ON THE FREE STATE, BEHIND THE PLAIN RUN (Keith, 2026-11-03: "lets also do nav-sensed-detector-range-hold-range-hold-confirm-hold", asked while the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 were being flown; REGISTERED BEFORE ANY CODE FOR IT AND BEFORE ITS SET IS FLOWN, but NOT blind to results: see the paragraph after the next)

**What the name was taken to mean, said first, and this shares the doubt of follow-ups 54 and 56.** No backlog entry had that name. It is follow-up 51's name (`detector-range-hold-range-hold-confirm`: the plain run, three in a row beyond 80 m and a first reading within, with the gated evidence hold, the ghost and the clear confirmed) with `-hold` on the end. Follow-up 51's name already has a second hold (the ghost), so the trailing word is read by the rule follow-ups 54 and 56 used: **a lock on the free state**, the mirror of the ghost. A cell cleared with fewer than 4 occupied readings (never earned) is **locked free for 8 readings of that cell**: an occupied reading in that time is recorded in its window and cannot mark it. If it meant something else, say so before the build; the readings not taken are the ghost judged by range (follow-up 55's), a decayed evidence and a hold on the neighbours.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 to 22 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 18's (the ghost added +9 to the gated hold and +5 to the hold at every range; its price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals), follow-up 21's (the confirmed clear did not beat the run clear) and follow-up 22's (the run of three was the top of its family under white false returns and the lowest under leaking dropouts, where it lost the 24 m gaps). **The plain run (follow-ups 19 and 33), the confirmed clear with it (follow-up 44), the ghost behind it (follow-ups 47 and 51) and the lock (follow-up 54) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**One interaction worth saying before the data.** The ghost's threshold and the lock's are the same 4 occupied readings, and a run of three marks a far cell at **3**: a far cell marked by a run and cleared by a dropout before its fourth occupied reading is **locked**, not a ghost, so the cells a run has just confirmed are the ones the lock keeps out for 8 readings after the dropout. Behind the strict 3-of-4 (follow-up 54) a cell is marked at 3 or 4 and the same holds; behind the 2-of-3 or a mark of one (follow-ups 56 and 18) it holds for more cells. This is the place the lock could cost the most.

**The rule** (follow-up 51's designated detector with a lock). A free cell **beyond `near` = 80 m** of the sonar at the ping is marked after 3 consecutive occupied readings, one **within `near`** (inclusive) at the first occupied reading, **unless it is locked**. A marked cell is cleared when at least `K = ceil(0.8 x L)` of its last `L = min(clear, occ)` readings were free, `clear` = 30 within `near` and 1 beyond (gated). A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell, and is marked at an occupied reading in that time, from any range, with `occ = 4`; **a cell cleared with `occ` of fewer than 4 is locked for 8 readings of that cell** (`l`): its readings go into the window, its lock falls by one at each, and it can be marked only after the lock has run out.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 56): **`R3D30`** (follow-up 17's: follow-up 4's four white faults with the range law and the false rate 0.03 at its mean, with dropouts of 30 pings and the range law), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where the repeats are) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix and the gated evidence hold): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under each of `R3D30` and `WD30L` the five detectors **`crre30k`** (the run, the clear confirmed, no ghost: follow-up 44's designated), **`crre30kg`** (with the ghost: follow-up 51's designated), **`crre30kl`** (with the lock, no ghost), **`crre30kgl`** (**the designated: the ghost and the lock**) and **`rm23re30kgl`** (the 2-of-3 far mark with the ghost and the lock: the control for the run against the memory) (10 arms); under `P` `crre30kg` and `crre30kgl` (2); and `crre30kgl` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD62 = seeds 6201 to 6260 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `crre30kgl` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `R3D30` and under `WD30L`**: six cells. **SUPPORTED** if all six, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if at least four, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The lock keeps out repeats and costs a delay on every unearned cell a dropout clears; behind a run of three, which is slow to mark and has only just earned a cell at its marking, that is most of the far cells a dropout meets. The run was the lowest of its family under leaking dropouts in follow-up 22, and the lock adds a delay to it. The expected effect is a cost under dropouts and under the leak and a gain only under the persistent mix.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters BF).**
* **BF1** where the law holds the lock adds little to the ghost: under `R3D30`, `crre30kgl` is within 8 of `crre30kg` (either side).
* **BF2** under the uniform false rate and the leak the lock costs: under `WD30L`, `crre30kgl` reaches at least 3 fewer than `crre30kg`. (A leaking dropout clears cells before they are earned, and the lock keeps them out.)
* **BF3** under the persistent mix the lock helps: under `P`, `crre30kgl` reaches at least 3 more than `crre30kg`. (An unearned persistent phantom returns after 8 readings instead of at once.)
* **BF4** the lock alone does nothing measurable: under `R3D30`, `crre30kl` is within 8 of `crre30k` (either side).
* **BF5** `crre30kgl` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `R3D30`.
* **BF6** and under `WD30L`.
* **BF7** behind the lock the run is no different from the memory: under `R3D30`, `crre30kgl` is within 10 of `rm23re30kgl` (either side). (Follow-up 22: one rule under a hold.)
* **BF8** on a sea with no faults `crre30kgl` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`crre30kgl` against `crre30kg` per kind and per gap, the number of locked re-markings refused (`detStats.lockRefused`) and the number of ghost re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 8 readings and a threshold of 4 are the right numbers for the lock, or that the lock is the hold the name meant; that 80 m, a run of three, 0.8, 30 and a life of 60 are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **one new field in the module**, follow-up 54's lock (`lock: { life, below }`, built once), four registry rows (`crre30kl`, `crre30kgl` and `rm23re30kgl`, and `crre30kg` if follow-up 51 has not added it; `crre30k` is follow-up 44's row), the set HELD62 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD62 is.

If it meant something else, say so before the build; the readings not taken are the ghost judged by range, a decayed evidence and a hold on the neighbours.

**Registered after results were read, and said so.** Follow-ups 25 to 31 were registered before any set was flown. This one was not: the results of follow-ups 7 to 18 and 20 to 22 had been read when it was written, and the predictions below were written with them in view: follow-up 15's (the ramp hold held the bar under dropouts), follow-up 18's (the ghost's price under the persistent mix was not visible in the totals) and follow-up 21's (the confirmed clear did not beat the run clear; every arm did better with the leak). **The carry (follow-up 32), its zone (follow-up 37) and the lock (follow-up 54) are unread, unflown or unbuilt**, so what is predicted here about them is made without any result, and follow-up 37 itself expects a small effect. The registrations of follow-ups 8 to 31 were not written with a result in view.

**The arithmetic worth saying before the data** (follow-up 56's). For a mark of one a cell cleared with fewer than 4 occupied readings has a window of 1 to 3 readings; the lock and the carry therefore act on **disjoint sets of cleared cells**, which is the reason they are put in one detector. A far carry leaves the near carry's cells (cleared from within 80 m with 4 or more readings) as ordinary cells, and the lock does not touch them either.

**The rule** (follow-up 37's designated detector with a lock). A free cell is marked at one occupied reading, **unless it is locked**. A marked cell is cleared after `min(g(r), occ)` consecutive free readings, `r` the least range it has been read occupied from since it was last cleared. A cell cleared with `occ` of at least 4 becomes a ghost for 60 readings of that cell; read occupied in that time **from beyond 80 m (`far`)** or from any range (`all`) it is marked with `occ = 4`; **a cell cleared with `occ` of fewer than 4 is locked for 8 readings of that cell** (`l`): its readings go into the window, its lock falls by one at each, and it can be marked only after the lock has run out.

**Conditions** (everything else as follows-ups 3 to 57): **`D30`** (whole-obstacle dropouts of 30 pings with the range law and none of follow-up 4's white faults: follow-ups 15's, 32's and 37's; the bar's), **`WD30L`** (follow-up 21's: the four white faults with the false rate 0.01 uniform, and dropouts of 30 pings leaking at 0.15), **`P`** (follow-up 5's `pall`, the persistent mix: where the repeats are) and a sea with no faults.

**Arms** (every arm but `exact` is the 200 m sonar with the white 3 m fix, a mark of one and the ramp hold on the nearest range): `exact`; the clean `long+fix+forget`; under `D30` the six detectors **`inc`** (the map that forgets), **`rn30`** (follow-up 15's designated, no carry, no lock), **`rn30c`** (follow-up 32's designated: the carry at every range), **`rn30cf`** (follow-up 37's designated: the carry beyond 80 m only), **`rn30l`** (the lock alone) and **`rn30cfl`** (**the designated: the far carry and the lock**); under `WD30L` `rn30`, `rn30cf` and `rn30cfl` (3) and under `P` the same three (3); `rn30cfl` on a sea with no faults (1). Fifteen arms, 2,700 flights. **The set is HELD63 = seeds 6301 to 6360 of each of `hard-islands`, `hard-gaps` and `hard-current`** (180 scenarios), fresh, flown ONCE.

**Bar and verdict.** The designated `rn30cfl` reaches **at least 80% of what `exact` reaches in EACH kind under `D30`**: three cells, follow-up 15's bar on a fresh set. **SUPPORTED** if all three, **PARTLY SUPPORTED** if two, **NOT SUPPORTED** otherwise. (`WD30L`, `P` and the clean sea are in the predictions, not the bar; `rn30` held this bar in follow-up 15, so a verdict here speaks of the carry and the lock only through the predictions.)

**The tension, said before the data.** The lock is a delay, and a whole-obstacle dropout clears every cell marked fewer than four readings ago, real or not: after the dropout those cells wait 8 readings, while the vessel may be closing on them. The persistent mix repeats the same false returns and a mark of one takes each at once, which the lock refuses for 8 readings after each clear. The carry and the lock are expected to be small each, by construction (follow-up 37's tension and follow-up 56's), and so is their sum.

**Predictions (scored held or missed, moving nothing; pooled over the three kinds unless said; each with its reason; the ids carry the letters BG).**
* **BG1** the lock costs under the dropouts: under `D30`, `rn30cfl` reaches at least 3 fewer than `rn30cf`. (Cells cleared unearned by a dropout wait 8 readings after it.)
* **BG2** and does nothing against random false returns: under `WD30L`, `rn30cfl` is within 8 of `rn30cf` (either side). (A white false return does not repeat at its cell.)
* **BG3** and helps against repeats: under `P`, `rn30cfl` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30cf`. (The persistent mix is made of repeats.)
* **BG4** the lock's price does not need the carry: under `D30`, `rn30` reaches at least 3 more than `rn30l`.
* **BG5** the far carry is the carry that matters: under `D30`, `rn30cf` is within 8 of `rn30c` (either side). (Follow-up 37's AL1.)
* **BG6** `rn30cfl` holds the bar (80% of exact) in every kind under `D30`.
* **BG7** the designated beats the map that forgets under the dropout: under `D30`, `rn30cfl` reaches more than `inc`. (Follow-up 15's T4 asked 10 more and missed at 4 more.)
* **BG8** on a sea with no faults `rn30cfl` is within 8 of the clean `long+fix+forget`.
`rn30cfl` against `rn30cf` per kind and per gap, the number of locked re-markings refused (`detStats.lockRefused`) and the number of carried re-marks (`detStats.ghostMarks`) are flown and reported and not predicted.

**What it would not say.** That 8 readings and a threshold of 4 are the right numbers for the lock, or that the lock is the hold the name meant; that 80 m, 4 occupied readings, a life of 60 and the ramp to 200 m are the right numbers; that the leak is a measured flicker (it is made up, follow-up 21). Not a different pilot, a different generator or the moving ships. A result is a statement about this generator and these models.

**The build waits.** The module and the study engine of follow-ups 8 to 24 are frozen until their sets are flown; this round needs **three new fields in the module**, `ghost.carry` (follow-up 32's), `ghost.zone` (follow-ups 37, 38, 48 and 49's) and the lock (follow-up 54's `lock: { life, below }`), each built once, two registry rows (`rn30l` and `rn30cfl`; `rn30` is follow-up 15's row, `rn30c` follow-up 32's and `rn30cf` follow-up 37's), the set HELD63 and a spec, added **after** the sets of follow-ups 8 to 24 are flown and **before** HELD63 is.


### AMENDMENT 1 TO FOLLOW-UP 58 (2026-11-03; written AFTER the general detector (`brain/subSensedDetect.mjs`, extended for follow-ups 25 to 58), ping's dropout object and leak, the table of sets and the study engine (`tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy.mjs`, specs `tools/ship/subSensedDetSpecs.mjs`) were gated and sabotaged and the DEV set was flown, and BEFORE HELD63 is flown; DEV is a sanity run, NOT scored, and nothing below moves a prediction)

**What was run.** The modules and the engine as logged in their gates' headers: the detector module with the five optional fields of this stretch (`clear.fNear`, `ghost.carry`, `ghost.zone`, `ghost.at`, `lock`) and 77 more registry rows (59 mutants of the new lines, 55 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 3 equivalent and said; 154 mechanical mutants of the 77 rows, all red); the table of sets (HELD30 to HELD63, seeds 6301 to 6360 for this one); the study engine with its new operation `noBar` and the 34 more specs (1,377 mechanical mutants of the specs, all red by the row that holds them to the registrations; 10 of the helpers they call and 2 of the list of operations; 12 of the engine's `noBar` line, 9 red by a row's name, 1 by the gate throwing and 2 equivalent and said; each logged in `tools/ship/subSensedDetStudy-selfcheck.mjs`); the specs checked line for line against a literal copy of the registrations in the gate, and the table that generated both against the registered prose by a script (272 predictions' operations, thresholds, directions and arms mechanically, 14 by hand: no disagreement); the DEV set (seeds 1 to 6 of each kind, 18 scenarios an arm; `--set dev` writes nothing). Neither module nor the engine was edited after its sabotage.

**What DEV showed, read as sanity only (reached of 18, the three kinds pooled):** `exact` 18/18; `long+fix+forget` 18/18; `inc@D30` 15/18; `rn30@D30` 16/18; `rn30c@D30` 16/18; `rn30cf@D30` 16/18; `rn30l@D30` 16/18; `rn30cfl@D30` 15/18; `rn30@WD30L` 16/18; `rn30cf@WD30L` 16/18; `rn30cfl@WD30L` 13/18; `rn30@P` 18/18; `rn30cf@P` 17/18; `rn30cfl@P` 18/18; `rn30cfl@clean` 18/18. **The printed DEV verdict lines are not read**: the thresholds are made for 180 flights and mean nothing on 18. What DEV was read FOR is the plumbing: every arm of the spec was flown, none crashed or timed out wholesale, no arm that should differ from another is identical to it by construction (the detectors differ only in their rules, the faults in their presets).

**Unchanged.** The arms, the designated detector `rn30cfl`, the bar, the verdict rule, the predictions with their reasons, the set (HELD63 = seeds 6301 to 6360 of each of the three kinds) and its single reading are as registered above. Any post hoc test is labelled post hoc.
